def utc_now_z() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


class TerminalColorFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    COLORS = {
        logging.INFO: "\033[38;5;81m",
        logging.WARNING: "\033[38;5;214m",
        logging.ERROR: "\033[38;5;203m",
        logging.CRITICAL: "\033[1;38;5;196m",
    }
    RESET = "\033[0m"
    DIM = "\033[2m"

    def __init__(self, fmt: str, datefmt: str):
        super().__init__(fmt, datefmt)
        stream = getattr(sys, "stdout", None)
        self.use_color = bool(stream and hasattr(stream, "isatty") and stream.isatty())
        if os.getenv("WT_SESSION"):
            self.use_color = True

    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        text = super().format(record)
        if not self.use_color:
            return text
        color = self.COLORS.get(record.levelno, "")
        if not color:
            return text
        parts = text.split(" | ", 2)
        if len(parts) == 3:
            return f"{self.DIM}{parts[0]}{self.RESET} | {color}{parts[1]}{self.RESET} | {parts[2]}"
        return f"{color}{text}{self.RESET}"


def make_json_safe(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, torch.Tensor):
        if value.numel() == 1:
            return float(value.detach().cpu().item())
        return value.detach().cpu().tolist()
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return value.tolist()
    if isinstance(value, (np.floating,)):
        return float(value)
    if isinstance(value, (np.integer,)):
        return int(value)
    if isinstance(value, (np.bool_,)):
        return bool(value)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(k): make_json_safe(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
        return [make_json_safe(v) for v in value]
    return value

def atomic_json_write(path: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    payload = make_json_safe(payload)
    last_exc: Optional[Exception] = None
    for attempt in range(6):
        tmp = f"{path}.tmp.{os.getpid()}.{int(time.time() * 1000)}.{attempt}"
        try:
            with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(payload, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                f.flush()
                try:
                    os.fsync(f.fileno())
                except Exception:
                    pass
            os.replace(tmp, path)
            try:
                if os.path.exists(tmp):
                    os.remove(tmp)
            except Exception:
                pass
            return
        except PermissionError as exc:
            last_exc = exc
            time.sleep(0.10 * (attempt + 1))
        except Exception as exc:
            last_exc = exc
            break
        finally:
            try:
                if os.path.exists(tmp):
                    os.remove(tmp)
            except Exception:
                pass
    if last_exc is not None:
        raise last_exc


def append_jsonl(path: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    payload = make_json_safe(payload)
    with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


def clip_float(value: float, low: float, high: float) -> float:
    return float(min(high, max(low, value)))


def current_model_signature(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, int]:
    return {
        "n_embd": int(config["n_embd"]),
        "n_head": int(config["n_head"]),
        "n_layer": int(config["n_layer"]),
        "block_size": int(config["block_size"]),
        "vocab_size": int(config["vocab_size"]),
    }


def compatible_state_load(
    model: nn.Module,
    state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor],
    target_signature: Dict[str, int],
    source_signature: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    source_signature = dict(source_signature or {})
    target_state = model.state_dict()
    matched: Dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
    mismatched = []
    missing = []
    unexpected = []

    for key, target_tensor in target_state.items():
        source_tensor = state_dict.get(key)
        if source_tensor is None:
            missing.append(key)
            continue
        if tuple(source_tensor.shape) == tuple(target_tensor.shape):
            matched[key] = source_tensor
        else:
            mismatched.append((key, tuple(source_tensor.shape), tuple(target_tensor.shape)))

    source_geom = {k: source_signature.get(k) for k in ("n_embd", "n_head", "n_layer")}
    target_geom = {k: target_signature.get(k) for k in ("n_embd", "n_head", "n_layer")}
    geometry_mismatch = any(
        source_geom.get(k) is not None and target_geom.get(k) is not None and int(source_geom[k]) != int(target_geom[k])
        for k in target_geom
    )
    for key in state_dict.keys():
        if key not in target_state:
            unexpected.append(key)

    if len(matched) == len(target_state) and not mismatched and not unexpected:
        model.load_state_dict(state_dict, strict=True)
        policy = "full compatible load"
    elif matched:
        model.load_state_dict(matched, strict=False)
        policy = "partial compatible load"
    else:
        policy = "fresh init due to geometry mismatch" if geometry_mismatch or mismatched else "fresh init"

    return {
        "policy": policy,
        "matched": len(matched),
        "total": len(target_state),
        "mismatched": len(mismatched),
        "missing": len(missing),
        "unexpected": len(unexpected),
        "geometry_mismatch": bool(geometry_mismatch),
        "sample_mismatches": mismatched[:5],
        "sample_unexpected": unexpected[:5],
    }


def setup_logging(logs_dir: str) -> logging.Logger:
    os.makedirs(logs_dir, exist_ok=True)
    logger = logging.getLogger("JGPT1v12")
    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    logger.handlers.clear()
    stream_fmt = TerminalColorFormatter("%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(message)s", "%H:%M:%S")
    file_fmt = logging.Formatter("%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(message)s", "%H:%M:%S")
    sh = logging.StreamHandler()
    sh.setFormatter(stream_fmt)
    logger.addHandler(sh)
    fh = logging.FileHandler(os.path.join(logs_dir, "jgpt1_v12.log"), encoding="utf-8")
    fh.setFormatter(file_fmt)
    logger.addHandler(fh)
    return logger


def find_latest_checkpoint(save_dir: str) -> Optional[str]:
    if not os.path.isdir(save_dir):
        return None
    found = []
    for name in os.listdir(save_dir):
        m = re.match(r"checkpoint_(\d+)\.pt$", name)
        if m:
            found.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(save_dir, name)))
    if not found:
        return None
    found.sort(reverse=True)
    return found[0][1]


def load_code_corpus(root_dir: str, extensions: List[str], max_files: int, max_file_bytes: int) -> Tuple[bytes, Dict[str, Any]]:
    root_dir = os.path.abspath(root_dir)
    texts: List[bytes] = []
    file_count = 0
    total_bytes = 0
    ext_set = {e.lower() for e in extensions}
    largest = 0
    for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(root_dir):
        for fname in filenames:
            if file_count >= max_files:
                break
            ext = os.path.splitext(fname)[1].lower()
            if ext not in ext_set:
                continue
            path = os.path.join(dirpath, fname)
            try:
                size = min(os.path.getsize(path), max_file_bytes)
                if size <= 0:
                    continue
                with open(path, "rb") as f:
                    raw = f.read(size)
                if raw:
                    texts.append(raw + b"\n\n")
                    total_bytes += len(raw)
                    largest = max(largest, len(raw))
                    file_count += 1
            except Exception:
                continue
        if file_count >= max_files:
            break
    return b"".join(texts), {
        "root_dir": root_dir,
        "files": file_count,
        "bytes": total_bytes,
        "largest_file_bytes": largest,
    }


class SystemMonitor:
    def gpu_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        if NVML_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                handle = pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetHandleByIndex(0)
                util = pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetUtilizationRates(handle)
                temp = pynvml.nvmlDeviceGetTemperature(handle, pynvml.NVML_TEMPERATURE_GPU)
                return {"gpu_util": float(util.gpu), "gpu_temp": float(temp)}
            except Exception:
                pass
        return {"gpu_util": 0.0, "gpu_temp": 0.0}

    def cpu_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        return {"cpu_util": float(psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1)), "cpu_temp": 0.0}

    def ram_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        return {"ram_util": float(psutil.virtual_memory().percent)}

    def cache_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        try:
            vm = psutil.virtual_memory()
            cached = getattr(vm, "cached", 0)
            ratio = float(cached / max(vm.used, 1)) if cached else 1.0
            return {"cache_ratio": ratio}
        except Exception:
            return {"cache_ratio": 1.0}

    def igpu_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        return {"igpu_load": 0.0}

    def entropy_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        for _ in range(30000):
            pass
        jitter = time.perf_counter() - t0
        entropy = abs(jitter) / 10.0 + 0.001
        purity = 1.0 / (entropy * 50.0 + 1e-12)
        return {
            "timing_jitter": float(jitter),
            "hardware_entropy": float(entropy),
            "purity_score": float(min(100.0, purity)),
        }

    def snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        out = {}
        out.update(self.gpu_metrics())
        out.update(self.cpu_metrics())
        out.update(self.ram_metrics())
        out.update(self.cache_metrics())
        out.update(self.igpu_metrics())
        out.update(self.entropy_metrics())
        return out


class TachyonField:
    def __init__(self, lead_factor=1.8):
        self.prev_score = None
        self.prev_prev_score = None
        self.prev_aux = None
        self.lead = float(lead_factor)

    def step(self, score: float, aux: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float, float]:
        if self.prev_score is None:
            self.prev_score = score
            self.prev_prev_score = score
            self.prev_aux = aux
            return score, aux, 0.0, 0.0
        velocity = score - self.prev_score
        acceleration = score - 2 * self.prev_score + self.prev_prev_score
        predicted_score = score + velocity * self.lead + 0.5 * acceleration
        predicted_aux = aux + (aux - self.prev_aux) * self.lead
        self.prev_prev_score = self.prev_score
        self.prev_score = score
        self.prev_aux = aux
        return predicted_score, predicted_aux, velocity, acceleration


class SymbiosisController:
    def __init__(self, base_batch: int):
        self.base_batch = int(base_batch)

    def adapt(self, gpu_load, cpu_load, gpu_temp, igpu_load=0.0, cache_ratio=1.0):
        if gpu_load < 30 and gpu_temp < 65 and igpu_load < 50 and cache_ratio < 1.1:
            return self.base_batch, 0.0
        stress = max(
            gpu_load / 100.0,
            cpu_load / 100.0,
            max(gpu_temp - 50, 0.0) / 40.0,
        )
        if igpu_load > 50:
            stress = max(stress, igpu_load / 100.0)
        if cache_ratio > 1.2:
            stress = min(1.0, stress * cache_ratio)
        stress = min(1.0, stress)
        current_batch = max(8, int(self.base_batch * (1.0 - stress)))
        current_batch = 2 ** max(3, int(np.log2(current_batch)))
        current_pause = stress * 2.5
        return current_batch, current_pause


class GradientBlackholeGuard:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.grad_norm_ema: Optional[float] = None
        self.blackhole_hits = 0
        self.starvation_hits = 0

    def inspect(self, model: nn.Module) -> Dict[str, float]:
        total_sq, max_abs, nonfinite, small, count, mean_abs_total = 0.0, 0.0, False, 0, 0, 0.0
        for p in model.parameters():
            if p.grad is None:
                continue
            g = p.grad.detach()
            if not torch.isfinite(g).all():
                nonfinite = True
            total_sq += float(torch.norm(g).item()) ** 2
            max_abs = max(max_abs, float(g.abs().max().item()))
            small += int((g.abs() < self.cfg["gradient_starvation_floor"]).sum().item())
            count += int(g.numel())
            mean_abs_total += float(g.abs().mean().item()) * float(g.numel())
        grad_norm = math.sqrt(max(total_sq, 0.0))
        mean_abs_grad = float(mean_abs_total / max(1, count))
        return {
            "grad_norm": grad_norm,
            "max_abs_grad": max_abs,
            "nonfinite": 1.0 if nonfinite else 0.0,
            "starvation_ratio": float(small / max(1, count)),
            "mean_abs_grad": mean_abs_grad,
        }

    def apply(self, model: nn.Module, runtime_state: Dict[str, float], diag: Dict[str, float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        event, scale = "stable", 1.0
        grad_norm = float(diag["grad_norm"])
        self.grad_norm_ema = grad_norm if self.grad_norm_ema is None else 0.92 * self.grad_norm_ema + 0.08 * grad_norm
        grad_spike_ratio = grad_norm / max(self.grad_norm_ema, 1e-8)
        diag["grad_norm_ema"] = float(self.grad_norm_ema)
        diag["grad_spike_ratio"] = float(grad_spike_ratio)

        if bool(diag["nonfinite"]):
            event, scale = "nonfinite", 0.0
            self.blackhole_hits = 0
            self.starvation_hits = 0
            for p in model.parameters():
                if p.grad is not None:
                    p.grad = torch.nan_to_num(p.grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                    p.grad.zero_()
            runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.35, runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.5)
        else:
            is_spike = (
                grad_norm > self.cfg["gradient_blackhole_threshold"]
                and grad_spike_ratio > self.cfg["gradient_blackhole_spike_ratio"]
            )
            if is_spike:
                self.blackhole_hits += 1
                self.starvation_hits = 0
                if self.blackhole_hits >= int(self.cfg.get("gradient_blackhole_confirm_steps", 2)):
                    event = "blackhole"
                    scale = self.cfg["gradient_blackhole_threshold"] / max(grad_norm, 1e-8)
                    scale *= self.cfg["gradient_blackhole_decay"]
                    for p in model.parameters():
                        if p.grad is not None:
                            p.grad.mul_(scale)
                    runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.45, runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.85)
                else:
                    event = "spike_guard"
                    scale = min(1.0, self.cfg["gradient_blackhole_threshold"] / max(grad_norm, 1e-8))
                    scale = 0.75 + 0.25 * scale
                    for p in model.parameters():
                        if p.grad is not None:
                            p.grad.mul_(scale)
                    runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.50, runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.93)
            elif (
                diag["starvation_ratio"] >= self.cfg.get("gradient_starvation_ratio_threshold", 0.995)
                and grad_norm < max(self.cfg["gradient_starvation_floor"] * 25.0, self.grad_norm_ema * 0.15)
            ):
                self.starvation_hits += 1
                self.blackhole_hits = 0
                if self.starvation_hits >= int(self.cfg.get("gradient_starvation_confirm_steps", 2)):
                    event = "starvation"
                    runtime_state["lr_scale"] = min(1.20, runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 1.02)
            else:
                self.blackhole_hits = max(0, self.blackhole_hits - 1)
                self.starvation_hits = max(0, self.starvation_hits - 1)
                runtime_state["lr_scale"] = 0.985 * runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) + 0.015
        diag["blackhole_event"] = event
        diag["blackhole_scale"] = scale
        diag["blackhole_hits"] = float(self.blackhole_hits)
        diag["starvation_hits"] = float(self.starvation_hits)
        return diag


class ByteTransformer(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any]):
        super().__init__()
        self.vocab_size = int(config["vocab_size"])
        self.block_size = int(config["block_size"])
        self.n_embd = int(config["n_embd"])
        self.n_head = int(config["n_head"])
        self.n_layer = int(config["n_layer"])
        self.dropout = float(config["dropout"])

        self.wte = nn.Embedding(self.vocab_size, self.n_embd)
        self.wpe = nn.Embedding(self.block_size, self.n_embd)
        enc = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
            d_model=self.n_embd,
            nhead=self.n_head,
            dim_feedforward=4 * self.n_embd,
            dropout=self.dropout,
            activation="gelu",
            batch_first=True,
            norm_first=True,
        )
        self.transformer = nn.TransformerEncoder(enc, num_layers=self.n_layer, enable_nested_tensor=False)
        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(self.n_embd)
        self.lm_head = nn.Linear(self.n_embd, self.vocab_size, bias=False)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)

    def _init_weights(self, module):
        if isinstance(module, (nn.Linear, nn.Embedding)):
            nn.init.normal_(module.weight, mean=0.0, std=0.02)
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear) and module.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)

    def forward(self, idx: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        seq_len = min(idx.size(1), self.block_size)
        idx = idx[:, :seq_len]
        pos = torch.arange(0, seq_len, device=idx.device)
        x = self.wte(idx) + self.wpe(pos)[None, :, :]
        mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(seq_len, seq_len, device=idx.device) * float("-inf"), diagonal=1)
        x = self.transformer(x, mask=mask)
        return self.lm_head(self.ln_f(x))


class RollingEMA:
    def __init__(self, alpha: float):
        self.alpha = float(alpha)
        self.value = None

    def update(self, x: float) -> float:
        x = float(x)
        self.value = x if self.value is None else (1.0 - self.alpha) * self.value + self.alpha * x
        return float(self.value)


class MetricMemory:
    def __init__(self, maxlen: int):
        self.items = deque(maxlen=maxlen)

    def push(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.items.append(dict(payload))

    def values(self, key: str, window: int) -> List[float]:
        vals = [x.get(key) for x in list(self.items)[-window:]]
        return [float(v) for v in vals if isinstance(v, (int, float))]

    def summary(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        if not self.items:
            return {"avg_train": 0.0, "avg_val": 0.0, "avg_reward": 0.0}
        return {
            "avg_train": float(np.mean([x.get("train_loss", 0.0) for x in self.items])),
            "avg_val": float(np.mean([x.get("val_smooth", 0.0) for x in self.items])),
            "avg_reward": float(np.mean([x.get("honest_reward", 0.0) for x in self.items])),
        }

    def save(self, path: str) -> None:
        atomic_json_write(path, {"items": list(self.items)})

    def load(self, path: str) -> None:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return
        try:
            data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.items.clear()
            for item in data.get("items", [])[-self.items.maxlen:]:
                if isinstance(item, dict):
                    self.items.append(item)
        except Exception:
            pass


class WorldMemory:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int, priority_keep: int):
        self.capacity = int(capacity)
        self.priority_keep = int(priority_keep)
        self.records = deque(maxlen=self.capacity)

    def add(self, text: str, score: float = 0.0, source: str = "world") -> None:
        if text:
            self.records.append({"text": text, "score": float(score), "source": source, "t": utc_now_z()})

    def add_many(self, texts: Iterable[str], score: float = 0.0, source: str = "world") -> int:
        n = 0
        for t in texts:
            self.add(t, score=score, source=source)
            n += 1
        return n

    def sample_texts(self, n: int) -> List[str]:
        if not self.records or n <= 0:
            return []
        n = min(n, len(self.records))
        ranked = sorted(list(self.records), key=lambda x: x.get("score", 0.0), reverse=True)
        priority = ranked[: min(len(ranked), self.priority_keep)]
        pool = priority if len(priority) >= n else list(self.records)
        picks = random.sample(pool, n)
        return [p["text"] for p in picks]

    def save(self, path: str) -> None:
        atomic_json_write(path, {"records": list(self.records)})

    def load(self, path: str) -> None:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return
        try:
            data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.records.clear()
            for rec in data.get("records", [])[-self.capacity:]:
                if isinstance(rec, dict) and rec.get("text"):
                    self.records.append(rec)
        except Exception:
            pass


class ReplayBuffer:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.items = deque(maxlen=capacity)

    def add(self, text: str, priority: float, meta: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> None:
        if text:
            self.items.append({"text": text, "priority": float(priority), "meta": meta or {}, "t": utc_now_z()})

    def sample(self, n: int) -> List[str]:
        if not self.items or n <= 0:
            return []
        ranked = sorted(list(self.items), key=lambda x: x["priority"], reverse=True)
        ranked = ranked[: max(n * 4, n)]
        random.shuffle(ranked)
        return [x["text"] for x in ranked[:n]]

    def save(self, path: str) -> None:
        atomic_json_write(path, {"items": list(self.items)})

    def load(self, path: str) -> None:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return
        try:
            data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.items.clear()
            for item in data.get("items", [])[-self.items.maxlen:]:
                if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("text"):
                    self.items.append(item)
        except Exception:
            pass


class FailureMemory:
    def __init__(self, capacity: int):
        self.items = deque(maxlen=capacity)

    def add(self, reason: str, cycle: int, metrics: Dict[str, Any], runtime_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.items.append({
            "reason": reason,
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "metrics": metrics,
            "runtime_state": runtime_state,
            "t": utc_now_z(),
        })

    def recent(self, n: int = 5) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return list(self.items)[-n:]

    def save(self, path: str) -> None:
        atomic_json_write(path, {"items": list(self.items)})

    def load(self, path: str) -> None:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return
        try:
            data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.items.clear()
            for item in data.get("items", [])[-self.items.maxlen:]:
                if isinstance(item, dict):
                    self.items.append(item)
        except Exception:
            pass


class LightPhysarumTrace:
    def __init__(self, decay: float, update: float, prune_threshold: float, prune_cap: float):
        self.decay = float(decay)
        self.update = float(update)
        self.prune_threshold = float(prune_threshold)
        self.prune_cap = float(prune_cap)
        self.trace: Dict[int, float] = {}

    def update_trace(self, model: nn.Module) -> Dict[str, float]:
        vals = []
        for p in model.parameters():
            if p.grad is None:
                continue
            pid = id(p)
            old = self.trace.get(pid, 1.0)
            grad_mag = float(torch.nan_to_num(p.grad.detach(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0).abs().mean().item())
            bond = math.exp(-min(12.0, grad_mag))
            new = self.decay * old + self.update * (0.70 * min(10.0, grad_mag) + 0.30 * bond)
            self.trace[pid] = new
            vals.append(new)
        return {"trace_mean": float(np.mean(vals)) if vals else 0.0}

    def prune(self, model: nn.Module, threshold_scale: float = 1.0, cap_scale: float = 1.0) -> int:
        removed = 0
        threshold = max(1e-6, self.prune_threshold * threshold_scale)
        cap = max(1e-6, self.prune_cap * cap_scale)
        for p in model.parameters():
            if p.requires_grad and p.ndim >= 2:
                score = self.trace.get(id(p), 1.0)
                if score < threshold:
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        thr = cap * float(p.detach().abs().mean().item())
                        mask = p.detach().abs() < thr
                        removed += int(mask.sum().item())
                        p[mask] = 0.0
        return removed


class ProceduralTextCore:
    def __init__(self, seed_bank: int):
        self.seed_bank = [random.randint(0, 10_000_000) for _ in range(max(4, seed_bank))]
        self.recipe_kinds = ["json", "log", "config", "dialog", "patch", "batch", "python"]

    def _rng(self, idx: int) -> random.Random:
        return random.Random(self.seed_bank[idx % len(self.seed_bank)])

    def recipe(self, idx: int) -> str:
        r = self._rng(idx)
        kind = self.recipe_kinds[idx % len(self.recipe_kinds)]
        if kind == "json":
            return json.dumps({
                "device_id": f"node_{r.randint(1,999)}",
                "cycle": r.randint(1, 5000),
                "entropy": round(r.uniform(0.01, 0.99), 4),
                "m2r": round(r.uniform(0.0, 1.2), 4),
                "status": r.choice(["ok", "cooldown", "retry", "fallback"]),
            }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        if kind == "log":
            return "\n".join(
                f"{r.randint(0,23):02d}:{r.randint(0,59):02d}:{r.randint(0,59):02d} | INFO | cycle={i} val={r.uniform(0.8,5.0):.4f} ema={r.uniform(0.8,5.0):.4f} orbit={r.choice(['False','True'])}"
                for i in range(r.randint(3, 8))
            )
        if kind == "config":
            return "\n".join([
                "[bridge]",
                f"teacher_forcing={r.uniform(0.35,0.92):.4f}",
                f"dropout={r.uniform(0.03,0.18):.4f}",
                f"lr={r.uniform(5e-6,7e-4):.7f}",
                f"batch_size={r.choice([8,12,16,24,32])}",
            ])
        if kind == "dialog":
            return (
                "USER: import loop снова жрет cpu\n"
                "ASSISTANT: добавь cooldown и freshest package only\n"
                "USER: best=1.2345, pred_err=0.5432\n"
                "ASSISTANT: тогда усили governor и replay retrain"
            )
        if kind == "patch":
            return (
                "diff --git a/core.py b/core.py\n"
                "@@ -100,6 +100,10 @@\n"
                "+ if rollback_ratio > 0.4:\n"
                "+     escape_mode = True\n"
                "+     lr_scale *= 0.82\n"
            )
        if kind == "batch":
            return "\n".join([
                "@echo off",
                "set PYTHONUTF8=1",
                f"python JGPT.py --cycles {r.choice([50,100,200])} --batch-size {r.choice([8,16,24])}",
            ])
        return (
            "def stabilize_training(val, best, pred_err):\n"
            "    if val > best * 1.12:\n"
            "        return 'freeze'\n"
            "    if pred_err > 0.8:\n"
            "        return 'retrain'\n"
            "    return 'continue'\n"
        )

    def generate_texts(self, n: int) -> List[str]:
        return [self.recipe(i) for i in range(max(0, n))]


def iter_live_files(paths: List[str], recursive: bool, extensions: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    ext_set = {e.lower() for e in extensions}
    out, seen = [], set()
    for p in paths:
        if not p:
            continue
        if os.path.isfile(p):
            if os.path.splitext(p)[1].lower() in ext_set and p not in seen:
                out.append(p)
                seen.add(p)
            continue
        if not os.path.isdir(p):
            continue
        if recursive:
            for root, _, files in os.walk(p):
                for name in files:
                    full = os.path.join(root, name)
                    if os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower() in ext_set and full not in seen:
                        out.append(full)
                        seen.add(full)
        else:
            for name in os.listdir(p):
                full = os.path.join(p, name)
                if os.path.isfile(full) and os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower() in ext_set and full not in seen:
                    out.append(full)
                    seen.add(full)
    return out


def read_textish_file(path: str, max_bytes: int = 250_000) -> Optional[str]:
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            raw = f.read(max_bytes)
        return raw.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore") if raw else None
    except Exception:
        return None


class OnlineCorpusBridge:
    def __init__(self, timeout: float, urls: List[str]):
        self.timeout = float(timeout)
        self.urls = [u for u in urls if u]

    def _yield_jsonish(self, payload: Any) -> Iterable[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            yield payload
            for v in payload.values():
                if isinstance(v, (dict, list)):
                    yield from self._yield_jsonish(v)
        elif isinstance(payload, list):
            for item in payload:
                yield from self._yield_jsonish(item)

    def fetch_texts(self) -> List[str]:
        out: List[str] = []
        for url in self.urls:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(url, timeout=self.timeout)
                if resp.status_code != 200:
                    continue
                payload = resp.json()
                for obj in self._yield_jsonish(payload):
                    out.append(json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False))
            except Exception:
                continue
        return out


class DualModeEvolutionMemory:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.best_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self.mode = 0  # 0 explore, 1 exploit
        self.candidate_score = -float("inf")
        self.candidate_mode = 0
        self.candidate_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self.candidate_confirm_seen = 0
        self.hope_score = -float("inf")
        self.hope_mode = 0
        self.hope_config: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None

        self.param_ranges = {
            "gain": (0.3, 2.5),
            "temperature": (0.3, 2.0),
            "lr": (1e-4, 5e-3),
            "n_embd": [256, 320, 384, 512, 768],
            "n_head": [4, 8, 12, 16],
            "n_layer": [4, 6, 8, 12],
        }

        self.lethal_stats = {param: {"count": 0, "values": []} for param in self.param_ranges}
        self.lessons_stats = {param: {"count": 0, "values": []} for param in self.param_ranges}
        self.lethal_regions = {param: {} for param in self.param_ranges}
        self.recent_failed_configs = deque(maxlen=int(cfg.get("artmoney_failed_recent_keep", 96)))
        self.total_lethal = 0
        self.total_alive = 0
        self.bad_candidate_streak = 0
        self.swarm_successes = 0
        self.swarm_failures = 0
        self.load_policy_counts = {
            "full compatible load": 0,
            "partial compatible load": 0,
            "fresh init due to geometry mismatch": 0,
            "fresh init": 0,
        }
        self.last_load_policy = "fresh init"
        self.strategy = {"exploration": 0.55, "stability": 0.45, "risk": 0.25}

        self.G = float(cfg["gravity_G"])
        self.singularities: List[str] = []
        self.black_hole: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self.protective_field: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self.protection_radius = float(cfg.get("protective_field_radius", 0.16))
        self.protection_growth = float(cfg.get("protective_field_growth", 0.04))
        self.directional_hints: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
        self.absorption_radius = float(cfg["absorption_radius_base"])
        self.absorption_growth = float(cfg["absorption_growth"])
        self.quantum_tunnel_prob = float(cfg["quantum_tunnel_prob"])

    def save(self, path: str) -> None:
        atomic_json_write(path, {
            "history": self.history[-5000:],
            "best_config": self.best_config,
            "mode": self.mode,
            "candidate_score": self.candidate_score,
            "candidate_mode": self.candidate_mode,
            "candidate_config": self.candidate_config,
            "candidate_confirm_seen": self.candidate_confirm_seen,
            "hope_score": self.hope_score,
            "hope_mode": self.hope_mode,
            "hope_config": self.hope_config,
            "lethal_stats": self.lethal_stats,
            "lessons_stats": self.lessons_stats,
            "lethal_regions": self.lethal_regions,
            "recent_failed_configs": list(self.recent_failed_configs),
            "total_lethal": self.total_lethal,
            "total_alive": self.total_alive,
            "bad_candidate_streak": self.bad_candidate_streak,
            "swarm_successes": self.swarm_successes,
            "swarm_failures": self.swarm_failures,
            "load_policy_counts": self.load_policy_counts,
            "last_load_policy": self.last_load_policy,
            "strategy": self.strategy,
            "singularities": self.singularities[-500:],
            "black_hole": self.black_hole,
            "protective_field": self.protective_field,
            "protection_radius": self.protection_radius,
            "directional_hints": self.directional_hints,
            "absorption_radius": self.absorption_radius,
        })

    def load(self, path: str) -> None:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return
        try:
            data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.history = data.get("history", [])
            self.best_config = data.get("best_config")
            self.mode = int(data.get("mode", 0))
            self.candidate_score = float(data.get("candidate_score", -float("inf")))
            self.candidate_mode = int(data.get("candidate_mode", 0))
            self.candidate_config = data.get("candidate_config")
            self.candidate_confirm_seen = int(data.get("candidate_confirm_seen", 0))
            self.hope_score = float(data.get("hope_score", -float("inf")))
            self.hope_mode = int(data.get("hope_mode", 0))
            self.hope_config = data.get("hope_config")
            self.lethal_stats = data.get("lethal_stats", self.lethal_stats)
            self.lessons_stats = data.get("lessons_stats", self.lessons_stats)
            self.lethal_regions = data.get("lethal_regions", self.lethal_regions)
            self.recent_failed_configs = deque(
                data.get("recent_failed_configs", []),
                maxlen=int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_failed_recent_keep", 96)),
            )
            self.total_lethal = int(data.get("total_lethal", 0))
            self.total_alive = int(data.get("total_alive", 0))
            self.bad_candidate_streak = int(data.get("bad_candidate_streak", 0))
            self.swarm_successes = int(data.get("swarm_successes", 0))
            self.swarm_failures = int(data.get("swarm_failures", 0))
            self.load_policy_counts.update(data.get("load_policy_counts", {}))
            self.last_load_policy = str(data.get("last_load_policy", self.last_load_policy))
            self.strategy.update(data.get("strategy", {}))
            self.singularities = data.get("singularities", [])
            self.black_hole = data.get("black_hole")
            self.protective_field = data.get("protective_field")
            self.protection_radius = float(data.get("protection_radius", self.protection_radius))
            self.directional_hints = data.get("directional_hints", {})
            self.absorption_radius = float(data.get("absorption_radius", self.absorption_radius))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _config_signature(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        parts = []
        for key in sorted(self.param_ranges.keys()):
            value = conf.get(key)
            if isinstance(value, float):
                parts.append(f"{key}={value:.6f}")
            else:
                parts.append(f"{key}={value}")
        return "|".join(parts)

    def _region_key(self, param: str, value: Any) -> str:
        ranges = self.param_ranges[param]
        if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
            low, high = ranges
            pos = (float(value) - low) / max(1e-9, high - low)
            bucket = int(np.clip(round(pos * 15.0), 0, 15))
            return str(bucket)
        return str(int(value))

    def _fix_geometry(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        conf["n_embd"] = int(conf["n_embd"])
        conf["n_head"] = int(conf["n_head"])
        conf["n_layer"] = int(conf["n_layer"])
        if conf["n_embd"] % conf["n_head"] != 0:
            possible = [int(x) for x in self.param_ranges["n_head"] if conf["n_embd"] % int(x) == 0]
            conf["n_head"] = int(possible[0]) if possible else int(self.cfg["n_head"])
        return conf

    def register_load_policy(self, policy: str) -> None:
        self.last_load_policy = str(policy)
        self.load_policy_counts[policy] = int(self.load_policy_counts.get(policy, 0)) + 1

    def note_swarm_result(self, success: bool) -> None:
        if success:
            self.swarm_successes += 1
        else:
            self.swarm_failures += 1

    def swarm_success_rate(self) -> float:
        total = self.swarm_successes + self.swarm_failures
        return float(self.swarm_successes / max(1, total))

    def _remember_lesson(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        for param in self.param_ranges:
            value = conf.get(param)
            if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                self.lessons_stats[param]["count"] += 1
                self.lessons_stats[param]["values"].append(float(value))
                self.lessons_stats[param]["values"] = self.lessons_stats[param]["values"][-120:]

    def _build_directional_hints(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]:
        hints: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
        valid = [h for h in self.history if isinstance(h.get("score"), (int, float)) and math.isfinite(float(h.get("score", -float("inf"))))]
        if len(valid) < 8:
            return hints
        ordered = sorted(valid, key=lambda x: float(x.get("score", -float("inf"))), reverse=True)
        sample = max(3, min(8, len(ordered) // 4))
        best_group = ordered[:sample]
        worst_group = ordered[-sample:]
        for param, ranges in self.param_ranges.items():
            best_vals = [float(x[param]) for x in best_group if isinstance(x.get(param), (int, float))]
            worst_vals = [float(x[param]) for x in worst_group if isinstance(x.get(param), (int, float))]
            if len(best_vals) < 2 or len(worst_vals) < 2:
                continue
            if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                span = max(1e-9, float(ranges[1]) - float(ranges[0]))
            else:
                span = max(1.0, float(max(ranges) - min(ranges)))
            delta = float(np.mean(best_vals) - np.mean(worst_vals))
            confidence = min(1.0, abs(delta) / span)
            if confidence < float(self.cfg.get("directional_bias_min_confidence", 0.06)):
                continue
            hints[param] = {
                "direction": 1.0 if delta > 0 else -1.0,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "delta": delta,
            }
        return hints

    def _apply_directional_hints(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        hints = self.directional_hints or self._build_directional_hints()
        if not hints:
            return conf
        out = dict(conf)
        strength = float(self.cfg.get("directional_bias_strength", 0.14))
        for param, hint in hints.items():
            if param not in self.param_ranges or param not in out:
                continue
            ranges = self.param_ranges[param]
            direction = float(hint.get("direction", 0.0))
            confidence = float(hint.get("confidence", 0.0))
            if direction == 0.0 or confidence <= 0.0:
                continue
            if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                span = float(ranges[1] - ranges[0])
                shift = span * strength * confidence * direction
                out[param] = clip_float(float(out[param]) + shift, ranges[0], ranges[1])
            else:
                ordered = sorted(int(x) for x in ranges)
                current = int(out[param])
                if current not in ordered:
                    current = int(ordered[0])
                idx = ordered.index(current)
                step = 1 if direction > 0 else -1
                bias_steps = 1 if confidence < 0.18 else 2
                idx = int(np.clip(idx + step * bias_steps, 0, len(ordered) - 1))
                out[param] = int(ordered[idx])
        return self._fix_geometry(out)

    def _apply_protective_field_guidance(self, conf: Dict[str, Any], branch: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        if not bool(self.cfg.get("protective_field_enabled", True)):
            return conf
        if not isinstance(self.protective_field, dict):
            return conf
        out = dict(conf)
        dist = self.gravitational_distance(self.protective_field, out)
        if self.mode == 1 or branch == "hyperparam_only":
            if dist > self.protection_radius * 1.15:
                pull = float(self.cfg.get("protective_field_exploit_pull", 0.18))
                for param, ranges in self.param_ranges.items():
                    ref = self.protective_field.get(param)
                    cur = out.get(param)
                    if ref is None or cur is None:
                        continue
                    if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                        mixed = (1.0 - pull) * float(cur) + pull * float(ref)
                        out[param] = clip_float(mixed, ranges[0], ranges[1])
                    elif random.random() < pull and ref in ranges:
                        out[param] = int(ref)
        elif dist < self.protection_radius:
            push = float(self.cfg.get("protective_field_explore_push", 0.10))
            for param, ranges in self.param_ranges.items():
                ref = self.protective_field.get(param)
                cur = out.get(param)
                if ref is None or cur is None:
                    continue
                if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                    span = float(ranges[1] - ranges[0])
                    direction = -1.0 if float(cur) >= float(ref) else 1.0
                    nudged = float(cur) + direction * span * push
                    out[param] = clip_float(nudged, ranges[0], ranges[1])
                elif int(cur) == int(ref) and len(ranges) > 1:
                    ordered = sorted(int(x) for x in ranges)
                    idx = ordered.index(int(cur))
                    step = -1 if idx > 0 and random.random() < 0.5 else 1
                    idx = int(np.clip(idx + step, 0, len(ordered) - 1))
                    out[param] = int(ordered[idx])
        return self._fix_geometry(out)

    def _random_param(self, param: str):
        values = self.param_ranges[param]
        if isinstance(values, tuple):
            return random.uniform(values[0], values[1])
        return random.choice(values)

    def random_config(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        conf = {p: self._random_param(p) for p in self.param_ranges}
        if self.best_config and random.random() < 0.35:
            for param, ranges in self.param_ranges.items():
                best_value = self.best_config.get(param)
                if best_value is None:
                    continue
                if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                    span = (ranges[1] - ranges[0]) * 0.18
                    conf[param] = clip_float(float(best_value) + random.uniform(-span, span), ranges[0], ranges[1])
                else:
                    conf[param] = best_value if random.random() < 0.60 else conf[param]
        return self._fix_geometry(conf)

    def gravitational_distance(self, p1: Dict[str, Any], p2: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        coord_keys = ["lr", "gain", "temperature", "n_embd", "n_head", "n_layer"]
        vec1 = []
        vec2 = []
        for k in coord_keys:
            scale = 1.0
            ranges = self.param_ranges.get(k)
            if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                scale = max(1e-9, ranges[1] - ranges[0])
            elif isinstance(ranges, list):
                scale = max(1.0, max(ranges) - min(ranges))
            vec1.append(float(p1.get(k, 0)) / scale)
            vec2.append(float(p2.get(k, 0)) / scale)
        vec1 = np.array(vec1, dtype=np.float64)
        vec2 = np.array(vec2, dtype=np.float64)
        euclid = np.linalg.norm(vec1 - vec2)
        m1 = max(float(p1.get("score", 0.1)), 0.1)
        m2 = max(float(p2.get("score", 0.1)), 0.1)
        return float(euclid / (1.0 + self.G * (m1 + m2) + 1e-9))

    def get_elite(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.history:
            return []
        return sorted(self.history, key=lambda x: x.get("score", -float("inf")), reverse=True)[: min(self.cfg["elite_size"], len(self.history))]

    def select_parents(self, elite: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], Dict[str, Any]]:
        if len(elite) < 2:
            if len(elite) == 1:
                return elite[0], elite[0]
            base = self.random_config()
            return base, self.random_config()

        idx1 = random.randint(0, len(elite) - 1)
        parent1 = elite[idx1]
        weights = []
        for i, p2 in enumerate(elite):
            if i == idx1:
                weights.append(0.0)
            else:
                dist = self.gravitational_distance(parent1, p2)
                weights.append(1.0 / (dist + 1e-9))
        total = sum(weights)
        probs = [w / total if total else (0.0 if i == idx1 else 1.0 / max(1, len(elite) - 1)) for i, w in enumerate(weights)]
        idx2 = int(np.random.choice(len(elite), p=probs))
        return parent1, elite[idx2]

    def crossover(self, p1: Dict[str, Any], p2: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        child = {}
        for param in self.param_ranges:
            if isinstance(self.param_ranges[param], tuple):
                low = min(p1.get(param, self._random_param(param)), p2.get(param, self._random_param(param)))
                high = max(p1.get(param, self._random_param(param)), p2.get(param, self._random_param(param)))
                child[param] = random.uniform(low, high)
            else:
                child[param] = random.choice([p1.get(param, self._random_param(param)), p2.get(param, self._random_param(param))])
        return self._fix_geometry(child)

    def update_strategy(self, score: float) -> None:
        best_score = self.best_config.get("score", -float("inf")) if self.best_config else -float("inf")
        if score > best_score:
            self.strategy["exploration"] *= 0.90
            self.strategy["stability"] = min(0.95, self.strategy["stability"] + 0.08)
        else:
            self.strategy["exploration"] = min(0.92, self.strategy["exploration"] + 0.05)
            self.strategy["stability"] = max(0.10, self.strategy["stability"] - 0.03)
        diff = 0.0 if not math.isfinite(best_score) else max(0.0, best_score - score)
        self.strategy["risk"] = clip_float(0.15 + diff * 0.2, 0.10, 0.90)
        self.mode = 1 if self.strategy["exploration"] < 0.45 else 0

    def mutate(self, conf: Dict[str, Any], mutation_rate: float) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], bool]:
        mutated = False
        for param in self.param_ranges:
            if random.random() < mutation_rate:
                mutated = True
                ranges = self.param_ranges[param]
                if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                    if self.best_config and random.random() > self.strategy["exploration"]:
                        center = float(self.best_config.get(param, conf[param]))
                        span = (ranges[1] - ranges[0]) * max(0.05, 0.22 * self.strategy["exploration"])
                        conf[param] = clip_float(center + random.uniform(-span, span), ranges[0], ranges[1])
                    else:
                        conf[param] = self._random_param(param)
                else:
                    conf[param] = self._random_param(param)
        return self._fix_geometry(conf), mutated

    def _lethal_penalty(self, param: str, value: Any) -> float:
        penalty = 0.0
        regions = self.lethal_regions.get(param, {})
        key = self._region_key(param, value)
        penalty += min(1.5, 0.20 * float(regions.get(key, 0)))

        vals = self.lethal_stats.get(param, {}).get("values", [])
        ranges = self.param_ranges[param]
        if vals and isinstance(ranges, tuple):
            low, high = ranges
            span = max(1e-9, high - low)
            lethal_mean = float(np.mean(vals))
            lethal_std = float(np.std(vals) + 1e-6)
            dist = abs(float(value) - lethal_mean)
            closeness = max(0.0, 1.0 - dist / max(span * 0.35, lethal_std * 2.0))
            penalty += 1.35 * closeness
        elif isinstance(ranges, list):
            count = float(regions.get(str(int(value)), 0))
            penalty += min(1.5, 0.30 * count)
        return float(penalty)

    def avoid_lethal_zones(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], float]:
        conf = dict(conf)
        total_penalty = 0.0
        for param, ranges in self.param_ranges.items():
            value = conf.get(param)
            penalty = self._lethal_penalty(param, value)
            total_penalty += penalty
            if penalty <= 0.85:
                continue
            if isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                low, high = ranges
                span = high - low
                lethal_vals = self.lethal_stats.get(param, {}).get("values", [])
                lethal_mean = float(np.mean(lethal_vals)) if lethal_vals else float(value)
                direction = 1.0 if float(value) <= lethal_mean else -1.0
                shift = span * min(0.35, 0.08 + 0.07 * penalty)
                conf[param] = clip_float(float(value) + direction * shift, low, high)
            else:
                safe_values = sorted(ranges, key=lambda candidate: self._lethal_penalty(param, candidate))
                conf[param] = int(safe_values[0]) if safe_values else conf[param]

        signature = self._config_signature(conf)
        if signature in self.recent_failed_configs:
            return None, total_penalty + 2.0
        if total_penalty > 4.25:
            return None, total_penalty
        return self._fix_geometry(conf), total_penalty

    def propose(self, branch: str = "geometry_explorer") -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], bool, bool]:
        attempts = 0
        last_penalty = 0.0
        self.directional_hints = self._build_directional_hints()
        while attempts < 6:
            attempts += 1
            if len(self.history) < 2 or random.random() < self.strategy["exploration"]:
                child = self.random_config()
                mutated = attempts > 1
            else:
                elite = self.get_elite()
                p1, p2 = self.select_parents(elite)
                child = self.crossover(p1, p2)
                rate = float(self.cfg["mutation_rate"]) * (0.75 + 0.75 * self.strategy["exploration"])
                child, mutated = self.mutate(child, rate)

            if branch == "hyperparam_only":
                child["n_embd"] = int(self.cfg["n_embd"])
                child["n_head"] = int(self.cfg["n_head"])
                child["n_layer"] = int(self.cfg["n_layer"])
                child = self._fix_geometry(child)

            for param in ["lr", "gain", "temperature"]:
                lesson_vals = self.lessons_stats.get(param, {}).get("values", [])
                ranges = self.param_ranges[param]
                if len(lesson_vals) > 10 and isinstance(ranges, tuple):
                    mean_val = float(np.mean(lesson_vals[-32:]))
                    span = (ranges[1] - ranges[0]) * 0.08
                    if random.random() > self.strategy["exploration"]:
                        child[param] = clip_float(0.82 * float(child[param]) + 0.18 * mean_val, ranges[0], ranges[1])
                    elif abs(float(child[param]) - mean_val) < span:
                        direction = 1.0 if random.random() > 0.5 else -1.0
                        child[param] = clip_float(float(child[param]) + direction * span, ranges[0], ranges[1])

            child = self._apply_directional_hints(child)
            child = self._apply_protective_field_guidance(child, branch)

            quantum_tunnel = False
            if self.black_hole is not None:
                dist = self.gravitational_distance(self.black_hole, child)
                if dist < self.absorption_radius:
                    if random.random() < self.quantum_tunnel_prob:
                        quantum_tunnel = True
                    else:
                        self.black_hole["score"] = max(float(self.black_hole.get("score", 0.0)), float(child.get("score", 0.0)))
                        self.absorption_radius += self.absorption_growth
                        continue

            child, penalty = self.avoid_lethal_zones(child)
            last_penalty = penalty
            if child is None:
                continue

            child["gain"] = round(float(child["gain"]), 5)
            child["temperature"] = round(float(child["temperature"]), 5)
            child["lr"] = round(float(child["lr"]), 6)
            child["id"] = str(time.time()) + str(random.randint(0, 1000))
            child["branch"] = branch
            child["lethal_penalty"] = float(last_penalty)
            return child, mutated, quantum_tunnel

        if self.bad_candidate_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("swarm_bad_candidate_kill_switch", 3)):
            return None, False, False
        fallback = self.random_config()
        if branch == "hyperparam_only":
            fallback["n_embd"] = int(self.cfg["n_embd"])
            fallback["n_head"] = int(self.cfg["n_head"])
            fallback["n_layer"] = int(self.cfg["n_layer"])
            fallback = self._fix_geometry(fallback)
        fallback["gain"] = round(float(fallback["gain"]), 5)
        fallback["temperature"] = round(float(fallback["temperature"]), 5)
        fallback["lr"] = round(float(fallback["lr"]), 6)
        fallback["id"] = str(time.time()) + str(random.randint(0, 1000))
        fallback["branch"] = branch
        fallback["lethal_penalty"] = float(last_penalty)
        return fallback, True, False

    def register_lethal(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.total_lethal += 1
        self.bad_candidate_streak += 1
        self.note_swarm_result(False)
        self.recent_failed_configs.append(self._config_signature(conf))
        for param, value in conf.items():
            if param in self.lethal_stats:
                self.lethal_stats[param]["count"] += 1
                self.lethal_stats[param]["values"].append(value)
                self.lethal_stats[param]["values"] = self.lethal_stats[param]["values"][-100:]
                region_key = self._region_key(param, value)
                regions = self.lethal_regions.setdefault(param, {})
                regions[region_key] = int(regions.get(region_key, 0)) + 1

    def register_alive(self, conf: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.total_alive += 1
        self.bad_candidate_streak = 0
        if conf.get("swarm"):
            self.note_swarm_result(True)

    def switch_mode(self) -> None:
        self.mode = 1 if self.strategy["exploration"] < 0.45 else 0

    def detect_anomaly(self, metric_values: List[float], current_value: float) -> Tuple[bool, float, float, float]:
        if len(metric_values) < 8:
            return False, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0
        mean_val = float(np.mean(metric_values))
        std_val = float(np.std(metric_values) + 1e-9)
        z_score = abs(current_value - mean_val) / std_val
        cohens_d = z_score
        p_value = math.erfc(z_score / math.sqrt(2))
        alpha = self.cfg["anomaly_alpha"]
        total_experiments = max(1, len(self.history) + 1)
        fdr_threshold = alpha / total_experiments
        is_anomaly = p_value < fdr_threshold and cohens_d > self.cfg["anomaly_effect_size_min"]
        return is_anomaly, z_score, p_value, cohens_d

    def commit(self, conf: Dict[str, Any], score: float, additional: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        item = dict(conf)
        item["score"] = float(score)
        item["timestamp"] = utc_now_z()
        item.update(additional)
        self.history.append(item)
        self.register_alive(item)
        mode_snapshot = int(self.mode)
        self.update_strategy(score)

        is_record = False
        best_score = self.best_config.get("score", -float("inf")) if self.best_config else -float("inf")
        hope_promoted = False

        if score > self.hope_score:
            self.hope_score = float(score)
            self.hope_mode = mode_snapshot
            self.hope_config = dict(item)
        elif score > best_score and mode_snapshot == self.hope_mode:
            promoted = dict(self.hope_config) if isinstance(self.hope_config, dict) else dict(item)
            if float(item.get("score", -float("inf"))) > float(promoted.get("score", -float("inf"))):
                promoted = dict(item)
            self.best_config = promoted
            is_record = True
            hope_promoted = True
            if "id" in self.best_config:
                self.singularities.append(self.best_config["id"])
            self.black_hole = dict(self.best_config)
            self.protective_field = dict(self.best_config)
            self.protection_radius = float(self.cfg.get("protective_field_radius", 0.16))
            self.absorption_radius = self.cfg["absorption_radius_base"]
            self.hope_score = -float("inf")
            self.hope_config = None
            self._remember_lesson(self.best_config)
        elif (
            self.hope_config is not None
            and mode_snapshot == self.hope_mode
            and score >= self.hope_score - max(float(self.cfg.get("candidate_confirm_margin", 0.0025)), 0.01)
        ):
            if float(item.get("score", -float("inf"))) > float(self.hope_config.get("score", -float("inf"))):
                self.hope_config = dict(item)

        if not hope_promoted and score > self.candidate_score:
            self.candidate_score = float(score)
            self.candidate_mode = mode_snapshot
            self.candidate_config = dict(item)
            self.candidate_confirm_seen = 1
        elif (
            not hope_promoted
            and self.candidate_config is not None
            and mode_snapshot == self.candidate_mode
            and score >= self.candidate_score - self.cfg["candidate_confirm_margin"]
        ):
            self.candidate_confirm_seen += 1

        if not hope_promoted and self.candidate_config is not None:
            if self.candidate_confirm_seen >= self.cfg["candidate_confirm_streak"] and self.candidate_config.get("score", -float("inf")) > best_score:
                self.best_config = dict(self.candidate_config)
                is_record = True
                if "id" in self.best_config:
                    self.singularities.append(self.best_config["id"])
                self.black_hole = dict(self.best_config)
                self.protective_field = dict(self.best_config)
                self.protection_radius = float(self.cfg.get("protective_field_radius", 0.16))
                self.absorption_radius = self.cfg["absorption_radius_base"]
                self.hope_score = -float("inf")
                self.hope_config = None
                self.candidate_score = -float("inf")
                self.candidate_config = None
                self.candidate_confirm_seen = 0
                self._remember_lesson(self.best_config)
        elif score >= best_score - 0.05:
            self._remember_lesson(item)
            if isinstance(self.protective_field, dict) and score >= best_score - 0.02:
                self.protective_field["score"] = max(float(self.protective_field.get("score", best_score)), float(score))
                self.protection_radius = min(0.60, float(self.protection_radius) + float(self.protection_growth))

        self.directional_hints = self._build_directional_hints()

        return is_record



class QuantumSlimeQuantizer:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.named_trace: Dict[str, float] = {}
        self.sensor_context: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.quant_history = deque(maxlen=128)

    @staticmethod
    def _clip(value: float, low: float, high: float) -> float:
        return float(min(high, max(low, value)))

    def _sensor_factor(self, sensor_context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> float:
        ctx = sensor_context or {}
        sync = float(ctx.get("sync_hint", ctx.get("eye_sync", 0.0)) or 0.0)
        fit = float(ctx.get("fit", 0.0) or 0.0)
        loss = float(ctx.get("eye_loss", ctx.get("loss", 0.0)) or 0.0)
        entropy = float(ctx.get("entropy", 0.0) or 0.0)
        factor = 1.0
        factor += float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_sensor_sync_gain", 0.12)) * self._clip(sync, 0.0, 1.5)
        factor += float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_sensor_fit_gain", 0.10)) * self._clip(fit, 0.0, 3.0)
        factor -= float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_sensor_loss_penalty", 0.10)) * self._clip(loss, 0.0, 2.0)
        factor -= float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_sensor_entropy_penalty", 0.08)) * self._clip(entropy / 10.0, 0.0, 1.0)
        return self._clip(factor, 0.60, 1.40)

    def set_runtime_context(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        slime_trace: Dict[int, float],
        sensor_context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        diagnostics: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        factor = self._sensor_factor(sensor_context)
        trace_default = float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_trace_default", 1.0))
        named = {}
        for name, param in model.named_parameters():
            base = float(slime_trace.get(id(param), trace_default))
            diag_boost = 1.0
            if diagnostics:
                coherence = float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0) or 0.0)
                deploy_trust = float(diagnostics.get("deploy_trust", 0.0) or 0.0)
                diag_boost += 0.06 * self._clip(coherence, 0.0, 1.0)
                diag_boost += 0.04 * self._clip(deploy_trust, 0.0, 1.0)
            named[name] = self._clip(base * factor * diag_boost, 0.0, 2.0)
        self.named_trace = named
        self.sensor_context = dict(sensor_context or {})
        return {
            "trace_mean": float(np.mean(list(named.values()))) if named else 0.0,
            "trace_min": float(np.min(list(named.values()))) if named else 0.0,
            "trace_max": float(np.max(list(named.values()))) if named else 0.0,
            "sensor_factor": float(factor),
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _levels_for_bits(bits: int) -> int:
        bits = max(2, int(bits))
        return (2 ** (bits - 1)) - 1

    @classmethod
    def _quantize_scalar(cls, value: float, bits: int) -> int:
        levels = cls._levels_for_bits(bits)
        clipped = max(-1.0, min(1.0, float(value)))
        return int(round(clipped * levels))

    def quantize_array(self, arr: np.ndarray, mode: str, trace_value: float) -> Any:
        mode = str(mode).strip().lower()
        if mode == "int8":
            bits = 8
        elif mode == "int4":
            bits = 4
        else:
            if trace_value >= float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_int8_threshold", 0.70)):
                bits = 16
            elif trace_value >= float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_int4_threshold", 0.30)):
                bits = 8
            else:
                bits = 4

        if bits >= 16:
            rounded = np.round(arr.astype(np.float32), 4)
            return rounded.tolist()

        if arr.ndim == 2:
            return [[self._quantize_scalar(v, bits) for v in row] for row in arr.tolist()]
        return [self._quantize_scalar(v, bits) for v in arr.tolist()]

    def maybe_prune_tensor(self, tensor: torch.Tensor, trace_value: float) -> torch.Tensor:
        if not bool(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_prune_on_export", False)):
            return tensor
        if trace_value >= float(self.cfg.get("slime_quant_int4_threshold", 0.30)):
            return tensor
        thr = float(tensor.detach().abs().mean().item()) * float(self.cfg.get("slime_prune_cap", 0.005))
        out = tensor.detach().clone()
        out[out.abs() < thr] = 0.0
        return out

    def summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        traces = list(self.named_trace.values())
        if not traces:
            return {"trace_mean": 0.0, "trace_min": 0.0, "trace_max": 0.0, "sensor_context": dict(self.sensor_context)}
        return {
            "trace_mean": float(np.mean(traces)),
            "trace_min": float(np.min(traces)),
            "trace_max": float(np.max(traces)),
            "sensor_context": dict(self.sensor_context),
        }



class NASShadowExporter:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.quantum = QuantumSlimeQuantizer(cfg)

    def set_runtime_context(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        slime_trace: Dict[int, float],
        sensor_context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        diagnostics: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self.quantum.set_runtime_context(model, slime_trace, sensor_context=sensor_context, diagnostics=diagnostics)

    def model_config(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "arch": "ByteTransformer",
            "export_runtime": "pc_jgpt",
            "lightweight_runtime_target": "janus_nas_microgpt",
            "vocab_size": int(self.cfg["vocab_size"]),
            "block_size": int(self.cfg["block_size"]),
            "n_layer": int(self.cfg["n_layer"]),
            "n_embd": int(self.cfg["n_embd"]),
            "n_head": int(self.cfg["n_head"]),
            "dropout": float(self.cfg["dropout"]),
        }

    def nas_runtime_aliases(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "wte.weight": "wte",
            "wpe.weight": "wpe",
            "lm_head.weight": "lm_head",
        }

    def export_weight_summary(self, state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        out = {}
        for name, tensor in state_dict.items():
            arr = tensor.detach().float().cpu()
            out[name] = {
                "shape": list(arr.shape),
                "mean": float(arr.mean().item()),
                "std": float(arr.std().item()),
                "absmax": float(arr.abs().max().item()),
                "slime_trace": float(self.quantum.named_trace.get(name, 1.0)),
            }
        return out

    def export_quantized_weights(self, state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        names = ["wte.weight", "wpe.weight", "ln_f.weight", "ln_f.bias", "lm_head.weight"]
        quant_mode = str(self.cfg.get("quantize_mode", "int8")).strip().lower()
        aliases = self.nas_runtime_aliases()
        out = {}
        for n in names:
            if n not in state_dict:
                continue
            trace_value = float(self.quantum.named_trace.get(n, 1.0))
            tensor = self.quantum.maybe_prune_tensor(state_dict[n], trace_value)
            arr = tensor.detach().float().cpu().numpy()
            quantized = self.quantum.quantize_array(arr, quant_mode, trace_value)
            out[n] = quantized
            alias = aliases.get(n)
            if alias:
                out[alias] = quantized
        return out

    def transfer_manifest(self, state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        aliases = self.nas_runtime_aliases()
        exported = []
        alias_pairs = []
        pc_only = []
        for name in ["wte.weight", "wpe.weight", "ln_f.weight", "ln_f.bias", "lm_head.weight"]:
            if name not in state_dict:
                continue
            exported.append(name)
            alias = aliases.get(name)
            if alias:
                alias_pairs.append({"source": name, "target": alias})
            else:
                pc_only.append(name)
        return {
            "runtime_target": "janus_nas_microgpt",
            "exported_tensor_names": exported,
            "nas_alias_pairs": alias_pairs,
            "pc_only_tensors": pc_only,
            "quantum_slime": self.quantum.summary(),
        }

    def build_meta(self, cycle: int, best_val: float, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], transport: str, payload_branch: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        diagnostics = diagnostics if isinstance(diagnostics, dict) else {}
        return {
            "source": self.cfg["nas_source_name"],
            "device_id": self.cfg["nas_http_device_id"],
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "best_val": float(best_val),
            "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
            "transport": str(transport),
            "payload_branch": str(payload_branch),
            "search_mode": diagnostics.get("search_mode"),
            "mode_label": diagnostics.get("mode_label"),
            "model_signature": diagnostics.get("model_config"),
            "last_load_policy": diagnostics.get("last_load_policy"),
            "swarm_success_rate": diagnostics.get("swarm_success_rate"),
            "handoff_role": "pc_to_nas_quantized_export",
            "event_id": diagnostics.get("event_id"),
            "dedupe_key": diagnostics.get("dedupe_key"),
            "lineage": diagnostics.get("lineage"),
            "graph_hint": diagnostics.get("graph_hint"),
            "thought_hint": diagnostics.get("thought_hint"),
            "experience_hints": diagnostics.get("experience_hints"),
            "titan_hints": diagnostics.get("titan_hints"),
            "memory_contract": diagnostics.get("memory_contract"),
            "import_contract": diagnostics.get("import_contract"),
            "transfer_manifest": diagnostics.get("transfer_manifest"),
            "janus_context": diagnostics.get("janus_context"),
            "quantum_slime": self.quantum.summary(),
            "diagnostics": diagnostics,
            "provenance": {
                "trainer": "JGPT_CODEX.py",
                "ecosystem": ["HRAIN", "Titan", "janus_nas", "pc_to_nas_quant_handoff"],
                "windows_safe": True,
            },
        }

    def build_http_payload(self, state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor], cycle: int, best_val: float, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        payload_branch = "canonical_weights"
        return {
            "format": "janus_import_canonical_v1",
            "event_id": diagnostics.get("event_id"),
            "dedupe_key": diagnostics.get("dedupe_key"),
            "payload_branch": payload_branch,
            "transport": "http",
            "quant_mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"],
            "weights": self.export_quantized_weights(state_dict),
            "model_config": self.model_config(),
            "weight_summary": self.export_weight_summary(state_dict),
            "meta": self.build_meta(cycle, best_val, diagnostics, transport="http", payload_branch=payload_branch),
        }

    def build_smb_payload(self, state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor], cycle: int, best_val: float, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        payload_branch = "smb_quantized_model"
        return {
            "format": "janus_import_smb_quant_v1",
            "event_id": diagnostics.get("event_id"),
            "dedupe_key": diagnostics.get("dedupe_key"),
            "payload_branch": payload_branch,
            "transport": "smb_inbox",
            "quant_mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"],
            "quantized_model": {"state": self.export_quantized_weights(state_dict), "mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"]},
            "model_config": self.model_config(),
            "weight_summary": self.export_weight_summary(state_dict),
            "meta": self.build_meta(cycle, best_val, diagnostics, transport="smb_inbox", payload_branch=payload_branch),
        }


class TachyonMemoryEngine:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.max_samples = int(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_window", 192))
        self.min_samples = int(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_min_samples", 24))
        self.horizon = int(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_horizon", 3))
        self.min_horizon = int(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_min_horizon", 2))
        self.max_horizon = int(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_max_horizon", 6))
        self.ridge = float(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_ridge", 1e-3))
        self.error_alpha = float(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_error_ema_alpha", 0.12))
        self.auto_tune = bool(cfg.get("tachyon_memory_auto_tune", True))
        self.samples: List[Tuple[np.ndarray, float]] = []
        self.last_pred = None
        self.last_fit_error = None
        self.fit_error_ema = None

    def encode(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], runtime_state: Dict[str, Any], cfg: Dict[str, Any]) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.array([
            float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", diagnostics.get("ema", 0.0))),
            float(diagnostics.get("ema", diagnostics.get("val_smooth", 0.0))),
            float(diagnostics.get("div", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("mi", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("stability", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("alignment", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("entropy_w", runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0))),
            float(runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)),
            float(runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", cfg.get("teacher_forcing", 0.82))),
            float(runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0)),
            float(cfg.get("dropout", 0.1)),
        ], dtype=np.float32)

    def add_sample(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], runtime_state: Dict[str, Any], cfg: Dict[str, Any], target_val: float) -> None:
        self.samples.append((self.encode(diagnostics, runtime_state, cfg), float(target_val)))
        if len(self.samples) > self.max_samples:
            self.samples = self.samples[-self.max_samples:]

    def predict_outcome(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], runtime_state: Dict[str, Any], cfg: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[float]:
        if len(self.samples) < self.min_samples:
            return None
        x_now = self.encode(diagnostics, runtime_state, cfg)
        horizon = min(self.horizon, max(1, len(self.samples)//8))
        if len(self.samples) <= horizon:
            return None
        X=[];Y=[]
        for i in range(len(self.samples)-horizon):
            X.append(self.samples[i][0])
            Y.append(float(np.mean([self.samples[i+j][1] for j in range(1,horizon+1)])))
        X=np.stack(X,axis=0)
        Y=np.array(Y,dtype=np.float32)
        Xb=np.concatenate([X,np.ones((X.shape[0],1),dtype=np.float32)],axis=1)
        reg=float(self.ridge)
        XtX=Xb.T@Xb + np.eye(Xb.shape[1],dtype=np.float32)*reg
        Xty=Xb.T@Y
        try:
            w=np.linalg.solve(XtX,Xty)
        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
            if hasattr(self, "last_pred") and self.last_pred is not None:
                return float(self.last_pred)
            try:
                w = np.linalg.pinv(XtX) @ Xty
            except Exception:
                return None
        pred=float(np.dot(np.concatenate([x_now,np.array([1.0],dtype=np.float32)]),w))
        self.last_pred=pred
        self.last_fit_error=float(np.mean(np.abs(Xb@w-Y)))
        self.fit_error_ema = self.last_fit_error if self.fit_error_ema is None else (1.0 - self.error_alpha) * self.fit_error_ema + self.error_alpha * self.last_fit_error
        if self.auto_tune and self.fit_error_ema is not None:
            if self.fit_error_ema > 0.18:
                self.horizon = max(self.min_horizon, self.horizon - 1)
                self.ridge = min(5e-2, self.ridge * 1.10)
            elif self.fit_error_ema < 0.06:
                self.horizon = min(self.max_horizon, self.horizon + 1)
                self.ridge = max(1e-5, self.ridge * 0.98)
        return pred

class ThermalColdMemory:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any]):
        self.keep = int(cfg.get("thermal_cold_memory_keep", 24))
        self.min_better_ratio = float(cfg.get("thermal_cold_memory_min_better_ratio", 1.01))
        self.bank: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    def add_state(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], runtime_state: Dict[str, Any], model_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        entry = {
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "val": float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", float("inf"))),
            "ema": float(diagnostics.get("ema", diagnostics.get("val_smooth", float("inf")))),
            "entropy": float(diagnostics.get("entropy_w", runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0))),
            "div": float(diagnostics.get("div", 0.0)),
            "mi": float(diagnostics.get("mi", 0.0)),
            "coherence": float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0)),
            "stability": float(diagnostics.get("stability", 0.0)),
            "state": {k: v.detach().cpu().clone() for k, v in model_state.items()},
        }
        self.bank.append(entry)
        self.bank.sort(key=lambda x:(float(x["val"]),-float(x["coherence"]),-float(x["stability"])))
        self.bank=self.bank[:self.keep]

    def find_best_match(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], current_val: float) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.bank:
            return None
        entropy=float(diagnostics.get("entropy_w",1.0))
        div=float(diagnostics.get("div",0.0))
        coherence=float(diagnostics.get("coherence",0.0))
        best=None; best_dist=float("inf")
        for entry in self.bank:
            if float(entry["val"]) >= float(current_val)/self.min_better_ratio:
                continue
            dist = abs(float(entry["entropy"])-entropy)*1.0 + abs(float(entry["div"])-div)*1.25 + abs(float(entry["coherence"])-coherence)*0.60
            if dist < best_dist:
                best_dist=dist; best=entry
        return best

class EvolutionTrainer:
    def __init__(self, cfg: Dict[str, Any], logger: logging.Logger):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.logger = logger
        self.device = torch.device(cfg["device"])

        self.monitor = SystemMonitor()
        self.guard = GradientBlackholeGuard(cfg)
        self.symbiosis = SymbiosisController(cfg["batch_size"])
        self.tachyon = TachyonField(cfg["tachyon_lead_factor"])
        self.ema = RollingEMA(cfg["ema_alpha"])
        self.metric_memory = MetricMemory(cfg["world_memory_priority_keep"])
        self.world_memory = WorldMemory(cfg["world_memory_capacity"], cfg["world_memory_priority_keep"])
        self.replay = ReplayBuffer(cfg["replay_capacity"])
