        self.failures = FailureMemory(cfg["failure_memory_capacity"])
        self.slime = LightPhysarumTrace(cfg["slime_trace_decay"], cfg["slime_trace_update"], cfg["slime_prune_threshold"], cfg["slime_prune_cap"])
        self.procedural = ProceduralTextCore(cfg["procedural_seed_bank"])
        self.online_bridge = OnlineCorpusBridge(cfg["online_telemetry_timeout"], cfg["online_telemetry_urls"])
        self.evomem = DualModeEvolutionMemory(cfg)
        self.exporter = NASShadowExporter(cfg)
        self.quantum_sensor_context: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.last_quantum_trace_summary: Dict[str, Any] = {}

        self.model = ByteTransformer(cfg).to(self.device)
        self.optimizer = optim.AdamW(self.model.parameters(), lr=cfg["lr"], weight_decay=cfg["weight_decay"])

        self.runtime_state: Dict[str, float] = {
            "lr_scale": 1.0,
            "entropy_weight": cfg["entropy_weight_base"],

        self.best_state_dict = None
        self.best_optimizer_state = None
        self.best_state_cfg = None
        self.best_runtime_state = None
        self.best_runtime_missing_alerted = False
        self.best_runtime_loaded_from = ""
        self.train_data = None
        self.val_data = None
        self.corpus_meta: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.start_cycle = 1
        self.current_cycle = 0

        self.no_improve_streak = 0
        self.orbit_streak = 0
        self.http_backoff_left = 0
        self.restart_count = 0
        self.last_restart_reason = ""
        self.last_self_heal_cycle = 0
        self.div_collapse_streak = 0
        self.last_score = None
        self.last_aux = None
        self.last_improvement_cycle = 0
        self.last_train_interrupt = False
        self.training_loss_ema = None
        self.last_stable_cycle = 0
        self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = 0
        self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = 0
        self.export_suppressed_until = 0
        self.active_swarm_candidate: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self.last_swarm_rejection: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.last_swarm_acceptance: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.last_export_state: Dict[str, Any] = {"cycle": 0, "http_ok": None, "smb_ok": False, "timestamp": None}
        self.last_ecosystem_event: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.artmoney_state = self._default_artmoney_state()
        self._prev_lr_scale = float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0))
        self._prev_entropy_weight = float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))
        self._prev_coherence_metrics = {
            "prediction_error": 0.0,
            "stability": 0.0,
            "divergence": 0.0,
            "coherence": 0.0,
        }
        self.negative_alignment_streak = 0
        self.adaptive_best_val = float("inf")
        self.champion_pool: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self.last_champion_capture_cycle = 0
        self.tachyon_rollback_hits = 0

        # v14.4 mycelium / occam homeostat
        self.beta = float(cfg.get("occam_beta_init", 1.0))
        self.xi = float(cfg.get("xi_init", 0.45))
        self.tau = float(cfg.get("tau_init", 0.01))
        self.mycelium_toxic_signatures: List[str] = []

        self.clock_phase = 0.0

    def artmoney_bounds(self) -> Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]:
        return {
            "lr_scale": (0.55, 1.10),
            "teacher_forcing": (self.cfg["teacher_forcing_min"], self.cfg["teacher_forcing_max"]),
            "dropout": (self.cfg["dropout_min"], self.cfg["dropout_max"]),
            "langer_scale": (self.cfg["langer_scale_min"], self.cfg["langer_scale_max"]),
            "reflex_gain": (self.cfg["reflex_gain_min"], self.cfg["reflex_gain_max"]),
            "tachyon_gain": (self.cfg["tachyon_gain_min"], self.cfg["tachyon_gain_max"]),
            "entropy_weight": (self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"], self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"]),
        }

    def _default_artmoney_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        ranges = {}
        state: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "search_mode": self.cfg.get("search_mode_initial", "WIDE_SCAN"),
            "best_pool": [],
            "candidate_memory": [],
            "failed_recent": [],
            "improve_streak": 0,
            "degrade_streak": 0,
            "anomaly_success_streak": 0,
            "swarm_successes": 0,
            "swarm_failures": 0,
            "export_http_fail_streak": 0,
            "export_smb_fail_streak": 0,
            "recovery_expand_cycles": 0,
            "last_good_export": {},
            "directional_bias": {},
            "hope_runtime": {},
            "protective_runtime": {},
            "last_transition": utc_now_z(),
            "ranges": ranges,
        }
        for key, (lo, hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            center = (lo + hi) * 0.5
            ranges[key] = {
                "base": [float(lo), float(hi)],
                "active": [float(lo), float(hi)],
                "locked_center": float(center),
                "best_center": float(center),
                "wins": 0,
                "losses": 0,
            }
            state[f"{key}_range"] = [float(lo), float(hi)]
        return state

    def current_model_signature(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        return current_model_signature(self.cfg)

    def _set_artmoney_range(self, key: str, low: float, high: float, center: Optional[float] = None) -> None:
        lo, hi = self.artmoney_bounds()[key]
        low = clip_float(low, lo, hi)
        high = clip_float(high, low, hi)
        center = clip_float(center if center is not None else (low + high) * 0.5, low, high)
        slot = self.artmoney_state.setdefault("ranges", {}).setdefault(key, {})
        slot["base"] = [float(lo), float(hi)]
        slot["active"] = [float(low), float(high)]
        slot["locked_center"] = float(slot.get("locked_center", center))
        slot["best_center"] = float(center)
        slot["wins"] = int(slot.get("wins", 0))
        slot["losses"] = int(slot.get("losses", 0))
        self.artmoney_state[f"{key}_range"] = [float(low), float(high)]

    def _normalize_artmoney_state(self) -> None:
        default_state = self._default_artmoney_state()
        for key, value in default_state.items():
            if key not in self.artmoney_state:
                self.artmoney_state[key] = value
        self.artmoney_state.setdefault("ranges", {})
        for key, (lo, hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            legacy = self.artmoney_state.get(f"{key}_range", [lo, hi])
            if not isinstance(legacy, list) or len(legacy) != 2:
                legacy = [lo, hi]
            slot = self.artmoney_state["ranges"].get(key, {})
            active = slot.get("active", legacy)
            best_center = slot.get("best_center", sum(active) / 2.0)
            self._set_artmoney_range(key, float(active[0]), float(active[1]), float(best_center))

    def _get_artmoney_range(self, key: str) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        rng = self.artmoney_state.get(f"{key}_range", list(self.artmoney_bounds()[key]))
        return float(rng[0]), float(rng[1])

    def _blend_with_physarum_hint(self, key: str, current: float, winners: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> float:
        if not winners:
            return current
        vals = [float(w.get(key, current)) for w in winners if isinstance(w.get(key, current), (int, float))]
        if not vals:
            return current
        return float(0.85 * current + 0.15 * float(np.mean(vals)))

    def _build_runtime_directional_bias(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]:
        winners = list(self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", []))
        memory = list(self.artmoney_state.get("candidate_memory", []))
        if len(winners) < 2 or len(memory) < 8:
            return {}
        losers = sorted(memory, key=lambda x: float(x.get("score", -float("inf"))))[: max(3, min(8, len(memory) // 3))]
        winners = winners[: max(2, min(6, len(winners)))]
        hints: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
        for key, (lo, hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            good_vals = [float(x.get(key)) for x in winners if isinstance(x.get(key), (int, float))]
            bad_vals = [float(x.get(key)) for x in losers if isinstance(x.get(key), (int, float))]
            if len(good_vals) < 2 or len(bad_vals) < 2:
                continue
            span = max(1e-9, hi - lo)
            delta = float(np.mean(good_vals) - np.mean(bad_vals))
            confidence = min(1.0, abs(delta) / span)
            if confidence < float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_directional_bias_min_confidence", 0.06)):
                continue
            hints[key] = {
                "direction": 1.0 if delta > 0 else -1.0,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "delta": delta,
            }
        return hints

    def _apply_runtime_directional_bias(self, key: str, target: float, lo: float, hi: float) -> float:
        hint = self.artmoney_state.get("directional_bias", {}).get(key)
        if not isinstance(hint, dict):
            return target
        direction = float(hint.get("direction", 0.0))
        confidence = float(hint.get("confidence", 0.0))
        if direction == 0.0 or confidence <= 0.0:
            return target
        span = max(1e-9, hi - lo)
        shift = span * float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_directional_bias_strength", 0.14)) * confidence * direction
        return clip_float(float(target) + shift, lo, hi)

    def _apply_teacher_forcing_inputs(self, x: torch.Tensor, teacher_forcing: float) -> torch.Tensor:
        teacher_forcing = clip_float(teacher_forcing, self.cfg["teacher_forcing_min"], self.cfg["teacher_forcing_max"])
        if teacher_forcing >= 0.999:
            return x
        keep_mask = torch.rand(x.shape, device=x.device) < teacher_forcing
        noise = torch.randint(0, self.cfg["vocab_size"], x.shape, device=x.device)
        shifted = torch.cat([x[:, :1], x[:, :-1]], dim=1)
        use_shift = torch.rand(x.shape, device=x.device) < self.cfg.get("teacher_forcing_corrupt_prob", 0.50)
        alt = torch.where(use_shift, shifted, noise)
        return torch.where(keep_mask, x, alt)

    def _apply_runtime_dropout(self, model: nn.Module, dropout: float) -> float:
        dropout = clip_float(dropout, self.cfg["dropout_min"], self.cfg["dropout_max"])
        for module in model.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Dropout):
                module.p = dropout
            elif hasattr(module, "dropout") and isinstance(getattr(module, "dropout"), float):
                try:
                    module.dropout = dropout
                except Exception:
                    pass
        if hasattr(model, "dropout"):
            model.dropout = dropout
        return dropout

    def _entropy_regularized_loss(self, logits: torch.Tensor, y: torch.Tensor, entropy_weight: float) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, float]:
        ce = F.cross_entropy(logits.reshape(-1, self.cfg["vocab_size"]), y.reshape(-1))
        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
        entropy = (-probs * torch.log(probs + 1e-12)).sum(dim=-1).mean()
        reg = float(self.cfg.get("entropy_regularization", 0.0010)) * float(entropy_weight)
        total = ce - reg * entropy
        return total, float(entropy.detach().item())

    def _composite_stability(self, losses: List[float], grad_norms: List[float]) -> float:
        if not losses or not grad_norms:
            return 0.0
        loss_std = float(np.std(losses))
        grad_std = float(np.std(grad_norms))
        grad_mean = float(np.mean(grad_norms)) + 1e-9
        smooth_loss = 1.0 / (1.0 + loss_std)
        smooth_grad = 1.0 / (1.0 + grad_std / grad_mean)
        return float(np.clip(0.5 * smooth_loss + 0.5 * smooth_grad, 0.0, 1.5))

    def compose_score(
        self,
        val_loss: float,
        gap: float,
        mi: float,
        div: float,
        stability: float,
        anomaly_bonus: float = 0.0,
        coherence: float = 1.0,
        alignment: float = 0.0,
    ) -> float:
        gap_penalty = max(0.0, gap)
        generalization_credit = max(0.0, -gap)
        real_score = float(
            -val_loss
            - 0.55 * gap_penalty
            + 0.10 * generalization_credit
            + 0.90 * mi
            + 0.55 * div
            + 0.35 * stability
            + anomaly_bonus
        )
        coherence = clip_float(float(coherence), 0.0, 1.0)
        alignment = clip_float(float(alignment), -1.0, 1.0)
        orientation_bonus = max(0.0, alignment) * float(self.cfg.get("orientation_bonus_scale", 0.20))
        coherence_penalty = (1.0 - coherence) ** 2 * 0.50

        # v14.4 Occam beta: режем лишнюю сложность и шум
        dropout = float(self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg.get("dropout", 0.12)))
        complexity_penalty = (
            0.001 * float(self.cfg.get("n_embd", 0))
            + 0.01 * float(self.cfg.get("n_layer", 0))
            + 0.05 * max(0.0, 1.0 - dropout)
        )
        recent_grads = self.metric_memory.values("grad_norm_mean", 6)
        grad_complexity = 0.0
        if recent_grads and len(recent_grads) >= 2:
            mean_g = float(np.mean(recent_grads))
            grad_complexity = float(np.std(recent_grads) / (abs(mean_g) + 1e-8))
        recent_entropy = self.metric_memory.values("entropy_mean", 4)
        entropy_complexity = float(np.mean(recent_entropy)) if recent_entropy else 0.0
        occam_penalty = float(self.beta) * (
            float(self.cfg.get("occam_complexity_scale", 1.0)) * complexity_penalty
            + float(self.cfg.get("occam_gradstd_scale", 0.10)) * grad_complexity
            + float(self.cfg.get("occam_entropy_scale", 0.20)) * entropy_complexity
        )
        self.last_occam_penalty = float(occam_penalty)

        return float(real_score - coherence_penalty + orientation_bonus - occam_penalty)

    def compute_coherence(self, prediction_error: float, stability: float, divergence: float) -> float:
        pred_term = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(0.0, float(prediction_error)))
        div_term = 1.0 / (1.0 + max(0.0, float(divergence)))
        stability = clip_float(float(stability), 0.0, 1.0)
        coherence = (
            float(self.cfg.get("coherence_weight_pred", 0.40)) * pred_term
            + float(self.cfg.get("coherence_weight_stability", 0.35)) * stability
            + float(self.cfg.get("coherence_weight_div", 0.25)) * div_term
        )
        return clip_float(coherence, 0.0, 1.0)

    def compute_orientation_metrics(self, prediction_error: float, stability: float, divergence: float, coherence: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        prev = getattr(self, "_prev_coherence_metrics", None) or {
            "prediction_error": 0.0,
            "stability": 0.0,
            "divergence": 0.0,
            "coherence": 0.0,
        }
        prev_lr = float(getattr(self, "_prev_lr_scale", self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)))
        prev_entropy = float(getattr(self, "_prev_entropy_weight", self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])))
        cur_lr = float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0))
        cur_entropy = float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))

        update_vector = np.array([
            cur_lr - prev_lr,
            cur_entropy - prev_entropy,
        ], dtype=np.float64)
        coherence_vector = np.array([
            float(prev.get("prediction_error", 0.0)) - float(prediction_error),
            float(stability) - float(prev.get("stability", 0.0)),
            float(prev.get("divergence", 0.0)) - float(divergence),
            float(coherence) - float(prev.get("coherence", 0.0)),
        ], dtype=np.float64)

        norm_update = float(np.linalg.norm(update_vector))
        norm_coherence = float(np.linalg.norm(coherence_vector))
        if norm_update > 1e-8 and norm_coherence > 1e-8:
            alignment = float(np.dot(update_vector.mean(), coherence_vector.mean()))
            alignment = float(np.dot(update_vector, np.array([coherence_vector[0], coherence_vector[3]], dtype=np.float64)) / (norm_update * max(1e-8, float(np.linalg.norm([coherence_vector[0], coherence_vector[3]])))))
        else:
            alignment = 0.0
        alignment = clip_float(alignment, -1.0, 1.0)
        # NOTE: alignment intentionally uses only pred_err + coherence changes
        # (stability/divergence excluded to reduce noise)
        orientation_bonus = max(0.0, alignment)

        self._prev_lr_scale = cur_lr
        self._prev_entropy_weight = cur_entropy
        self._prev_coherence_metrics = {
            "prediction_error": float(prediction_error),
            "stability": float(stability),
            "divergence": float(divergence),
            "coherence": float(coherence),
        }
        return alignment, orientation_bonus

    def _should_interrupt_training_midway(
        self,
        elapsed_ratio: float,
        loss_value: float,
        recent_losses: List[float],
        diag: Dict[str, float],
        predicted_score: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> bool:
        if not self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_enabled", True):
            return False

        # v15: если уже было >2 rewind за цикл, отключаем прерывание
        if getattr(self, "mid_rewind_count_cycle", 0) > 2:
            return False

        # v14.2: даём recovery больше воздуха.
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= 18:
            return False
        in_recovery = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
        recovery_cycles = int(self.artmoney_state.get("recovery_expand_cycles", 0))
        if in_recovery and recovery_cycles < 10:
            return False

        effective_best = self._effective_best_anchor()
        # если raw-loss уже близко к якорю, не рубим хвост слишком рано
        if math.isfinite(effective_best) and float(loss_value) <= effective_best * 1.03:
            return False

        if elapsed_ratio < float(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_time_hard", 0.93)):
            return False
        if len(recent_losses) < 8:
            return False

        loss_floor = float(np.mean(recent_losses[-8:])) * float(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_loss_multiplier", 1.30))
        severe_pred = predicted_score is not None and predicted_score < float(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_pred_floor", -0.10)) - 0.10

        if diag.get("blackhole_event") in {"blackhole", "nonfinite"}:
            return True
        if severe_pred and loss_value > loss_floor * 0.98:
            return True
        if diag.get("starvation_ratio", 0.0) > 0.999 and elapsed_ratio > 0.95 and loss_value > loss_floor:
            return True
        if elapsed_ratio > 0.975 and loss_value > loss_floor * 1.02:
            return True
        return False

    def _capture_cycle_anchor(self, model: nn.Module, optimizer: Optional[optim.Optimizer] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        anchor = {"model": {k: v.detach().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}}
        if optimizer is not None:
            anchor["optimizer"] = copy.deepcopy(optimizer.state_dict())
        return anchor

    def _apply_mid_interrupt_rewind(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        optimizer: Optional[optim.Optimizer],
        runtime_state: Dict[str, float],
        anchor: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
        scope: str,
    ) -> bool:
        if not self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_rewind_enabled", True):
            return False
        if not isinstance(anchor, dict) or not isinstance(anchor.get("model"), dict):
            return False
        model.load_state_dict(anchor["model"], strict=False)
        opt_state = anchor.get("optimizer")
        if optimizer is not None and isinstance(opt_state, dict):
            optimizer.load_state_dict(copy.deepcopy(opt_state))
        lr_factor = float(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_rewind_lr_factor", 0.82))
        runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.45, float(runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * lr_factor)
        if "teacher_forcing" in runtime_state:
            runtime_state["teacher_forcing"] = clip_float(
                float(runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"])) + 0.03,
                self.cfg["teacher_forcing_min"],
                self.cfg["teacher_forcing_max"],
            )
        if "entropy_weight" in runtime_state:
            runtime_state["entropy_weight"] = clip_float(
                float(runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])) * 1.02,
                self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"],
                self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"],
            )
        if optimizer is not None:
            for pg in optimizer.param_groups:
                pg["lr"] = max(self.cfg["lr_min"], min(self.cfg["lr_max"], float(pg.get("lr", self.cfg["lr"])) * lr_factor))
        self.logger.warning(
            "MID-INTERRUPT REWIND | scope=%s | lr_scale=%.4f | teacher_forcing=%.4f",
            scope,
            float(runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)),
            float(runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"])),
        )
        return True

    def _safe_load_model_state(
        self,
        model: nn.Module,
        state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor],
        context: str,
        source_signature: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        info = compatible_state_load(model, state_dict, current_model_signature({
            "n_embd": getattr(model, "n_embd", self.cfg["n_embd"]),
            "n_head": getattr(model, "n_head", self.cfg["n_head"]),
            "n_layer": getattr(model, "n_layer", self.cfg["n_layer"]),
            "block_size": getattr(model, "block_size", self.cfg["block_size"]),
            "vocab_size": getattr(model, "vocab_size", self.cfg["vocab_size"]),
        }), source_signature=source_signature)
        self.evomem.register_load_policy(info["policy"])
        mismatch_preview = ", ".join([f"{name}:{src}->{dst}" for name, src, dst in info["sample_mismatches"][:2]]) or "none"
        self.logger.info(
            "%s | %s | matched=%s/%s | mismatched=%s | missing=%s | geom_mismatch=%s | sample=%s",
            context,
            info["policy"],
            info["matched"],
            info["total"],
            info["mismatched"],
            info["missing"],
            info["geometry_mismatch"],
            mismatch_preview,
        )
        return info

    def _swarm_geometry_ratio(self) -> float:
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until):
            return 0.0
        mode = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN")
        mapping = {
            "WIDE_SCAN": self.cfg.get("swarm_geometry_explorer_ratio_wide", 0.45),
            "NARROWING": self.cfg.get("swarm_geometry_explorer_ratio_narrow", 0.25),
            "LOCKED_HUNT": self.cfg.get("swarm_geometry_explorer_ratio_locked", 0.15),
            "RECOVERY_EXPAND": self.cfg.get("swarm_geometry_explorer_ratio_recovery", 0.35),
        }
        ratio = float(mapping.get(mode, 0.25))
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until):
            return 0.0
        return ratio

    def ecosystem_paths(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "state": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "janus_ecosystem_state.json"),
            "latest_signal": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "latest_ecosystem_signal.json"),
            "signal_log": os.path.join(self.cfg["logs_dir"], "janus_ecosystem_signal.jsonl"),
        }

    def _world_memory_source_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        by_source: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}
        for rec in self.world_memory.records:
            src = str(rec.get("source", "world") or "world")
            slot = by_source.setdefault(src, {"count": 0, "avg_score_acc": 0.0})
            slot["count"] += 1
            slot["avg_score_acc"] += float(rec.get("score", 0.0))
        ranked = []
        for src, stats in by_source.items():
            count = max(1, int(stats["count"]))
            ranked.append({
                "source": src,
                "count": int(stats["count"]),
                "avg_score": float(stats["avg_score_acc"] / count),
            })
        ranked.sort(key=lambda x: (x["count"], x["avg_score"]), reverse=True)
        return {
            "total_records": len(self.world_memory.records),
            "sources": ranked[:8],
        }

    def _best_pool_excerpt(self, limit: int = 4) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        excerpt = []
        for item in self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", [])[: max(1, limit)]:
            excerpt.append({
                "signature": item.get("signature"),
                "score": float(item.get("score", 0.0)),
                "val_raw": float(item.get("val_raw", 0.0)),
                "mi": float(item.get("mi", 0.0)),
                "div": float(item.get("div", 0.0)),
                "stability": float(item.get("stability", 0.0)),
                "lr_scale": float(item.get("lr_scale", self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0))),
                "teacher_forcing": float(item.get("teacher_forcing", self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]))),
                "dropout": float(item.get("dropout", self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))),
                "langer_scale": float(item.get("langer_scale", self.runtime_state.get("langer_scale", 1.0))),
                "reflex_gain": float(item.get("reflex_gain", self.runtime_state.get("reflex_gain", self.cfg["reflex_gain"]))),
                "tachyon_gain": float(item.get("tachyon_gain", self.runtime_state.get("tachyon_gain", self.cfg["tachyon_gain"]))),
                "entropy_weight": float(item.get("entropy_weight", self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))),
            })
        return excerpt

    def _lethal_region_summary(self, limit: int = 8) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        rows = []
        for param, regions in self.evomem.lethal_regions.items():
            if not isinstance(regions, dict):
                continue
            for region, hits in regions.items():
                rows.append({
                    "param": param,
                    "region": region,
                    "hits": int(hits),
                })
        rows.sort(key=lambda x: x["hits"], reverse=True)
        return rows[: max(1, limit)]

    def _range_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        out = {}
        for key in self.artmoney_bounds():
            slot = self.artmoney_state.get("ranges", {}).get(key, {})
            active = slot.get("active", list(self.artmoney_bounds()[key]))
            out[key] = {
                "active": [float(active[0]), float(active[1])],
                "locked_center": float(slot.get("locked_center", sum(active) / 2.0)),
                "best_center": float(slot.get("best_center", sum(active) / 2.0)),
                "wins": int(slot.get("wins", 0)),
                "losses": int(slot.get("losses", 0)),
            }
        return out

    def _build_ecosystem_signal(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], rollback_done: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        search_mode = str(self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"))
        mode_label = "EXPLORE" if self.evomem.mode == 0 else "EXPLOIT"
        device_id = str(self.cfg["nas_http_device_id"])
        source_name = str(self.cfg["nas_source_name"])
        model_config = self.exporter.model_config()
        transfer_manifest = self.exporter.transfer_manifest(self.model.state_dict())
        signal_seed = {
            "device_id": device_id,
            "source": source_name,
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "search_mode": search_mode,
            "mode_label": mode_label,
            "quant_mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"],
            "best_val": round(float(self.best_val), 6) if math.isfinite(self.best_val) else None,
            "val_smooth": round(float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", self.best_val)), 6),
            "score": round(float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)), 6),
            "model_config": model_config,
        }
        canonical = json.dumps(signal_seed, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
        event_digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()
        event_id = f"jgpt:{device_id}:cycle:{cycle}:{event_digest[:12]}"
        dedupe_key = f"{source_name}|{device_id}|{cycle}|{search_mode}|{self.cfg['quantize_mode']}|{event_digest[:16]}"

        trainer_node = f"janus:trainer:{device_id}"
        model_node = f"janus:model:{source_name}"
        export_node = f"janus:export:{device_id}:{cycle}"
        nas_node = "janus:nas:runtime:microgpt"
        search_node = f"janus:search:{search_mode.lower()}"
        thought_tags = [
            "jgpt",
            "pc",
            "trainer",
            "nas_export",
            "hrain",
            "titan",
            search_mode.lower(),
            mode_label.lower(),
            str(self.cfg["quantize_mode"]).lower(),
        ]

        graph_hint = {
            "namespace": "janus:jgpt",
            "nodes": [
                {
                    "id": trainer_node,
                    "label": "JGPT Trainer",
                    "type": "janus_trainer",
                    "device_id": device_id,
                    "description": "PC-side Demiurge trainer for Janus ecosystem",
                },
                {
                    "id": model_node,
                    "label": "JGPT Quant Export",
                    "type": "janus_model_export",
                    "quant_mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"],
                    "description": "Quantized export lineage for NAS handoff",
                },
                {
                    "id": export_node,
                    "label": f"Cycle {cycle} Export",
                    "type": "janus_export_event",
                    "cycle": int(cycle),
                    "event_id": event_id,
                    "search_mode": search_mode,
                    "description": "Cycle export event prepared for NAS and HRAIN",
                },
                {
                    "id": nas_node,
                    "label": "janus_nas MicroGPT",
                    "type": "janus_runtime_target",
                    "description": "Lightweight NAS runtime target for quant handoff",
                },
                {
                    "id": search_node,
                    "label": search_mode,
                    "type": "janus_search_mode",
                    "description": "Active evolutionary search regime",
                },
            ],
            "links": [
                {"source": trainer_node, "target": model_node, "reason": "produces", "strength": 0.96},
                {"source": model_node, "target": export_node, "reason": "exported_as", "strength": 0.95},
                {"source": export_node, "target": nas_node, "reason": "targets_runtime", "strength": 0.92},
                {"source": trainer_node, "target": search_node, "reason": "governed_by", "strength": 0.78},
                {"source": search_node, "target": export_node, "reason": "shapes_export", "strength": 0.74},
            ],
        }

        thought_hint = {
            "source": f"JGPT:{source_name}",
            "tags": thought_tags,
            "content": {
                "event_id": event_id,
                "dedupe_key": dedupe_key,
                "cycle": int(cycle),
                "search_mode": search_mode,
                "mode_label": mode_label,
                "score": float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)),
                "val_smooth": float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", self.best_val)),
                "mi": float(diagnostics.get("mi", 0.0)),
                "div": float(diagnostics.get("div", 0.0)),
                "swarm_success_rate": float(self.evomem.swarm_success_rate()),
                "summary": (
                    f"JGPT cycle {cycle} prepared NAS export in {search_mode} "
                    f"with score={float(diagnostics.get('score', 0.0)):.4f} "
                    f"val={float(diagnostics.get('val_smooth', self.best_val)):.4f}"
                ),
            },
        }

        experience_hints = [
            {
                "key": f"device:{device_id}:last_export",
                "value": {
                    "event_id": event_id,
                    "cycle": int(cycle),
                    "search_mode": search_mode,
                    "best_val": float(self.best_val),
                    "quant_mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"],
                },
            },
            {
                "key": f"trainer:{device_id}:search_state",
                "value": {
                    "search_mode": search_mode,
                    "mode_label": mode_label,
                    "ranges": self._range_summary(),
                    "best_pool": self._best_pool_excerpt(limit=3),
                    "swarm_success_rate": float(self.evomem.swarm_success_rate()),
                    "load_policy_counts": dict(self.evomem.load_policy_counts),
                },
            },
        ]

        titan_hints = {
            "cycle_result": {
                "device_id": device_id,
                "type": "cycle_result",
                "data": {
                    "cycle": int(cycle),
                    "config": {
                        "lr_scale": float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)),
                        "teacher_forcing": float(self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"])),
                        "dropout": float(self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"])),
                        "langer_scale": float(self.runtime_state.get("langer_scale", self.cfg["langer_scale"])),
                        "reflex_gain": float(self.runtime_state.get("reflex_gain", self.cfg["reflex_gain"])),
                        "tachyon_gain": float(self.runtime_state.get("tachyon_gain", self.cfg["tachyon_gain"])),
                        "entropy_weight": float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])),
                    },
                    "score": float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)),
                    "loss": float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", self.best_val)),
                    "mutual_info": float(diagnostics.get("mi", 0.0)),
                    "diversity": float(diagnostics.get("div", 0.0)),
                    "search_mode": search_mode,
                    "event_id": event_id,
                },
            },
            "detailed_metrics": {
                "device_id": device_id,
                "type": "detailed_metrics",
                "data": {
                    "cycle": int(cycle),
                    "metrics": {
                        "val_loss": float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", self.best_val)),
                        "gap": float(diagnostics.get("gap", 0.0)),
                        "mutual_info": float(diagnostics.get("mi", 0.0)),
                        "diversity": float(diagnostics.get("div", 0.0)),
                        "grad_norm": float(diagnostics.get("grad_norm_mean", 0.0)),
                        "stability": float(diagnostics.get("stability", 0.0)),
                        "swarm_success_rate": float(self.evomem.swarm_success_rate()),
                    },
                    "event_id": event_id,
                },
            },
        }

        memory_contract = {
            "search_mode": search_mode,
            "mode_label": mode_label,
            "ranges": self._range_summary(),
            "best_pool": self._best_pool_excerpt(limit=4),
            "lethal_regions": self._lethal_region_summary(limit=8),
            "world_memory": self._world_memory_source_summary(),
            "failure_count": len(self.failures.items),
            "recent_failures": [
                {"reason": item.get("reason"), "cycle": item.get("cycle"), "t": item.get("t")}
                for item in self.failures.recent(3)
            ],
            "strategy": dict(self.evomem.strategy),
            "hope_candidate": dict(self.evomem.hope_config) if isinstance(self.evomem.hope_config, dict) else None,
            "hope_score": float(self.evomem.hope_score) if math.isfinite(float(self.evomem.hope_score)) else None,
            "protective_field": dict(self.evomem.protective_field) if isinstance(self.evomem.protective_field, dict) else None,
            "protection_radius": float(self.evomem.protection_radius),
            "directional_hints": dict(self.evomem.directional_hints),
            "runtime_directional_bias": dict(self.artmoney_state.get("directional_bias", {})),
            "protective_runtime": dict(self.artmoney_state.get("protective_runtime", {})),
            "black_hole": dict(self.evomem.black_hole) if isinstance(self.evomem.black_hole, dict) else None,
            "last_good_export": dict(self.artmoney_state.get("last_good_export", {})),
        }

        import_contract = {
            "http_payload_format": "janus_import_canonical_v1",
            "smb_payload_format": "janus_import_smb_quant_v1",
            "import_request_semantics": "hint_only",
            "required_meta_fields": ["source", "device_id", "cycle", "best_val", "timestamp", "transport", "payload_branch"],
            "required_http_fields": ["weights", "quant_mode", "model_config", "meta", "transport", "payload_branch"],
            "required_smb_fields": ["quantized_model.state", "quantized_model.mode", "model_config", "meta", "transport", "payload_branch"],
            "lightweight_runtime_target": "janus_nas_microgpt",
        }

        return {
            "version": "janus_ecosystem_signal_v1",
            "event_id": event_id,
            "dedupe_key": dedupe_key,
            "event_digest": event_digest,
            "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
            "source": source_name,
            "device_id": device_id,
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "lineage": {
                "trainer_id": trainer_node,
                "model_id": model_node,
                "export_id": export_node,
                "runtime_target": "janus_nas_microgpt",
                "model_config": model_config,
            },
            "search_mode": search_mode,
            "mode_label": mode_label,
            "rollback_done": bool(rollback_done),
            "last_load_policy": self.evomem.last_load_policy,
            "graph_hint": graph_hint,
            "thought_hint": thought_hint,
            "experience_hints": experience_hints,
            "titan_hints": titan_hints,
            "memory_contract": memory_contract,
            "import_contract": import_contract,
            "transfer_manifest": transfer_manifest,
            "handoff": {
                "quant_mode": self.cfg["quantize_mode"],
                "transport_priority": ["http_push_primary", "smb_optional_fallback"],
                "model_config": model_config,
                "swarm_success_rate": float(self.evomem.swarm_success_rate()),
            },
            "co_evolution_hint": {
                "difficulty_bias": "stabilize" if search_mode == "LOCKED_HUNT" else ("expand" if search_mode == "RECOVERY_EXPAND" else "scan"),
                "stagnation": int(self.no_improve_streak),
                "improve_streak": int(self.artmoney_state.get("improve_streak", 0)),
            },
        }

    def _persist_ecosystem_signal(self, cycle: int, signal: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        paths = self.ecosystem_paths()
        per_cycle_path = os.path.join(self.cfg["exports_dir"], f"janus_signal_cycle_{cycle}.json")
        signal = dict(signal)
        signal["local_signal_path"] = per_cycle_path
        atomic_json_write(paths["latest_signal"], signal)
        atomic_json_write(per_cycle_path, signal)
        append_jsonl(paths["signal_log"], signal)
        self.last_ecosystem_event = dict(signal)

    # ---------- persistence ----------
    def memory_paths(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "metric": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "metric_memory.json"),
            "world": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "world_memory.json"),
            "replay": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "replay_memory.json"),
            "failures": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "failure_memory.json"),
            "watchdog": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "watchdog_state.json"),
            "evomem": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "evolutionary_memory.json"),
            "homeostasis": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "homeostatic_state.json"),
            "ecosystem": os.path.join(self.cfg["memory_dir"], "janus_ecosystem_state.json"),
        }

    def best_runtime_path(self) -> str:
        return os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], "best_runtime.pt")

    def _effective_best_anchor(self) -> float:
        anchors = []
        cycles_since_improvement = int(self.current_cycle) - int(self.last_improvement_cycle)

        if math.isfinite(float(self.best_val)):
            anchors.append(float(self.best_val))

        if bool(self.cfg.get("adaptive_best_anchor_enabled", True)) and math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)):
            # v14.1: если старый чемпион давно не обновлялся, adaptive-якорь получает приоритет.
            if cycles_since_improvement > 15:
                return float(self.adaptive_best_val)
            anchors.append(float(self.adaptive_best_val))

        if not anchors:
            return float("inf")
        return float(max(anchors))

    def _update_adaptive_best_anchor(self, val_smooth: float, improved: bool) -> None:
        if not bool(self.cfg.get("adaptive_best_anchor_enabled", True)):
            return
        val_smooth = float(val_smooth)
        if not math.isfinite(val_smooth):
            return
        if not math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)):
            self.adaptive_best_val = val_smooth
            return

        if improved:
            alpha = float(self.cfg.get("adaptive_best_anchor_alpha_down", 0.18))
        else:
            alpha = float(self.cfg.get("adaptive_best_anchor_alpha_up", 0.035))
        updated = (1.0 - alpha) * float(self.adaptive_best_val) + alpha * val_smooth
        floor_anchor = float(self.best_val) if math.isfinite(float(self.best_val)) else min(float(self.adaptive_best_val), val_smooth)
        max_ratio = float(self.cfg.get("adaptive_best_anchor_max_ratio", 1.28))
        ceiling = floor_anchor * max_ratio if math.isfinite(floor_anchor) else updated
        self.adaptive_best_val = clip_float(max(updated, floor_anchor), floor_anchor, max(ceiling, floor_anchor + 1e-6))

        # v14.3a: в RECOVERY_EXPAND adaptive best подтягивается заметно быстрее
        if self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND":
            alpha_recovery = 0.25
            blended = (1.0 - alpha_recovery) * float(self.adaptive_best_val) + alpha_recovery * val_smooth
            self.adaptive_best_val = max(blended, val_smooth * 0.98)

    def _normalize_recovery_windows(self, cycle: int) -> None:
        max_safe = min(12, int(self.cfg.get("safe_hyperparam_only_cycles_after_regression", 8)))
        max_geom = min(12, int(self.cfg.get("geometry_explorer_disable_cycles_after_regression", 8)))
        max_export = min(12, int(self.cfg.get("export_suppress_cycles_after_regression", 6)))
        self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = min(int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until), int(cycle) + max_safe)
        self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = min(int(self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until), int(cycle) + max_geom)
        self.export_suppressed_until = min(int(self.export_suppressed_until), int(cycle) + max_export)

    def _register_mycelium_toxic_signature(self, signature: str) -> None:
        if not signature:
            return
        pool = [x for x in self.mycelium_toxic_signatures if x != signature]
        pool.append(signature)
        keep = max(8, int(self.cfg.get("mycelium_toxic_pool_keep", 64)))
        self.mycelium_toxic_signatures = pool[-keep:]

    def _candidate_topological_similarity(self, candidate_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        key_subset = ["wte.weight", "wpe.weight", "lm_head.weight"]
        current_state = self.model.state_dict()
        sims = []
        for key in key_subset:
            if key in candidate_state and key in current_state:
                v1 = candidate_state[key].detach().float().flatten().cpu()
                v2 = current_state[key].detach().float().flatten().cpu()
                if v1.numel() and v2.numel() and v1.numel() == v2.numel():
                    denom = float(torch.norm(v1) * torch.norm(v2) + 1e-8)
                    sims.append(float(torch.dot(v1, v2) / denom))
        return float(np.mean(sims)) if sims else 0.0

    def _symbiotic_sandbox_check(self, candidate: Dict[str, Any], branch: str = "hyperparam_only") -> Tuple[bool, Dict[str, Any]]:
        if branch != "geometry_explorer" or self.xi < float(self.cfg.get("mycelium_isolation_ratio_threshold", 0.70)):
            return True, {"reason": "sandbox_skipped"}
        batches = self._capture_holdout_batches(int(self.cfg.get("mycelium_sandbox_batches", 3)))
        source_signature = {
            "n_embd": int(candidate["config"].get("n_embd", self.cfg["n_embd"])),
            "n_head": int(candidate["config"].get("n_head", self.cfg["n_head"])),
            "n_layer": int(candidate["config"].get("n_layer", self.cfg["n_layer"])),
            "block_size": int(self.cfg["block_size"]),
            "vocab_size": int(self.cfg["vocab_size"]),
        }
        projected_eval = self._evaluate_projected_state_on_holdout(
            candidate["best_state"],
            source_signature,
            batches,
            runtime_candidate=candidate.get("runtime_candidate"),
        )
        current_eval = self._evaluate_model_on_holdout(self.model, batches)
        if projected_eval is None:
            return False, {"reason": "sandbox_unusable"}
        margin = 1.0 - 0.008 * (1.0 + max(0.0, float(self.xi)))
        ok = float(projected_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))) <= float(current_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))) * margin
        return ok, {
            "reason": "sandbox_ok" if ok else "sandbox_regression",
            "projected_val_loss": float(projected_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))),
            "current_val_loss": float(current_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))),
        }

    def _update_occam_beta_and_mycelium(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        val_explosion = "val_explosion" in str((self.last_swarm_rejection or {}).get("reason", ""))
        honest_reward = float(diagnostics.get("honest_reward", 0.0))
        coherence = float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0))
        if self.div_collapse_streak > 2 or self.no_improve_streak > 5 or val_explosion:
            self.beta = min(float(self.cfg.get("occam_beta_max", 2.0)), float(self.beta) * 1.05)
        elif self.artmoney_state.get("improve_streak", 0) > 3 and coherence > 0.8:
            self.beta = max(float(self.cfg.get("occam_beta_min", 0.2)), float(self.beta) * 0.98)
        else:
            self.beta = float(self.beta) * 0.995 + 0.005 * 1.0

        if val_explosion or honest_reward < -1.0:
            self.xi = min(float(self.cfg.get("xi_max", 0.95)), float(self.xi) + 0.05)
            self.tau = min(float(self.cfg.get("tau_max", 0.05)), float(self.tau) * 1.03)
            self.stable_cycle_streak = 0
        else:
            self.stable_cycle_streak += 1
            if self.stable_cycle_streak > 10:
                self.xi = max(float(self.cfg.get("xi_min", 0.2)), float(self.xi) - 0.02)
            self.tau = max(float(self.cfg.get("tau_min", 0.005)), float(self.tau) * 0.997)

        if float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0)) > float(self.cfg.get("tachyon_brake_pred_err", 0.80)) or float(diagnostics.get("divergence", 0.0)) > float(self.cfg.get("tachyon_brake_divergence", 0.50)):
            self.logger.warning(
                "TACHYON BRAKE | pred_err=%.3f | divergence=%.3f | forcing NARROWING",
                float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0)),
                float(diagnostics.get("divergence", 0.0)),
            )
            self.artmoney_state["search_mode"] = "NARROWING"
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.6, float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * 0.92)
            self.runtime_state["teacher_forcing"] = min(0.85, float(self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", 0.82)) + 0.05)

    def _capture_champion_pool_entry(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, tag: str = "runtime") -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.best_state_dict, dict) or not self.best_state_dict:
            return
        if int(cycle) == int(getattr(self, "last_champion_capture_cycle", 0)):
            return
        entry = {
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "tag": str(tag),
            "val": float(self.best_val),
            "adaptive_best_val": float(self.adaptive_best_val) if math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)) else None,
            "score": float((diagnostics or {}).get("score", -float("inf"))),
            "coherence": float((diagnostics or {}).get("coherence", 0.0)),
            "alignment": float((diagnostics or {}).get("alignment", 0.0)),
            "state_dict": {k: v.detach().cpu().clone() for k, v in self.best_state_dict.items()},
            "optimizer_state": copy.deepcopy(self.best_optimizer_state) if isinstance(self.best_optimizer_state, dict) else None,
            "state_cfg": dict(self.best_state_cfg) if isinstance(self.best_state_cfg, dict) else self.current_model_signature(),
            "runtime_state": dict(self.best_runtime_state) if isinstance(self.best_runtime_state, dict) else dict(self.runtime_state),
            "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
        }
        pool = [x for x in self.champion_pool if int(x.get("cycle", -1)) != int(cycle)]
        pool.append(entry)
        current_cycle = int(cycle)
        pool.sort(
            key=lambda x: (
                float(x.get("val", float("inf"))) - math.exp(-float(self.tau) * max(0, current_cycle - int(x.get("cycle", current_cycle)))) * 0.01,
                -float(x.get("score", -float("inf"))),
                -float(x.get("coherence", 0.0)),
            )
        )
        keep = max(1, int(self.cfg.get("champion_pool_size", 5)))
        self.champion_pool = pool[:keep]
        self.last_champion_capture_cycle = int(cycle)

    def _select_champion_restore_entry(self, reason: str = "restore") -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.champion_pool:
            return None
        effective = self._effective_best_anchor()
        margin = float(self.cfg.get("champion_pool_keep_margin", 1.08))
        candidates = [x for x in self.champion_pool if float(x.get("val", float("inf"))) <= effective * margin]
        if not candidates:
            candidates = list(self.champion_pool)
        if reason in ("mid_interrupt_guard", "tachyon_preemptive", "negative_alignment_streak"):
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (
                -float(x.get("coherence", 0.0)),
                float(x.get("val", float("inf"))),
                -float(x.get("score", -float("inf"))),
            ))
        else:
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: (
                float(x.get("val", float("inf"))),
                -float(x.get("score", -float("inf"))),
                -float(x.get("coherence", 0.0)),
            ))
        return candidates[0] if candidates else None

    def _project_tachyon_rollback_val(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        current_val = float(diagnostics.get("val_smooth", float("inf")))
        recent_vals = self.metric_memory.values("val_smooth", 4)
        slope = 0.0
        if len(recent_vals) >= 2:
            slope = (float(recent_vals[-1]) - float(recent_vals[0])) / max(1, len(recent_vals) - 1)
        horizon = max(1, int(self.cfg.get("tachyon_rollback_horizon", 4)))
        prediction_error = max(0.0, float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0)))
        projected = current_val + max(0.0, slope) * horizon + prediction_error * 0.15
        if float(diagnostics.get("predicted_score", 0.0)) < float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)):
            projected += abs(float(diagnostics.get("predicted_score", 0.0)) - float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0))) * 0.08
        return float(projected)

    def _should_preemptive_tachyon_rollback(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if not bool(self.cfg.get("tachyon_rollback_enabled", True)):
            return False
        if int(cycle) - int(self.last_improvement_cycle) <= int(self.cfg.get("tachyon_rollback_grace_cycles", 6)):
            return False
        effective_best = self._effective_best_anchor()
        if not math.isfinite(effective_best):
            return False
        projected_val = self._project_tachyon_rollback_val(diagnostics)
        diagnostics["tachyon_projected_val"] = projected_val
        score_drop = float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)) - float(diagnostics.get("predicted_score", 0.0))
        if (
            projected_val > effective_best * float(self.cfg.get("tachyon_rollback_margin", 1.06))
            and score_drop > float(self.cfg.get("tachyon_rollback_score_drop", 0.22))
            and float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 1.0)) < 0.90
        ):
            return True
        return False

    def _stable_checkpoint_candidates(self) -> List[str]:
        if not os.path.isdir(self.cfg["save_dir"]):
            return []
        found = []
        for name in os.listdir(self.cfg["save_dir"]):
            m = re.match(r"checkpoint_(\d+)\.pt$", name)
            if m:
                found.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], name)))
        found.sort(reverse=True)
        limit = max(1, int(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_rebuild_scan_limit", 8)))
        return [path for _, path in found[:limit]]

    def _persist_best_runtime_snapshot(self, historical_best_val: Optional[float] = None) -> None:
        try:
            payload = {
                "best_state_dict": self.best_state_dict,
                "best_optimizer_state": self.best_optimizer_state,
                "best_state_cfg": self.best_state_cfg,
                "best_runtime_state": self.best_runtime_state,
                "best_val": self.best_val,
                "adaptive_best_val": self.adaptive_best_val,
                "champion_pool": self.champion_pool,
                "runtime_state": self.runtime_state,
                "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
            }
            if historical_best_val is not None and math.isfinite(float(historical_best_val)):
                payload["historical_best_val"] = float(historical_best_val)
            torch.save(payload, self.best_runtime_path())
        except Exception as exc:
            self.logger.warning("BEST-RUNTIME snapshot save skipped: %s", exc)

    def _load_best_runtime_snapshot(self, path: str, context: str) -> bool:
        try:
            best_cp = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu")
            best_state = best_cp.get("best_state_dict")
            if not isinstance(best_state, dict) or not best_state:
                raise RuntimeError("best_state_dict missing or empty")
            self.best_state_dict = best_state
            bos = best_cp.get("best_optimizer_state")
            self.best_optimizer_state = bos if isinstance(bos, dict) else None
            bcfg = best_cp.get("best_state_cfg", {})
            self.best_state_cfg = bcfg if isinstance(bcfg, dict) else self.current_model_signature()
            brs = best_cp.get("best_runtime_state")
            self.best_runtime_state = brs if isinstance(brs, dict) else dict(self.runtime_state)
            self.best_val = float(best_cp.get("best_val", self.best_val))
            self.adaptive_best_val = float(best_cp.get("adaptive_best_val", self.adaptive_best_val))
            cp_pool = best_cp.get("champion_pool")
            if isinstance(cp_pool, list):
                self.champion_pool = cp_pool[: max(1, int(self.cfg.get("champion_pool_size", 5)))]
            self.best_runtime_missing_alerted = False
            self.best_runtime_loaded_from = f"{context}:{os.path.basename(path)}"
            return True
        except Exception as exc:
            self.logger.error(
                "BEST-RUNTIME SNAPSHOT LOAD FAILED | context=%s | path=%s | error=%s",
                context,
                path,
                exc,
            )
            return False

    def _rebuild_best_runtime_from_checkpoints(self, context: str) -> bool:
        candidates = self._stable_checkpoint_candidates()
        if not candidates:
            self.logger.error(
                "BEST-RUNTIME REBUILD FAILED | context=%s | reason=no_stable_checkpoints | save_dir=%s",
                context,
                self.cfg["save_dir"],
            )
            return False

        historical_best = float(self.best_val) if math.isfinite(float(self.best_val)) else float("inf")
        eval_batches = max(2, int(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_rebuild_eval_batches", 6)))
        try:
            batches = self._capture_holdout_batches(eval_batches)
        except Exception as exc:
            self.logger.error("BEST-RUNTIME REBUILD FAILED | context=%s | reason=holdout_capture | error=%s", context, exc)
            return False

        best_pick = None
        for path in candidates:
            try:
                cp = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu")
            except Exception as exc:
                self.logger.warning("BEST-RUNTIME REBUILD skipped checkpoint | path=%s | error=%s", path, exc)
                continue
            state_dict = cp.get("model_state")
            if not isinstance(state_dict, dict) or not state_dict:
                continue
            source_signature = cp.get("model_config", {})
            runtime_candidate = cp.get("runtime_state") if isinstance(cp.get("runtime_state"), dict) else None
            metrics = self._evaluate_projected_state_on_holdout(
                state_dict,
                source_signature if isinstance(source_signature, dict) else self.current_model_signature(),
                batches,
                runtime_candidate=runtime_candidate,
            )
            if metrics is None:
                continue
            score = float(metrics.get("val_loss", float("inf")))
            if best_pick is None or score < best_pick["metrics"]["val_loss"]:
                best_pick = {
                    "path": path,
                    "checkpoint": cp,
                    "metrics": metrics,
                }

        if best_pick is None:
            self.logger.error("BEST-RUNTIME REBUILD FAILED | context=%s | reason=no_usable_checkpoint", context)
            return False

        cp = best_pick["checkpoint"]
        self.best_state_dict = {k: v.detach().cpu().clone() for k, v in cp["model_state"].items()}
        bos = cp.get("optimizer_state")
        self.best_optimizer_state = bos if isinstance(bos, dict) else None
        bcfg = cp.get("model_config", {})
        self.best_state_cfg = bcfg if isinstance(bcfg, dict) else self.current_model_signature()
        brs = cp.get("runtime_state")
        self.best_runtime_state = dict(brs) if isinstance(brs, dict) else dict(self.runtime_state)
        self.best_val = float(best_pick["metrics"]["val_loss"])
        if not math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)):
            self.adaptive_best_val = float(self.best_val)
        self.best_runtime_missing_alerted = False
        self.best_runtime_loaded_from = f"rebuild:{os.path.basename(best_pick['path'])}"
        self._persist_best_runtime_snapshot(historical_best_val=historical_best)
        self.logger.warning(
            "BEST-RUNTIME REBUILT FROM STABLE CHECKPOINT | context=%s | source=%s | operational_best=%.4f | historical_best=%.4f",
            context,
            os.path.basename(best_pick["path"]),
            self.best_val,
            historical_best,
        )
        return True

    def _ensure_best_runtime_anchor(self, context: str) -> bool:
        if isinstance(self.best_state_dict, dict) and self.best_state_dict:
            return True

        best_runtime_path = self.best_runtime_path()
        if os.path.exists(best_runtime_path) and self._load_best_runtime_snapshot(best_runtime_path, context):
            return True

        if not self.best_runtime_missing_alerted:
            self.logger.error(
                "BEST-RUNTIME SNAPSHOT MISSING | context=%s | path=%s | known_best=%.4f",
                context,
                best_runtime_path,
                float(self.best_val) if math.isfinite(float(self.best_val)) else float("inf"),
            )
            self.best_runtime_missing_alerted = True
        return self._rebuild_best_runtime_from_checkpoints(context)

    def _progress_bar(self, current: int, total: int) -> str:
        total = max(1, int(total))
        current = max(0, min(int(current), total))
        width = max(10, int(self.cfg.get("terminal_progress_bar_width", 28)))
        filled = int(round(width * (current / total)))
        return "[" + "#" * filled + "." * max(0, width - filled) + f"] {current}/{total}"

    def _action_explanation_ru(self, action: str) -> str:
        mapping = {
            "continue": "цикл идёт без защитного вмешательства",
            "overfit": "train улучшается быстрее, чем val; governor наблюдает перекос",
            "freeze": "обнаружен сильный уход от лучшего состояния; нужен жёсткий откат",
            "mid_interrupt": "защитный ранний стоп внутри цикла; это не падение процесса, а экономия времени на плохом хвосте",
        }
        return mapping.get(str(action), "служебное решение governor")

    def _search_explanation_ru(self, mode: str) -> str:
        mapping = {
            "WIDE_SCAN": "широкий поиск по пространству параметров",
            "NARROWING": "сужение диапазона вокруг сильных зон",
            "LOCKED_HUNT": "осторожный дожим лучшей найденной области",
            "RECOVERY_EXPAND": "контролируемое расширение после деградации или неудачной серии",
        }
        return mapping.get(str(mode), "эволюционный режим поиска")

    def _meta_mode_explanation_ru(self, mode: str) -> str:
        mapping = {
            "EXPLORE": "ищет новые области и пробует варианты",
            "EXPLOIT": "дожимает уже найденные сильные решения",
        }
        return mapping.get(str(mode), "мета-режим эволюции")

    def _export_explanation_ru(self, reason: str) -> str:
        mapping = {
            "cycle_gap": "экспорт пока не нужен: слишком мало циклов прошло после прошлого пакета",
            "regression_cooldown": "экспорт специально подавлен, чтобы не слать на NAS слабое или нестабильное состояние",
            "swarm_aftershock_pending": "после swarm-кандидата ещё идёт карантин, экспорт ждёт подтверждения",
            "mid_interrupt_recovery": "цикл прерван защитой в recovery-режиме, экспорт блокируется",
            "anomaly_recovery_collapse": "обнаружена плохая аномалия val, экспорт блокируется как защита",
            "post_self_heal": "экспорт временно закрыт сразу после self-heal",
            "recovery_expand_unconfirmed": "RECOVERY_EXPAND ещё не подтвердил стабильность",
            "improved": "есть подтверждённое улучшение, экспорт разрешён",
            "forced_after_stall": "долгий застой, система отправляет контрольный срез",
            "min_improvement": "ожидается более заметное улучшение перед новым экспортом",
            "export_block_ratio": "состояние слишком далеко от лучшего для экспорта",
        }
        return mapping.get(str(reason), "служебное решение export-governor")

    def _checkpoint_explanation_ru(self, stable: bool, reason: str) -> str:
        if stable:
            return "сохранён нормальный стабильный checkpoint"
        mapping = {
            "mid_interrupt": "checkpoint ушёл в карантин, потому что цикл завершён ранним защитным стопом",
            "regression_cooldown": "checkpoint в карантине: состояние пока нельзя считать экспортно- или runtime-безопасным",
            "rollback": "checkpoint в карантине, потому что был откат к champion состоянию",
            "anomaly_val": "checkpoint в карантине из-за подозрительной аномалии validation",
            "recovery_expand": "checkpoint в карантине, пока recovery-режим не подтвердил устойчивость",
            "post_self_heal": "checkpoint в карантине сразу после self-heal",
        }
        return mapping.get(str(reason), "checkpoint сохранён в карантинной полосе")

    def _relation_explanation_ru(
        self,
        diagnostics: Dict[str, Any],
        val_smooth: float,
        rollback_done: bool,
        export_allowed: bool,
        export_reason: str,
    ) -> str:
        action = str(diagnostics.get("action"))
        search_mode = str(self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"))
        if rollback_done:
            return "Связь простая: governor решил, что текущее состояние хуже допустимого, и вернул систему к champion-anchor."
        if action == "mid_interrupt" and float(val_smooth) <= float(self.best_val) + 1e-9:
            return "Несмотря на mid_interrupt, ema не растёт вверх: значит защита сейчас режет лишний хвост цикла, а качество всё равно улучшается."
        if not export_allowed:
            return "Линия принятия решений такая: val -> ema -> best определяют качество, а export-governor отдельно решает, можно ли это состояние отправлять наружу."
        if search_mode == "LOCKED_HUNT":
            return "Сейчас система дожимает сильную область: ArtMoney narrowing удерживает диапазоны, а governor следит, чтобы main не ушёл в хаос."
        return "Главная связка цикла: val формирует ema, ema сравнивается с best, а mi/div/stability добавляют смысл в composite score."

    def _log_terminal_legend(self, cycle: int, force: bool = False) -> None:
        if not self.cfg.get("terminal_human_logs", True):
            return
        every = max(1, int(self.cfg.get("terminal_legend_every", 25)))
        last_cycle = int(getattr(self, "_last_terminal_legend_cycle", -10**9))
        if not force and cycle - last_cycle < every:
            return
        self._last_terminal_legend_cycle = int(cycle)
        self.logger.info("=== ЛЕГЕНДА JGPT ===")
        self.logger.info("Качество: train = loss на обучении | val = raw validation | ema = сглаженный val | best = лучший ema-якорь")
        self.logger.info("Смысл: mi = информативность | div = разнообразие | score = составная полезность, где выше обычно лучше")
        self.logger.info("Контур: val -> ema -> best управляет откатами и champion-anchor; mi/div/stability влияют на выбор swarm и эволюции")
        self.logger.info("Память  : hope_candidate = сильный претендент | protective_field = защищённая сильная зона | directional_bias = урок из лучших и худших")
        self.logger.info("Режимы: EXPLORE/EXPLOIT = поведение эволюции | WIDE_SCAN/NARROWING/LOCKED_HUNT/RECOVERY_EXPAND = стиль поиска")
        self.logger.info("Action mid_interrupt = не crash, а ранний защитный стоп хвоста цикла, чтобы не жечь время на плохом участке")
        self.logger.info("v15.3: GEX Plateau Breaker | fingerprint dedupe + dual-axis export + plateau breakout")

    def _log_cycle_human_summary(
        self,
        cycle: int,
        block_start: int,
        block_end: int,
        train_loss: float,
        val_raw: float,
        val_smooth: float,
        metrics: Dict[str, Any],
        diagnostics: Dict[str, Any],
        rollback_done: bool,
        export_allowed: bool,
        export_reason: str,
        checkpoint_stable: bool,
        checkpoint_reason: str,
    ) -> None:
        if not self.cfg.get("terminal_human_logs", True):
            return
        every = max(1, int(self.cfg.get("terminal_explain_every", 1)))
        if cycle % every != 0:
            return

        local_idx = int(cycle) - int(block_start) + 1
        local_total = max(1, int(block_end) - int(block_start) + 1)
        meta_mode = "EXPLORE" if self.evomem.mode == 0 else "EXPLOIT"
        search_mode = str(self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"))
        delta_best = float(val_smooth) - float(self.best_val)
        export_state = "РАЗРЕШЁН" if export_allowed else "ЗАБЛОКИРОВАН"

        self.logger.info("=== ЦИКЛ %s | %s ===", cycle, self._progress_bar(local_idx, local_total))
        self.logger.info(
            "Качество : train=%.4f | val=%.4f | ema=%.4f | best=%.4f | delta_best=%+.4f",
            train_loss, val_raw, val_smooth, self.best_val, delta_best,
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Смысл    : score=%.4f | mi=%.4f | div=%.4f | pred_aux=%.4f | honest=%.4f",
            diagnostics["score"], diagnostics["mi"], diagnostics["div"], diagnostics["predicted_aux"], diagnostics["honest_reward"],
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Режим    : meta=%s (%s) | search=%s (%s) | action=%s",
            meta_mode,
            self._meta_mode_explanation_ru(meta_mode),
            search_mode,
            self._search_explanation_ru(search_mode),
            diagnostics["action"],
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Память   : hope=%s | protection=%.3f | dir_bias=%s",
            "ON" if isinstance(self.evomem.hope_config, dict) else "OFF",
            float(self.evomem.protection_radius),
            len(self.artmoney_state.get("directional_bias", {})),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Нагрузка : CPU=%.0f%% | RAM=%.0f%% | GPU=%.0f%% | orbit=%.2f | entropy_w=%.3f | failures=%s",
            metrics.get("cpu_util", 0.0),
            metrics.get("ram_util", 0.0),
            metrics.get("gpu_util", 0.0),
            diagnostics["orbit_ratio"],
            self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0),
            len(self.failures.items),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Часы    : tick=%s | фаза=%.3f | батарея=%.3f | торможение=%s | accel=%s",
            diagnostics.get("do_clock_tick", False),
            float(diagnostics.get("clock_phase", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("battery_charge", 1.0)),
            diagnostics.get("clock_brake", False),
            diagnostics.get("clock_accel", False),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Память  : tachyon_mem=%s | mem_ok=%s | thermal_revert=%s",
            ("%.4f" % float(diagnostics.get("tachyon_memory_pred"))) if diagnostics.get("tachyon_memory_pred") is not None else "none",
            diagnostics.get("memory_says_ok", False),
            diagnostics.get("thermal_revert", False),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Mycelium: toxic=%d | champions=%d | phi_trust=%.3f | phi_penalty=%.3f | phi_probe=%+.3f",
            len(self.mycelium_toxic_signatures),
            len(self.champion_pool),
            float(diagnostics.get("phi_trust", 1.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("phi_penalty", 1.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("phi_probe_delta", 0.0)),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "GEX/ΔG : gex=%+.3f | ΔG_mix=%.3f | temp=%.2f | deploy=%.3f | plateau=%s",
            float(diagnostics.get("gex", 0.0)),
            float((self.last_swarm_acceptance or self.last_swarm_rejection or {}).get("mixing_free_energy", 0.0)),
            float(diagnostics.get("jitter", 0.0)) * 1000.0,
            float(diagnostics.get("deploy_trust", 0.0)),
            self._is_plateau(),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Экспорт  : %s | reason=%s | %s",
            export_state,
            export_reason,
            self._export_explanation_ru(export_reason),
        )
        self.logger.info(
            "Сейв     : %s | reason=%s | %s",
            "STABLE" if checkpoint_stable else "QUARANTINE",
            checkpoint_reason,
            self._checkpoint_explanation_ru(checkpoint_stable, checkpoint_reason),
        )
        self.logger.info("Пояснение: %s", self._action_explanation_ru(str(diagnostics.get("action"))))
        self.logger.info("Связь    : %s", self._relation_explanation_ru(diagnostics, val_smooth, rollback_done, export_allowed, export_reason))

    def save_memories(self):
        p = self.memory_paths()
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()

        def _safe_persist(label: str, fn, *args, **kwargs) -> bool:
            try:
                fn(*[make_json_safe(a) for a in args], **{k: make_json_safe(v) for k, v in kwargs.items()})
                return True
            except PermissionError as exc:
                self.logger.warning("MEMORY SAVE LOCKED | target=%s | error=%s", label, exc)
                return False
            except Exception as exc:
                self.logger.warning("MEMORY SAVE FAILED | target=%s | error=%s", label, exc)
                return False

        _safe_persist("metric_memory", self.metric_memory.save, p["metric"])
        _safe_persist("world_memory", self.world_memory.save, p["world"])
        _safe_persist("replay_memory", self.replay.save, p["replay"])
        _safe_persist("failure_memory", self.failures.save, p["failures"])
        _safe_persist("evolutionary_memory", self.evomem.save, p["evomem"])
        _safe_persist("watchdog_state", atomic_json_write, p["watchdog"], {
            "no_improve_streak": self.no_improve_streak,
            "orbit_streak": self.orbit_streak,
            "http_backoff_left": self.http_backoff_left,
            "restart_count": self.restart_count,
            "last_restart_reason": self.last_restart_reason,
            "last_self_heal_cycle": self.last_self_heal_cycle,
            "div_collapse_streak": self.div_collapse_streak,
            "last_stable_cycle": self.last_stable_cycle,
            "geometry_explorer_disabled_until": self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until,
            "safe_hyperparam_only_until": self.safe_hyperparam_only_until,
            "export_suppressed_until": self.export_suppressed_until,
            "active_swarm_candidate": self.active_swarm_candidate,
            "last_swarm_rejection": self.last_swarm_rejection,
            "last_swarm_acceptance": self.last_swarm_acceptance,
            "runtime_state": self.runtime_state,
            "best_val": self.best_val,
            "adaptive_best_val": self.adaptive_best_val,
            "champion_pool": self.champion_pool,
            "last_improvement_cycle": self.last_improvement_cycle,
            "last_train_interrupt": self.last_train_interrupt,
            "last_export_state": self.last_export_state,
            "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
        })
        _safe_persist("homeostatic_state", atomic_json_write, p["homeostasis"], {
            "cycle": self.start_cycle,
            "last_score": self.last_score,
            "last_aux": self.last_aux,
            "best_val": self.best_val,
            "adaptive_best_val": self.adaptive_best_val,
            "champion_pool": self.champion_pool,
            "failed_configs_count": self.evomem.total_lethal,
            "artmoney_state": self.artmoney_state,
            "search_mode": self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"),
            "load_policy_counts": self.evomem.load_policy_counts,
            "swarm_successes": self.evomem.swarm_successes,
            "swarm_failures": self.evomem.swarm_failures,
            "best_pool": self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", []),
            "candidate_memory": self.artmoney_state.get("candidate_memory", []),
            "failed_recent": self.artmoney_state.get("failed_recent", []),
            "last_good_export": self.artmoney_state.get("last_good_export", {}),
            "best_runtime_state": self.best_runtime_state,
            "best_runtime_loaded_from": self.best_runtime_loaded_from,
            "active_swarm_candidate": self.active_swarm_candidate,
            "last_swarm_rejection": self.last_swarm_rejection,
            "last_swarm_acceptance": self.last_swarm_acceptance,
            "geometry_explorer_disabled_until": self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until,
            "safe_hyperparam_only_until": self.safe_hyperparam_only_until,
            "export_suppressed_until": self.export_suppressed_until,
            "last_ecosystem_event": self.last_ecosystem_event,
            "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
        })
        _safe_persist("ecosystem_state", atomic_json_write, p["ecosystem"], {
            "last_ecosystem_event": self.last_ecosystem_event,
            "last_export_state": self.last_export_state,
            "search_mode": self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"),
            "swarm_success_rate": self.evomem.swarm_success_rate(),
            "world_memory": self._world_memory_source_summary(),
            "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
        })

    def load_memories(self):
        p = self.memory_paths()
        self.metric_memory.load(p["metric"])
        self.world_memory.load(p["world"])
        self.replay.load(p["replay"])
        self.failures.load(p["failures"])
        self.evomem.load(p["evomem"])

        if os.path.exists(p["watchdog"]):
            try:
                data = json.loads(Path(p["watchdog"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                self.no_improve_streak = int(data.get("no_improve_streak", 0))
                self.orbit_streak = int(data.get("orbit_streak", 0))
                self.http_backoff_left = int(data.get("http_backoff_left", 0))
                self.restart_count = int(data.get("restart_count", 0))
                self.last_restart_reason = str(data.get("last_restart_reason", ""))
                self.last_self_heal_cycle = int(data.get("last_self_heal_cycle", 0))
                self.div_collapse_streak = int(data.get("div_collapse_streak", 0))
                self.last_stable_cycle = int(data.get("last_stable_cycle", 0))
                self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = int(data.get("geometry_explorer_disabled_until", 0))
                self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = int(data.get("safe_hyperparam_only_until", 0))
                self.export_suppressed_until = int(data.get("export_suppressed_until", 0))
                asc = data.get("active_swarm_candidate")
                if isinstance(asc, dict):
                    self.active_swarm_candidate = asc
                lsr = data.get("last_swarm_rejection")
                if isinstance(lsr, dict):
                    self.last_swarm_rejection = lsr
                lsa = data.get("last_swarm_acceptance")
                if isinstance(lsa, dict):
                    self.last_swarm_acceptance = lsa
                rs = data.get("runtime_state", {})
                if isinstance(rs, dict):
                    for k, v in rs.items():
                        if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                            self.runtime_state[k] = float(v)
                self.best_val = float(data.get("best_val", self.best_val))
                self.adaptive_best_val = float(data.get("adaptive_best_val", self.adaptive_best_val))
                cp_pool = data.get("champion_pool")
                if isinstance(cp_pool, list):
                    self.champion_pool = cp_pool[: max(1, int(self.cfg.get("champion_pool_size", 5)))]
                self.last_improvement_cycle = int(data.get("last_improvement_cycle", self.last_improvement_cycle))
                self.last_train_interrupt = bool(data.get("last_train_interrupt", self.last_train_interrupt))
                les = data.get("last_export_state", {})
                if isinstance(les, dict):
                    self.last_export_state.update(les)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if os.path.exists(p["homeostasis"]):
            try:
                data = json.loads(Path(p["homeostasis"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
