                self.last_score = data.get("last_score")
                self.last_aux = data.get("last_aux")
                self.best_val = float(data.get("best_val", self.best_val))
                self.adaptive_best_val = float(data.get("adaptive_best_val", self.adaptive_best_val))
                cp_pool = data.get("champion_pool")
                if isinstance(cp_pool, list):
                    self.champion_pool = cp_pool[: max(1, int(self.cfg.get("champion_pool_size", 5)))]
                am = data.get("artmoney_state", {})
                if isinstance(am, dict):
                    self.artmoney_state.update(am)
                if "search_mode" in data:
                    self.artmoney_state["search_mode"] = str(data["search_mode"])
                if isinstance(data.get("best_pool"), list):
                    self.artmoney_state["best_pool"] = data["best_pool"]
                if isinstance(data.get("candidate_memory"), list):
                    self.artmoney_state["candidate_memory"] = data["candidate_memory"]
                if isinstance(data.get("failed_recent"), list):
                    self.artmoney_state["failed_recent"] = data["failed_recent"]
                if isinstance(data.get("last_good_export"), dict):
                    self.artmoney_state["last_good_export"] = data["last_good_export"]
                if isinstance(data.get("last_ecosystem_event"), dict):
                    self.last_ecosystem_event = data["last_ecosystem_event"]
                brs = data.get("best_runtime_state")
                if isinstance(brs, dict):
                    self.best_runtime_state = brs
                self.best_runtime_loaded_from = str(data.get("best_runtime_loaded_from", self.best_runtime_loaded_from))
                asc = data.get("active_swarm_candidate")
                if isinstance(asc, dict):
                    self.active_swarm_candidate = asc
                lsr = data.get("last_swarm_rejection")
                if isinstance(lsr, dict):
                    self.last_swarm_rejection = lsr
                lsa = data.get("last_swarm_acceptance")
                if isinstance(lsa, dict):
                    self.last_swarm_acceptance = lsa
                self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = int(data.get("geometry_explorer_disabled_until", self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until))
                self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = int(data.get("safe_hyperparam_only_until", self.safe_hyperparam_only_until))
                self.export_suppressed_until = int(data.get("export_suppressed_until", self.export_suppressed_until))
            except Exception:
                pass
        if os.path.exists(p["ecosystem"]):
            try:
                data = json.loads(Path(p["ecosystem"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                if isinstance(data.get("last_ecosystem_event"), dict):
                    self.last_ecosystem_event = data["last_ecosystem_event"]
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()

    def save_checkpoint(self, cycle: int, stable: bool = True, reason: str = "stable"):
        stem = "checkpoint" if stable else "checkpoint_recovery"
        path = os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], f"{stem}_{cycle}.pt")
        torch.save({
            "cycle": cycle,
            "model_state": self.model.state_dict(),
            "optimizer_state": self.optimizer.state_dict(),
            "best_val": self.best_val,
            "runtime_state": self.runtime_state,
            "ema_val": self.ema.value,
            "model_config": self.current_model_signature(),
            "artmoney_state": self.artmoney_state,
            "last_export_state": self.last_export_state,
            "last_ecosystem_event": self.last_ecosystem_event,
        }, path)
        if stable:
            self.logger.info("Checkpoint saved: %s", path)
        else:
            self.logger.warning("Checkpoint saved in quarantine lane | reason=%s | path=%s", reason, path)

        cps = []
        bad_cps = []
        for name in os.listdir(self.cfg["save_dir"]):
            m = re.match(r"checkpoint_(\d+)\.pt$", name)
            if m:
                cps.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], name)))
                continue
            m = re.match(r"checkpoint_recovery_(\d+)\.pt$", name)
            if m:
                bad_cps.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], name)))
        cps.sort(reverse=True)
        for _, p in cps[self.cfg["keep_last_checkpoints"]:]:
            try:
                os.remove(p)
            except Exception:
                pass
        bad_cps.sort(reverse=True)
        for _, p in bad_cps[self.cfg.get("keep_last_bad_checkpoints", 4):]:
            try:
                os.remove(p)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def auto_resume(self):
        self.load_memories()
        latest = find_latest_checkpoint(self.cfg["save_dir"]) if self.cfg["auto_resume_latest"] else None
        if not latest:
            return
        cp = torch.load(latest, map_location=self.device)
        source_signature = cp.get("model_config", {})
        self._safe_load_model_state(self.model, cp["model_state"], "AUTO-RESUME LOAD", source_signature=source_signature)
        try:
            self.optimizer.load_state_dict(cp["optimizer_state"])
        except Exception as exc:
            self.logger.warning("AUTO-RESUME optimizer load skipped: %s", exc)
            self.optimizer = optim.AdamW(self.model.parameters(), lr=self.cfg["lr"], weight_decay=self.cfg["weight_decay"])
        self.best_val = float(cp.get("best_val", self.best_val))
        self.ema.value = cp.get("ema_val", None)
        rs = cp.get("runtime_state", {})
        if isinstance(rs, dict):
            for k, v in rs.items():
                if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                    self.runtime_state[k] = float(v)
        am = cp.get("artmoney_state", {})
        if isinstance(am, dict):
            self.artmoney_state.update(am)
        les = cp.get("last_export_state", {})
        if isinstance(les, dict):
            self.last_export_state.update(les)
        lee = cp.get("last_ecosystem_event", {})
        if isinstance(lee, dict):
            self.last_ecosystem_event = lee
        best_runtime_path = self.best_runtime_path()
        if os.path.exists(best_runtime_path):
            self._load_best_runtime_snapshot(best_runtime_path, "auto_resume")
        self._ensure_best_runtime_anchor("auto_resume")
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        self._apply_runtime_dropout(self.model, self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))
        self.start_cycle = int(cp.get("cycle", 0)) + 1
        if self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until < self.start_cycle:
            self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = 0
        if self.safe_hyperparam_only_until < self.start_cycle:
            self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = 0
        if self.export_suppressed_until < self.start_cycle:
            self.export_suppressed_until = 0
        if isinstance(self.active_swarm_candidate, dict):
            watch_until = int(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("watch_until", 0) or 0)
            if watch_until < self.start_cycle:
                self.active_swarm_candidate = None
        self._maybe_restore_best_runtime_on_resume()
        self.logger.info("Auto-resumed from %s", latest)


    def _main_model_regressed(self) -> bool:
        if self.best_val in (None, float("inf")):
            return False
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until):
            return True
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.export_suppressed_until):
            return True
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until):
            return True
        if isinstance(self.active_swarm_candidate, dict) and int(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("regression_hits", 0) or 0) > 0:
            return True
        current_est = None
        if self.ema.value is not None:
            current_est = float(self.ema.value)
        elif self.training_loss_ema is not None:
            current_est = float(self.training_loss_ema)
        if current_est is None:
            return False
        ratio = float(self.cfg.get("swarm_worker_source_switch_val_ratio", 1.10))
        return current_est > float(self.best_val) * ratio

    def _maybe_restore_best_runtime_on_resume(self) -> None:
        if not self._ensure_best_runtime_anchor("auto_resume_postcheck"):
            return
        try:
            eval_batches = int(self.cfg.get("auto_resume_postcheck_batches", 6))
            current_val, _, _ = self.evaluate(eval_batches)
            threshold = float(self.best_val) * float(self.cfg.get("auto_resume_prefer_best_runtime_ratio", 1.18))
            if current_val > threshold:
                restored = self._restore_best_runtime()
                if restored:
                    self.logger.warning(
                        "AUTO-RESUME POSTCHECK -> RESTORE BEST_RUNTIME | current=%.4f | best=%.4f | threshold=%.4f",
                        current_val,
                        float(self.best_val),
                        threshold,
                    )
        except Exception as exc:
            self.logger.warning("AUTO-RESUME POSTCHECK skipped: %s", exc)


    # ---------- corpus ----------
    def load_real_corpus(self):
        corpus, meta = load_code_corpus(self.cfg["code_root"], self.cfg["file_extensions"], self.cfg["max_files"], self.cfg["max_file_bytes"])
        if len(corpus) < self.cfg["min_corpus_bytes"]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Corpus too small: {len(corpus)} bytes from {meta}")
        split = int(len(corpus) * (1.0 - self.cfg["val_ratio"]))
        split = max(self.cfg["block_size"] + 2, split)
        train_bytes = corpus[:split]
        val_bytes = corpus[split:]
        if len(val_bytes) < self.cfg["block_size"] + 2:
            val_bytes = corpus[max(0, len(corpus) - max(self.cfg["block_size"] * 8, 4096)):]
        self.train_data = torch.tensor(list(train_bytes), dtype=torch.long)
        self.val_data = torch.tensor(list(val_bytes), dtype=torch.long)
        self.corpus_meta = {**meta, "train_bytes": int(len(train_bytes)), "val_bytes": int(len(val_bytes))}
        self.logger.info("Corpus loaded: files=%s | bytes=%s | train=%s | val=%s", meta["files"], meta["bytes"], len(train_bytes), len(val_bytes))

    def refresh_world_memory(self, cycle: int):
        live_files = iter_live_files(self.cfg["live_paths"], self.cfg["live_recursive"], self.cfg["file_extensions"])
        texts = []
        for path in live_files[:200]:
            txt = read_textish_file(path, max_bytes=120_000)
            if txt:
                texts.append(txt)

        # external bridge snapshots
        for p in self.cfg.get("external_bridge_paths", []):
            txt = read_textish_file(p, max_bytes=120_000)
            if txt:
                texts.append(txt)

        added_live = self.world_memory.add_many(texts[:256], score=0.15, source="live_files")
        added_online = self.world_memory.add_many(self.online_bridge.fetch_texts()[:96], score=0.20, source="online_api") if self.cfg.get("use_online_telemetry", True) else 0
        added_proc = self.world_memory.add_many(
            self.procedural.generate_texts(max(self.cfg["procedural_min_sequences"], self.cfg["procedural_recipes_per_cycle"])),
            score=0.12, source="procedural"
        ) if self.cfg.get("use_procedural_core", True) else 0

        self.logger.info("World refresh @cycle %s | live=%s online=%s procedural=%s total=%s",
                         cycle, added_live, added_online, added_proc, len(self.world_memory.records))

    # ---------- batches ----------
    def _get_batch(self, split: str, batch_size: Optional[int] = None):
        bs = batch_size or self.cfg["batch_size"]
        data = self.train_data if split == "train" else self.val_data
        ix = torch.randint(len(data) - self.cfg["block_size"] - 1, (bs,))
        x = torch.stack([data[i:i+self.cfg["block_size"]] for i in ix]).to(self.device)
        y = torch.stack([data[i+1:i+1+self.cfg["block_size"]] for i in ix]).to(self.device)
        return x, y

    def _text_to_windows(self, text: str) -> List[List[int]]:
        raw = text.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
        if len(raw) < self.cfg["block_size"] + 2:
            return []
        windows = []
        stride = max(8, self.cfg["block_size"] // 3)
        for start in range(0, len(raw) - self.cfg["block_size"] - 1, stride):
            chunk = raw[start:start + self.cfg["block_size"] + 1]
            if len(chunk) == self.cfg["block_size"] + 1:
                windows.append(list(chunk))
        return windows

    def _get_world_batch(self, batch_size: int):
        texts = self.world_memory.sample_texts(max(1, int(batch_size * self.cfg["world_memory_mix_ratio"])))
        if not texts:
            return None
        windows = []
        for t in texts:
            windows.extend(self._text_to_windows(t))
        if not windows:
            return None
        xs, ys = [], []
        for _ in range(min(batch_size, len(windows))):
            w = random.choice(windows)
            xs.append(w[:-1])
            ys.append(w[1:])
        return (
            torch.tensor(np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.int64), device=self.device),
            torch.tensor(np.asarray(ys, dtype=np.int64), device=self.device),
        )

    # ---------- best state / mutation ----------
    def _capture_best_runtime(self):
        self.best_state_dict = {k: v.detach().cpu().clone() for k, v in self.model.state_dict().items()}
        self.best_optimizer_state = self.optimizer.state_dict()
        self.best_state_cfg = self.current_model_signature()
        self.best_runtime_state = dict(self.runtime_state)
        self.best_runtime_missing_alerted = False
        self.best_runtime_loaded_from = f"capture:cycle_{self.current_cycle}"
        self._capture_champion_pool_entry(self.current_cycle, None, tag="best_runtime")
        self._persist_best_runtime_snapshot()

    def _restore_best_runtime(self) -> bool:
        if not self._ensure_best_runtime_anchor("restore"):
            return False
        entry = self._select_champion_restore_entry("restore")
        state_dict = self.best_state_dict
        state_cfg = self.best_state_cfg or self.current_model_signature()
        runtime_candidate = self.best_runtime_state
        optimizer_candidate = self.best_optimizer_state
        if isinstance(entry, dict):
            state_dict = entry.get("state_dict", state_dict)
            state_cfg = entry.get("state_cfg", state_cfg)
            runtime_candidate = entry.get("runtime_state", runtime_candidate)
            optimizer_candidate = entry.get("optimizer_state", optimizer_candidate)
        self._safe_load_model_state(self.model, state_dict, "BEST-RUNTIME RESTORE", source_signature=state_cfg or self.current_model_signature())
        if isinstance(runtime_candidate, dict):
            for k, v in runtime_candidate.items():
                if isinstance(v, (int, float)):
                    self.runtime_state[k] = float(v)
        self.optimizer = optim.AdamW(self.model.parameters(), lr=self.cfg["lr"], weight_decay=self.cfg["weight_decay"])
        if optimizer_candidate is not None:
            try:
                self.optimizer.load_state_dict(optimizer_candidate)
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._apply_runtime_dropout(self.model, self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))
        self.last_stable_cycle = max(int(self.last_stable_cycle), int(self.current_cycle))
        return True

    def _break_loop_stall(self, cycle: int, reason: str) -> bool:
        if not self._restore_best_runtime():
            return False
        if isinstance(self.best_runtime_state, dict):
            for key in ("lr_scale", "entropy_weight", "teacher_forcing", "dropout", "langer_scale", "reflex_gain", "tachyon_gain"):
                value = self.best_runtime_state.get(key)
                if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                    self.runtime_state[key] = float(value)
        self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.55, float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * 0.92)
        self.artmoney_state["search_mode"] = "WIDE_SCAN"
        self.artmoney_state["improve_streak"] = 0
        self.artmoney_state["degrade_streak"] = 0
        self.artmoney_state["anomaly_success_streak"] = 0
        self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"] = 0
        self.artmoney_state["last_transition"] = utc_now_z()
        self.active_swarm_candidate = None
        self.no_improve_streak = 0
        self.orbit_streak = 0
        self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = max(
            int(self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until),
            int(cycle) + int(self.cfg.get("stall_loop_safe_hyperparam_cycles", 12)),
        )
        self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = max(
            int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until),
            int(cycle) + int(self.cfg.get("stall_loop_safe_hyperparam_cycles", 12)),
        )
        self.export_suppressed_until = max(
            int(self.export_suppressed_until),
            int(cycle) + int(self.cfg.get("stall_loop_export_cooldown_cycles", 4)),
        )
        self.logger.warning(
            "STALL LOOP BREAK | cycle=%s | reason=%s | search=WIDE_SCAN | safe_hyperparam_only_until=%s",
            cycle,
            reason,
            self.safe_hyperparam_only_until,
        )
        return True

    def inject_noise(self, scale: float):
        with torch.no_grad():
            for p in self.model.parameters():
                if p.requires_grad:
                    p.add_(torch.randn_like(p) * scale)

    def procedural_mutation(self, seed: int, strength: float):
        torch.manual_seed(int(seed))
        random.seed(int(seed))
        np.random.seed(int(seed) % (2**32 - 1))
        with torch.no_grad():
            for p in self.model.parameters():
                if p.requires_grad:
                    noise = torch.randn_like(p)
                    p.mul_(1.0 - strength).add_(noise * strength)

    def partial_reset(self, fraction: float):
        with torch.no_grad():
            for p in self.model.parameters():
                if p.requires_grad:
                    mask = torch.rand_like(p) < fraction
                    fresh = torch.randn_like(p) * 0.02
                    p[mask] = fresh[mask]

    def hard_reset(self, fraction: float):
        self.partial_reset(fraction)
        self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.5, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.9)

    def adapt_from_failures(self):
        recent = self.failures.recent(5)
        if not recent:
            return
        avg_div = float(np.mean([r["metrics"].get("divergence", 0.0) for r in recent]))
        avg_pred = float(np.mean([r["metrics"].get("prediction_error", 0.0) for r in recent]))
        avg_train = float(np.mean([r["metrics"].get("train_loss", 0.0) for r in recent]))
        if avg_div > 0.02:
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.45, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.88)
        if avg_pred > 0.01:
            self.runtime_state["entropy_weight"] = min(self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"], self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0) + 0.05)
        if avg_train < self.cfg["overfit_train_threshold"]:
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.40, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.92)

    def register_failure(self, reason: str, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], train_loss: float, val_smooth: float):
        self.failures.add(reason, cycle, {
            "train_loss": float(train_loss),
            "val_smooth": float(val_smooth),
            "divergence": float(diagnostics.get("divergence", 0.0)),
            "prediction_error": float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0)),
            "action": str(diagnostics.get("action", "")),
        }, dict(self.runtime_state))
        self.adapt_from_failures()

    # ---------- stats / anomaly ----------
    def anomaly_gate(self, key: str, current_value: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        vals = self.metric_memory.values(key, self.cfg["anomaly_window"])
        is_anom, z_score, p_value, d = self.evomem.detect_anomaly(vals, current_value)
        return {
            "is_anomaly": bool(is_anom),
            "z_score": float(z_score),
            "p_value": float(p_value),
            "effect_size": float(d),
        }

    # ---------- swarm ----------
    def evaluate_state_on_val(self, model: nn.Module, eval_batches: int) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        model.eval()
        vals = []
        divs = []
        mis = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for _ in range(eval_batches):
                x, y = self._get_batch("val")
                logits = model(x)
                loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.reshape(-1, self.cfg["vocab_size"]), y.reshape(-1)).item()
                vals.append(float(loss))

                probs = torch.softmax(logits[:, -1, :], dim=-1)
                samples = torch.multinomial(probs, num_samples=1).squeeze(-1)
                div = float(len(torch.unique(samples)) / max(1, samples.numel()))
                divs.append(div)

                hist = torch.bincount(samples, minlength=self.cfg["vocab_size"]).float()
                p = hist / max(hist.sum(), 1.0)
                mi = float((-p[p > 0] * torch.log(p[p > 0] + 1e-12)).sum().item())
                mis.append(mi)

        avg_vl = float(np.mean(vals))
        avg_div = float(np.mean(divs))
        avg_mi = float(np.mean(mis))
        return avg_vl, avg_div, avg_mi

    def _sample_artmoney_runtime(self, branch: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        mode = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN")
        winners = self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", [])[: int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_top_winners", 4))]
        protective_runtime = self.artmoney_state.get("protective_runtime", {})
        out: Dict[str, float] = {}

        for key, (base_lo, base_hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            lo, hi = self._get_artmoney_range(key)
            current = float(self.runtime_state.get(key, (lo + hi) * 0.5))
            center = self._blend_with_physarum_hint(key, current, winners)
            if isinstance(protective_runtime, dict) and isinstance(protective_runtime.get(key), (int, float)):
                center = 0.82 * center + 0.18 * float(protective_runtime.get(key))
            span = hi - lo
            if mode == "WIDE_SCAN":
                target = random.uniform(lo, hi)
            elif mode == "NARROWING":
                target = center + random.uniform(-0.30 * span, 0.30 * span)
            elif mode == "LOCKED_HUNT":
                target = center + random.uniform(-0.14 * span, 0.14 * span)
            else:
                locked = float(self.artmoney_state["ranges"].get(key, {}).get("locked_center", center))
                anchor = 0.65 * locked + 0.35 * center
                target = anchor + random.uniform(-0.35 * span, 0.35 * span)

            if branch == "hyperparam_only" and mode in {"NARROWING", "LOCKED_HUNT"}:
                target = 0.65 * current + 0.35 * target
            target = self._apply_runtime_directional_bias(key, target, lo, hi)
            out[key] = clip_float(float(target), lo, hi)
        return out

    def _capture_holdout_batches(self, eval_batches: int) -> List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]:
        batches = []
        for _ in range(max(1, int(eval_batches))):
            x, y = self._get_batch("val")
            batches.append((x.detach().clone(), y.detach().clone()))
        return batches

    @torch.no_grad()
    def _evaluate_model_on_holdout(self, model: nn.Module, batches: List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        model.eval()
        vals, divs, mis = [], [], []
        for x, y in batches:
            logits = model(x)
            vals.append(float(F.cross_entropy(logits.reshape(-1, self.cfg["vocab_size"]), y.reshape(-1)).item()))
            probs = torch.softmax(logits[:, -1, :], dim=-1)
            samples = torch.multinomial(probs, num_samples=1).squeeze(-1)
            divs.append(float(len(torch.unique(samples)) / max(1, samples.numel())))
            hist = torch.bincount(samples, minlength=self.cfg["vocab_size"]).float()
            p = hist / max(hist.sum(), 1.0)
            mis.append(float((-p[p > 0] * torch.log(p[p > 0] + 1e-12)).sum().item()))
        return {
            "val_loss": float(np.mean(vals)) if vals else float("inf"),
            "div": float(np.mean(divs)) if divs else 0.0,
            "mi": float(np.mean(mis)) if mis else 0.0,
        }

    def _evaluate_projected_state_on_holdout(
        self,
        state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor],
        source_signature: Dict[str, Any],
        batches: List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]],
        runtime_candidate: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        local_cfg = dict(self.cfg)
        for key in ("n_embd", "n_head", "n_layer", "block_size", "vocab_size"):
            if key in self.cfg:
                local_cfg[key] = self.cfg[key]
        model = ByteTransformer(local_cfg).to(self.device)
        if isinstance(runtime_candidate, dict):
            self._apply_runtime_dropout(model, runtime_candidate.get("dropout", local_cfg["dropout"]))
        info = compatible_state_load(model, state_dict, current_model_signature(local_cfg), source_signature=source_signature)
        if info["matched"] <= 0 or info["policy"] == "fresh init due to geometry mismatch":
            del model
            if torch.cuda.is_available():
                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            return None
        metrics = self._evaluate_model_on_holdout(model, batches)
        metrics.update({
            "load_policy": info["policy"],
            "matched": int(info["matched"]),
            "total": int(info["total"]),
            "geometry_mismatch": bool(info["geometry_mismatch"]),
        })
        del model
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        return metrics

    def _best_runtime_holdout_eval(self, batches: List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self._ensure_best_runtime_anchor("holdout_eval"):
            return None
        return self._evaluate_projected_state_on_holdout(
            self.best_state_dict,
            self.best_state_cfg or self.current_model_signature(),
            batches,
            runtime_candidate=self.best_runtime_state if isinstance(self.best_runtime_state, dict) else self.runtime_state,
        )

    @torch.no_grad()
    def _evaluate_output_entropy_on_holdout(self, model: nn.Module, batches: List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]]) -> float:
        model.eval()
        ents = []
        for x, _ in batches[: max(1, min(2, len(batches)))]:
            logits = model(x)
            probs = torch.softmax(logits[:, -1, :], dim=-1)
            ent = -(probs * torch.log(probs + 1e-12)).sum(dim=-1).mean()
            ents.append(float(ent.item()))
        return float(np.mean(ents)) if ents else 0.0

    def _projected_output_entropy_on_holdout(
        self,
        state_dict: Dict[str, torch.Tensor],
        source_signature: Dict[str, Any],
        batches: List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]],
        runtime_candidate: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
    ) -> Optional[float]:
        local_cfg = dict(self.cfg)
        for key in ("n_embd", "n_head", "n_layer", "block_size", "vocab_size"):
            if key in self.cfg:
                local_cfg[key] = self.cfg[key]
        model = ByteTransformer(local_cfg).to(self.device)
        if isinstance(runtime_candidate, dict):
            self._apply_runtime_dropout(model, runtime_candidate.get("dropout", local_cfg["dropout"]))
        info = compatible_state_load(model, state_dict, current_model_signature(local_cfg), source_signature=source_signature)
        if info["matched"] <= 0 or info["policy"] == "fresh init due to geometry mismatch":
            del model
            if torch.cuda.is_available():
                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            return None
        out = self._evaluate_output_entropy_on_holdout(model, batches)
        del model
        if torch.cuda.is_available():
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        return out

    def _mixing_free_energy(
        self,
        state_current: Dict[str, torch.Tensor],
        state_candidate: Dict[str, torch.Tensor],
        batches: List[Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]],
        source_signature: Dict[str, Any],
        runtime_candidate: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None,
        temperature: float = 1.0,
    ) -> float:
        enthalpy = 0.0
        count = 0
        key_subset = ["wte.weight", "lm_head.weight", "ln_f.weight"]
        for key in key_subset:
            if key in state_current and key in state_candidate:
                v1 = state_current[key].detach().float().flatten().cpu()
                v2 = state_candidate[key].detach().float().flatten().cpu()
                if v1.numel() == v2.numel() and v1.numel() > 0:
                    cos_sim = torch.dot(v1, v2) / (torch.norm(v1) * torch.norm(v2) + 1e-8)
                    enthalpy += float(1.0 - float(cos_sim.item()))
                    count += 1
        if count == 0:
            return 0.0
        enthalpy /= count

        current_entropy = self._evaluate_output_entropy_on_holdout(self.model, batches)
        candidate_entropy = self._projected_output_entropy_on_holdout(
            state_candidate,
            source_signature,
            batches,
            runtime_candidate=runtime_candidate,
        )
        if candidate_entropy is None:
            candidate_entropy = current_entropy
        delta_s_mix = abs(float(candidate_entropy) - float(current_entropy))
        free_energy = float(
            enthalpy
            + max(0.1, float(temperature)) * float(self.cfg.get("mixing_entropy_scale", 0.10)) * delta_s_mix
        )
        return free_energy


    def _config_fingerprint(self, config: Dict[str, Any], runtime: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> str:
        runtime = runtime or {}
        keys = [
            ("n_embd", int(config.get("n_embd", self.cfg["n_embd"]))),
            ("n_head", int(config.get("n_head", self.cfg["n_head"]))),
            ("n_layer", int(config.get("n_layer", self.cfg["n_layer"]))),
            ("lr_scale", round(float(runtime.get("lr_scale", runtime.get("lr", 1.0))), 3)),
            ("dropout", round(float(runtime.get("dropout", self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))), 3)),
            ("entropy_weight", round(float(runtime.get("entropy_weight", self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))), 3)),
            ("teacher_forcing", round(float(runtime.get("teacher_forcing", self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]))), 3)),
            ("langer_scale", round(float(runtime.get("langer_scale", self.runtime_state.get("langer_scale", self.cfg["langer_scale"]))), 4)),
            ("reflex_gain", round(float(runtime.get("reflex_gain", self.runtime_state.get("reflex_gain", self.cfg["reflex_gain"]))), 3)),
            ("tachyon_gain", round(float(runtime.get("tachyon_gain", self.runtime_state.get("tachyon_gain", self.cfg["tachyon_gain"]))), 3)),
        ]
        blob = "|".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in keys)
        return hashlib.sha1(blob.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]

    def _dedupe_runtime_records(self, items: List[Any], keep: int, prefer: str = "score") -> List[Any]:
        best: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        order: List[str] = []
        for item in items:
            if isinstance(item, str):
                sig = str(item)
                if sig not in best:
                    best[sig] = sig
                    order.append(sig)
                continue
            if not isinstance(item, dict):
                continue
            sig = str(item.get("signature") or self._config_fingerprint(item, item))
            item["signature"] = sig
            prev = best.get(sig)
            if prev is None:
                best[sig] = item
                order.append(sig)
            else:
                lhs = float(item.get(prefer, item.get("val_raw", -float("inf"))))
                rhs = float(prev.get(prefer, prev.get("val_raw", -float("inf"))))
                if lhs > rhs:
                    best[sig] = item
        out = [best[sig] for sig in order if sig in best]
        if out and isinstance(out[0], dict):
            out = sorted(out, key=lambda x: float(x.get(prefer, -float("inf"))), reverse=True)
        return out[: max(1, int(keep))]

    def _compute_deploy_trust(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], val_smooth: float) -> float:
        divergence = float(diagnostics.get("divergence", 0.0))
        coherence = float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0))
        pred_err = float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0))
        battery = float(diagnostics.get("battery_charge", 1.0))
        best_anchor = max(1e-8, float(self._effective_best_anchor()))
        val_ratio = clip_float(float(val_smooth) / best_anchor, 0.8, 1.3)
        val_score = clip_float(1.15 - val_ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
        trust = clip_float((1.0 - divergence) * coherence * max(0.0, 1.0 - pred_err) * clip_float(battery, 0.5, 1.2) * max(0.4, val_score), 0.0, 1.0)
        return float(trust)

    def _is_plateau(self) -> bool:
        vals = self.metric_memory.values("val_smooth", int(self.cfg.get("plateau_window", 8)))
        if len(vals) < max(4, int(self.cfg.get("plateau_window", 8)) // 2):
            return False
        spread = max(vals) - min(vals)
        return bool(spread <= float(self.cfg.get("plateau_trigger", 0.012)))

    def _append_failed_signature(self, signature: str) -> None:
        fr = list(self.artmoney_state.get("failed_recent", []))
        fr.append(str(signature))
        self.artmoney_state["failed_recent"] = self._dedupe_runtime_records(
            fr,
            int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_failed_recent_keep", 96)),
            prefer="score",
        )

    def _apply_swarm_runtime_candidate(self, candidate: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        runtime_candidate = candidate.get("runtime_candidate", {})
        if not isinstance(runtime_candidate, dict):
            return
        blend = 0.85 if str(candidate.get("branch", "hyperparam_only")) == "hyperparam_only" else 0.65
        if str(candidate.get("quarantine", {}).get("projected_load_policy", "")) == "partial compatible load":
            blend = min(blend, 0.55)
        for key, (lo, hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            if not isinstance(runtime_candidate.get(key), (int, float)):
                continue
            current = float(self.runtime_state.get(key, runtime_candidate[key]))
            target = (1.0 - blend) * current + blend * float(runtime_candidate[key])
            self.runtime_state[key] = clip_float(target, lo, hi)
        self.runtime_state["dropout"] = self._apply_runtime_dropout(self.model, self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))

    def _reset_search_ranges(self, anchored: bool = True) -> None:
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        winners = self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", [])[: max(1, min(int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_top_winners", 4)), len(self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", []))))]
        for key, (base_lo, base_hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            if anchored and winners:
                vals = [float(w.get(key)) for w in winners if isinstance(w.get(key), (int, float))]
                if vals:
                    center = float(np.mean(vals))
                    spread = max(float(np.std(vals)) * 2.0, (max(vals) - min(vals)) * 0.90)
                    span = max((base_hi - base_lo) * 0.18, spread)
                    self._set_artmoney_range(key, center - span * 0.5, center + span * 0.5, center=center)
                    continue
            self._set_artmoney_range(key, base_lo, base_hi, center=(base_lo + base_hi) * 0.5)

    def _enter_safe_recovery(self, cycle: int, reason: str) -> bool:
        restored = self._restore_best_runtime()
        if restored:
            self.logger.warning("ROLLBACK TO BEST_RUNTIME | cycle=%s | reason=%s", cycle, reason)
        self._reset_search_ranges(anchored=True)
        self.artmoney_state["search_mode"] = "NARROWING"
        self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"] = 0
        self.artmoney_state["last_transition"] = utc_now_z()

        # v14.3a: любые safe-блокировки не дольше 12 циклов
        max_geometry = min(12, int(self.cfg.get("geometry_explorer_disable_cycles_after_regression", 8)))
        max_safe = min(12, int(self.cfg.get("safe_hyperparam_only_cycles_after_regression", 8)))
        max_export = min(12, int(self.cfg.get("export_suppress_cycles_after_regression", 6)))

        self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until = max(
            self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until,
            int(cycle) + max_geometry,
        )
        self.safe_hyperparam_only_until = max(
            self.safe_hyperparam_only_until,
            int(cycle) + max_safe,
        )
        self.export_suppressed_until = max(
            self.export_suppressed_until,
            int(cycle) + max_export,
        )

        # v14.1: после recovery поднимаем adaptive-якорь, чтобы old best не душил выход.
        if math.isfinite(float(self.best_val)):
            if not math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)):
                self.adaptive_best_val = float(self.best_val) * 1.05
            else:
                self.adaptive_best_val = max(float(self.adaptive_best_val), float(self.best_val) * 1.05)

        self.logger.warning(
            "GEOMETRY EXPLORER TEMPORARILY DISABLED | until_cycle=%s | safe_hyperparam_only_until=%s | reason=%s",
            self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until,
            self.safe_hyperparam_only_until,
            reason,
        )
        return restored

    def _quarantine_swarm_candidate(self, cycle: int, candidate: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        batches = self._capture_holdout_batches(int(self.cfg.get("swarm_quarantine_eval_batches", 8)))
        source_signature = {
            "n_embd": int(candidate["config"].get("n_embd", self.cfg["n_embd"])),
            "n_head": int(candidate["config"].get("n_head", self.cfg["n_head"])),
            "n_layer": int(candidate["config"].get("n_layer", self.cfg["n_layer"])),
            "block_size": int(self.cfg["block_size"]),
            "vocab_size": int(self.cfg["vocab_size"]),
        }
        current_eval = self._evaluate_model_on_holdout(self.model, batches)
        projected_eval = self._evaluate_projected_state_on_holdout(
            candidate["best_state"],
            source_signature,
            batches,
            runtime_candidate=candidate.get("runtime_candidate"),
        )
        best_eval = self._best_runtime_holdout_eval(batches)
        branch = str(candidate.get("branch", "hyperparam_only"))
        candidate_signature = self.evomem._config_signature(candidate.get("config", {}))
        if candidate_signature in self.mycelium_toxic_signatures:
            self.last_swarm_rejection = {
                "cycle": int(cycle),
                "accepted": False,
                "branch": str(candidate.get("branch", "hyperparam_only")),
                "candidate_signature": candidate_signature,
                "reason": "mycelium_toxic_signature",
            }
            self.logger.warning("SWARM REJECTED | cycle=%s | reason=mycelium_toxic_signature | branch=%s", cycle, candidate.get("branch", "hyperparam_only"))
            return False

        margin_main = float(self.cfg.get("swarm_accept_margin", 0.008))
        if self._is_plateau() and self.no_improve_streak >= 4:
            margin_main = max(margin_main, float(self.cfg.get("plateau_margin_relax", 0.012)))
        margin_best = float(self.cfg.get("swarm_accept_margin_vs_best", 0.004))
        reasons: List[str] = []
        serious_reject = False
        best_runtime_val = float(best_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))) if best_eval else float(self.best_val)

        if projected_eval is None:
            reasons.append("projected_candidate_unusable")
            serious_reject = True
        else:
            projected_policy = str(projected_eval.get("load_policy", "fresh init"))

            topo_similarity = self._candidate_topological_similarity(candidate.get("best_state", {}))
            if topo_similarity < float(self.cfg.get("topological_similarity_threshold", 0.85)):
                reasons.append("topological_gap")
                serious_reject = True

            if float(self.xi) >= float(self.cfg.get("mycelium_isolation_ratio_threshold", 0.55)):
                sandbox_ok, sandbox_meta = self._symbiotic_sandbox_check(candidate, branch=branch)
                candidate["sandbox_meta"] = sandbox_meta
                if not sandbox_ok:
                    reasons.append(str(sandbox_meta.get("reason", "sandbox_reject")))
                    serious_reject = True

            temperature = max(0.1, float(getattr(self, "last_cycle_jitter", 0.001)) * 1000.0)
            free_energy = self._mixing_free_energy(
                self.model.state_dict(),
                candidate.get("best_state", {}),
                batches,
                source_signature,
                runtime_candidate=candidate.get("runtime_candidate"),
                temperature=temperature,
            )
            candidate["mixing_free_energy"] = float(free_energy)
            if free_energy > float(self.cfg.get("mixing_free_energy_threshold", 0.50)):
                reasons.append("high_mixing_free_energy")
                serious_reject = True

            gnm = float(candidate.get("grad_norm_mean", 0.0) or 0.0)
            if gnm < float(self.cfg.get("mycelium_grad_norm_min", 0.01)) or gnm > float(self.cfg.get("mycelium_grad_norm_max", 5.0)):
                reasons.append("unstable_gradients")
                serious_reject = True
            if branch == "geometry_explorer" and projected_policy == "partial compatible load":
                margin_main = max(margin_main, float(self.cfg.get("swarm_accept_margin_geometry_partial", 0.020)))
                margin_best = max(margin_best, float(self.cfg.get("swarm_accept_margin_geometry_partial", 0.020)) * 0.60)
                if float(candidate.get("stability", 0.0)) < float(self.cfg.get("swarm_accept_min_stability", 0.42)):
                    reasons.append("geometry_partial_needs_stability_confirmation")
                    serious_reject = True
            if bool(candidate.get("interrupted")):
                # v14.2: worker mid_interrupt сам по себе не смертелен для hyperparam_only,
                # если кандидат всё ещё лучше main/best по holdout.
                reasons.append("mid_interrupt_worker")
                if branch == "geometry_explorer":
                    serious_reject = True
            if float(candidate.get("alignment", 0.0)) < 0.0:
                reasons.append("negative_alignment")
                serious_reject = True
            if projected_eval["val_loss"] > current_eval["val_loss"] - margin_main:
                reasons.append("fails_margin_vs_main")
            if math.isfinite(best_runtime_val) and projected_eval["val_loss"] > best_runtime_val - margin_best:
                reasons.append("fails_margin_vs_best_runtime")
            if branch == "geometry_explorer" and projected_policy == "partial compatible load":
                if projected_eval.get("mi", 0.0) + 1e-6 < current_eval.get("mi", 0.0) * 0.96:
                    reasons.append("geometry_partial_low_mi")
                    serious_reject = True
            if projected_eval["val_loss"] > current_eval["val_loss"] * float(self.cfg.get("swarm_post_inject_val_ratio", 1.04)):
                serious_reject = True

        accepted = not reasons
        if (not accepted) and reasons == ["mid_interrupt_worker"] and projected_eval:
            if (
                projected_eval["val_loss"] + margin_main < current_eval["val_loss"]
                and (not math.isfinite(best_runtime_val) or projected_eval["val_loss"] + margin_best < best_runtime_val)
            ):
                accepted = True
                decision_reason = "beats_main_and_best_despite_worker_interrupt"
            else:
                decision_reason = None
        else:
            decision_reason = None
        decision = {
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "accepted": bool(accepted),
            "branch": branch,
            "candidate_signature": candidate_signature,
            "reason": (decision_reason if accepted and decision_reason else "beats_main_and_best_on_holdout") if accepted else ",".join(reasons),
            "current_main_val_loss": round(float(current_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))), 6),
            "projected_val_loss": round(float(projected_eval.get("val_loss", float("inf"))) if projected_eval else float("inf"), 6),
            "best_runtime_val_loss": round(best_runtime_val, 6) if math.isfinite(best_runtime_val) else float("inf"),
            "projected_load_policy": projected_eval.get("load_policy") if projected_eval else "unusable",
            "stability": round(float(candidate.get("stability", 0.0)), 6),
            "mixing_free_energy": round(float(candidate.get("mixing_free_energy", 0.0)), 6),
        }
        candidate["quarantine"] = decision

        if accepted:
            self.last_swarm_acceptance = dict(decision)
            self.logger.info(
                "SWARM ACCEPTED | cycle=%s | reason=%s | branch=%s | projected_load=%s | projected=%.4f | main=%.4f | best_runtime=%.4f",
                cycle,
                decision["reason"],
                branch,
                decision["projected_load_policy"],
                decision["projected_val_loss"],
                decision["current_main_val_loss"],
                decision["best_runtime_val_loss"],
            )
            return True

        self.last_swarm_rejection = dict(decision)
        self._append_failed_signature(candidate_signature)
        if serious_reject:
            self.evomem.register_lethal(candidate.get("config", {}))
            self._register_mycelium_toxic_signature(candidate_signature)
        self.logger.warning(
            "SWARM REJECTED | cycle=%s | reason=%s | branch=%s | projected_load=%s | projected=%.4f | main=%.4f | best_runtime=%.4f",
            cycle,
            decision["reason"],
            branch,
            decision["projected_load_policy"],
            decision["projected_val_loss"],
            decision["current_main_val_loss"],
            decision["best_runtime_val_loss"],
        )
        return False


    def _post_inject_warmup(self, steps: int, lr_mul: float) -> int:
        steps = int(max(0, steps))
        if steps <= 0:
            return 0
        original_lr_scale = float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0))
        warm_lr = clip_float(original_lr_scale * float(lr_mul), 0.05, 2.0)
        self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = warm_lr
        completed = 0
        try:
            self.model.train()
            for _ in range(steps):
                xb, yb = self._get_batch("train")
                logits = self.model(xb)
                loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)), yb.view(-1))
                self.optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                loss.backward()
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.model.parameters(), self.cfg["grad_clip"])
                self.optimizer.step()
                completed += 1
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning("POST-INJECT WARMUP PARTIAL | completed=%s | error=%s", completed, e)
        finally:
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = original_lr_scale
        self.logger.info(
            "POST-INJECT WARMUP DONE | steps=%s | warm_lr=%.3f | lr_scale_restored=%.3f",
            completed, warm_lr, original_lr_scale
        )
        return completed


    def _arm_swarm_post_inject_watch(self, cycle: int, candidate: Dict[str, Any], load_info: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        quarantine = candidate.get("quarantine", {})
        self.active_swarm_candidate = {
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "watch_until": int(cycle) + int(self.cfg.get("swarm_post_inject_watch_cycles", 3)),
            "branch": str(candidate.get("branch", "hyperparam_only")),
            "config": dict(candidate.get("config", {})),
            "runtime_candidate": dict(candidate.get("runtime_candidate", {})),
            "load_policy": str(load_info.get("policy", "unknown")),
            "projected_load_policy": str(quarantine.get("projected_load_policy", load_info.get("policy", "unknown"))),
            "projected_val_loss": float(quarantine.get("projected_val_loss", candidate.get("val_loss", float("inf")))),
            "baseline_best_val": float(self.best_val),
            "regression_hits": 0,
            "reason": str(quarantine.get("reason", "")),
        }

        # v14.3: immediate post-inject stability check
        try:
            test_batches = int(self.cfg.get("post_inject_immediate_test_batches", 4))
            degrade_ratio = float(self.cfg.get("post_inject_degradation_ratio", 1.05))

            projected_eval = candidate.get("projected_eval") or {}
            if projected_eval.get("val_loss") is not None:
                test_loss_before = float(projected_eval.get("val_loss"))
            else:
                test_loss_before = float(self.evaluate(test_batches)[0])

            # model already injected into main at this point
            test_loss_after = float(self.evaluate(test_batches)[0])

            ratio = test_loss_after / max(test_loss_before, 1e-8)
            if ratio > degrade_ratio:
                self.logger.warning(
                    "POST-INJECT IMMEDIATE DEGRADATION | cycle=%s | before=%.4f after=%.4f ratio=%.3f -> rollback",
                    cycle, test_loss_before, test_loss_after, ratio
                )
                self._restore_best_runtime()
                self.active_swarm_candidate = None
                self.last_swarm_rejection = {
                    "cycle": cycle,
                    "accepted": False,
                    "reason": "immediate_post_inject_degradation",
                    "branch": candidate.get("branch"),
                    "before": test_loss_before,
                    "after": test_loss_after,
                    "ratio": ratio,
                }
                return
            else:
                self.logger.info(
                    "POST-INJECT STABLE | cycle=%s | before=%.4f after=%.4f ratio=%.3f",
                    cycle, test_loss_before, test_loss_after, ratio
                )
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning("Post-inject test failed: %s", e)

    def _post_inject_regression_check(
        self,
        cycle: int,
        diagnostics: Dict[str, Any],
        val_smooth: float,
        rollback_done: bool,
        self_heal_triggered: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.active_swarm_candidate, dict):
            return
        if int(cycle) > int(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("watch_until", 0)):
            self.logger.info(
                "SWARM CANDIDATE CONFIRMED SAFE | cycle=%s | branch=%s | load=%s",
                cycle,
                self.active_swarm_candidate.get("branch"),
                self.active_swarm_candidate.get("load_policy"),
            )
            self.active_swarm_candidate = None
            return

        reasons = []
        if rollback_done or bool(diagnostics.get("rollback")):
            reasons.append("rollback")
        if diagnostics.get("anomaly_val", {}).get("is_anomaly") and float(val_smooth) > float(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("baseline_best_val", self.best_val)) * 1.003:
            reasons.append("anomaly_val")
        if str(diagnostics.get("action")) == "mid_interrupt":
            reasons.append("mid_interrupt")
        if float(val_smooth) > float(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("baseline_best_val", self.best_val)) * float(self.cfg.get("swarm_post_inject_val_ratio", 1.04)):
            reasons.append("val_explosion")
        if self_heal_triggered:
            reasons.append("self_heal")

        if not reasons:
            return

        self.active_swarm_candidate["regression_hits"] = int(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("regression_hits", 0)) + 1
        self.active_swarm_candidate["last_reasons"] = list(reasons)
        self.logger.warning(
            "SWARM AFTERSHOCK | cycle=%s | hits=%s | reasons=%s",
            cycle,
            self.active_swarm_candidate["regression_hits"],
            ",".join(reasons),
        )
        severe = any(reason in {"rollback", "val_explosion", "self_heal"} for reason in reasons)
        if severe or self.active_swarm_candidate["regression_hits"] >= int(self.cfg.get("swarm_post_inject_confirm_cycles", 2)):
            conf = dict(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("config", {}))
            if conf:
                self.evomem.register_lethal(conf)
                self._register_mycelium_toxic_signature(self.evomem._config_signature(conf))
            self.last_swarm_rejection = {
                "cycle": int(cycle),
                "accepted": False,
                "reason": "post_inject_regression:" + ",".join(reasons),
                "branch": self.active_swarm_candidate.get("branch"),
                "projected_load_policy": self.active_swarm_candidate.get("projected_load_policy"),
            }
            self.logger.warning(
                "SWARM REJECTED | cycle=%s | reason=%s | branch=%s | load=%s",
                cycle,
                self.last_swarm_rejection["reason"],
                self.last_swarm_rejection.get("branch"),
                self.last_swarm_rejection.get("projected_load_policy"),
            )
            self._enter_safe_recovery(cycle, self.last_swarm_rejection["reason"])
            self.active_swarm_candidate = None

    def _checkpoint_state_classification(
        self,
        cycle: int,
        diagnostics: Dict[str, Any],
        val_smooth: float,
        rollback_done: bool,
    ) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        if cycle <= int(self.export_suppressed_until):
            return False, "regression_cooldown"
        if rollback_done or bool(diagnostics.get("rollback")):
            return False, "rollback"
        if str(diagnostics.get("action")) == "mid_interrupt":
            stable_margin = float(self.cfg.get("stable_mid_interrupt_margin", 1.002))
            if (
                not diagnostics.get("anomaly_val", {}).get("is_anomaly")
                and self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") != "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
                and float(val_smooth) <= float(self.best_val) * stable_margin
            ):
                return True, "stable_mid_interrupt"
            return False, "mid_interrupt"
        if diagnostics.get("anomaly_val", {}).get("is_anomaly") and float(val_smooth) > float(self.best_val) * 1.003:
            return False, "anomaly_val"
        divergence = float(diagnostics.get("divergence", 0.0))
        coherence = float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0))
        pred_err = float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0))
        battery = float(diagnostics.get("battery_charge", 1.0))
        best_anchor = max(1e-8, float(self._effective_best_anchor()))
        val_ratio = clip_float(float(val_smooth) / best_anchor, 0.8, 1.3)
        val_score = clip_float(1.15 - val_ratio, 0.0, 1.0)
        deploy_trust = clip_float(
            (1.0 - divergence)
            * coherence
            * max(0.0, 1.0 - pred_err)
            * clip_float(battery, 0.5, 1.2)
            * max(0.4, val_score),
            0.0,
            1.0,
        )
        diagnostics["deploy_trust"] = float(max(float(diagnostics.get("deploy_trust", 0.0)), deploy_trust))
        if int(self.last_self_heal_cycle) > 0 and int(cycle) <= int(self.last_self_heal_cycle) + 1:
            if deploy_trust >= float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_export_threshold", 0.55)) and float(val_smooth) <= float(self._effective_best_anchor()) * float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_val_ratio", 1.05)):
                return True, "post_self_heal_trust"
            return False, "post_self_heal"
        if self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND" and self.no_improve_streak >= 2:
            return False, "recovery_expand"
        return True, "stable"

    def _update_recovery_discipline(self, cycle: int, improved: bool) -> None:
        mode = str(self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"))
        if mode != "RECOVERY_EXPAND":
            self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"] = 0
            return

        diag = getattr(self, "last_cycle_diagnostics", {}) or {}
        mem_ok = bool(diag.get("memory_says_ok", False))
        coherence = float(diag.get("coherence", 0.0))
        best_ratio = float(diag.get("val_smooth", float("inf"))) / max(1e-8, float(self._effective_best_anchor()))

        if improved or (
            mem_ok
            and coherence >= float(self.cfg.get("recovery_to_exploit_coherence", 0.72))
            and best_ratio <= float(self.cfg.get("recovery_to_exploit_best_ratio", 1.015))
        ):
            self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"] = 0
            self.artmoney_state["search_mode"] = "LOCKED_HUNT"
            self.artmoney_state["last_transition"] = utc_now_z()
            self.evomem.mode = 1
            self.logger.info(
                "AUTO TRANSITION | cycle=%s | RECOVERY_EXPAND -> EXPLOIT/LOCKED_HUNT | mem_ok=%s | coherence=%.3f | best_ratio=%.4f",
                cycle, mem_ok, coherence, best_ratio
            )
            return

        self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"] = int(self.artmoney_state.get("recovery_expand_cycles", 0)) + 1
        if self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"] < int(self.cfg.get("recovery_expand_hard_cap", 6)):
            return
        self.logger.warning(
            "RECOVERY_EXPAND HARD CAP | cycle=%s | no_improve=%s | recovery_cycles=%s",
            cycle,
            self.no_improve_streak,
            self.artmoney_state["recovery_expand_cycles"],
        )
        self._enter_safe_recovery(cycle, "recovery_expand_hard_cap")
        self.active_swarm_candidate = None


    def _maybe_force_restore_after_mid_interrupt(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], val_smooth: float) -> bool:
        if str(diagnostics.get("action")) != "mid_interrupt":
            return False
        if not self._ensure_best_runtime_anchor("mid_interrupt_guard"):
            return False

        # v14.2: старт и ранний recovery не душим restore-ом.
        if int(self.current_cycle) <= 18:
            return False
        in_recovery = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
        recovery_cycles = int(self.artmoney_state.get("recovery_expand_cycles", 0))
        if in_recovery and recovery_cycles < 10:
            return False

        effective_best = self._effective_best_anchor()
        current_ref = float(self.ema.value) if self.ema.value is not None else float(val_smooth)

        # если raw/ema уже близко к лучшему якорю, не откатываем
        if math.isfinite(effective_best):
            if float(val_smooth) <= effective_best * 1.035:
                return False
            if current_ref <= effective_best * float(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_force_restore_mid_interrupt_ratio", 1.04)):
                return False

        restored = self._enter_safe_recovery(cycle, "mid_interrupt_guard")
        if restored:
            self.no_improve_streak = 0
            self.orbit_streak = 0
        return restored

    def _train_worker_seed(
        self,
        seed: int,
        config: Dict[str, Any],
        steps: int,
        batch_size: int,
        runtime_candidate: Dict[str, float],
        branch: str,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        random.seed(seed)
        np.random.seed(seed % (2**32 - 1))

        model = ByteTransformer(config).to(self.device)
        local_guard = GradientBlackholeGuard(self.cfg)

        source_state = self.model.state_dict()
        source_signature = self.current_model_signature()
        source_label = "current_main"

        if (
            self.cfg.get("swarm_worker_use_best_runtime_on_regression", True)
            and isinstance(self.best_state_dict, dict)
            and (
                self._main_model_regressed()
                or self.no_improve_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_force_restore_no_improve", 10))
                or self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
                or int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until)
                or int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.export_suppressed_until)
                or int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until)
            )
        ):
            source_state = self.best_state_dict
            source_signature = self.best_state_cfg or self.current_model_signature()
            source_label = "best_runtime"

        load_info = self._safe_load_model_state(
            model,
            source_state,
            f"SWARM LOAD | branch={branch} | seed={seed} | source={source_label}",
            source_signature=source_signature,
        )
        optimizer = optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=float(config["lr"]), weight_decay=self.cfg["weight_decay"])
        worker_runtime = dict(runtime_candidate)
        worker_runtime["dropout"] = self._apply_runtime_dropout(model, worker_runtime.get("dropout", config["dropout"]))

        train_losses = []
        grad_norms = []
        entropy_vals = []
        aborted = False
        interrupted = False
        rewind_count = 0
        rewind_anchor = self._capture_cycle_anchor(model, optimizer)
        model.train()
        start_t = time.time()

        for step_idx in range(steps):
            batch = self._get_world_batch(batch_size) if random.random() < self.cfg["world_memory_mix_ratio"] else None
            x, y = batch if batch is not None else self._get_batch("train", batch_size=batch_size)
            x = self._apply_teacher_forcing_inputs(x, worker_runtime.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]))
            logits = model(x)
            loss, entropy = self._entropy_regularized_loss(logits, y, worker_runtime.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss.backward()

            diag = local_guard.inspect(model)
            diag = local_guard.apply(model, worker_runtime, diag)
            if bool(diag["nonfinite"]) or math.isnan(diag["grad_norm"]) or math.isinf(diag["grad_norm"]) or diag["grad_norm"] > 20.0:
                aborted = True
                break

            grad_norms.append(diag["grad_norm"])
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), self.cfg["grad_clip"])
            optimizer.step()
            train_losses.append(float(loss.item()))
            entropy_vals.append(entropy)

            elapsed_ratio = float(step_idx + 1) / max(1, steps)
            predicted_score = -float(np.mean(train_losses[-6:])) if train_losses else None
            if self._should_interrupt_training_midway(elapsed_ratio, float(loss.item()), train_losses, diag, predicted_score):
                if (
                    rewind_count < int(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_rewind_max", 1))
                    and self._apply_mid_interrupt_rewind(model, optimizer, worker_runtime, rewind_anchor, scope=f"worker:{branch}")
                ):
                    rewind_count += 1
                    trim = min(len(train_losses), int(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_rewind_trim", 6)))
                    if trim > 0:
                        del train_losses[-trim:]
                        del grad_norms[-min(len(grad_norms), trim):]
                        del entropy_vals[-min(len(entropy_vals), trim):]
                    continue
                interrupted = True
                break

        if aborted or not train_losses:
            return {"success": False, "config": config, "seed": seed, "branch": branch, "load_info": load_info, "interrupted": interrupted}

        val_loss, div, mi = self.evaluate_state_on_val(model, self.cfg["swarm_eval_batches"])
        train_loss_avg = float(np.mean(train_losses)) if train_losses else float("inf")
        gap = train_loss_avg - val_loss
        stability = self._composite_stability(train_losses, grad_norms)
        anomaly_bonus = 0.05 if val_loss < (self.best_val - self.cfg.get("artmoney_min_improvement", 0.01)) else 0.0
        worker_pred_error = abs(float(np.mean(entropy_vals)) - float(mi)) if entropy_vals else 0.0
        worker_divergence = max(0.0, float(val_loss) - float(self.best_val if math.isfinite(self.best_val) else val_loss))
        worker_coherence = self.compute_coherence(worker_pred_error, stability, worker_divergence)
        alignment_proxy = 0.0
        if grad_norms:
            grad_dir = float(np.mean(grad_norms))
            coherence_dir = (stability + worker_coherence) - (worker_pred_error + worker_divergence)
            denom = abs(grad_dir) + abs(coherence_dir) + 1e-8
            alignment_proxy = clip_float((grad_dir * coherence_dir) / denom, -1.0, 1.0)
        score = self.compose_score(
            val_loss,
            gap,
            mi,
            div,
            stability,
            anomaly_bonus=anomaly_bonus,
            coherence=worker_coherence,
            alignment=alignment_proxy,
        )

        honest_reward_worker = float(score - 2.0 * (worker_pred_error + worker_divergence))
        if honest_reward_worker < float(self.cfg.get("honesty_kill_threshold", -1.50)):
            self.logger.warning(
                "HONESTY KILL | seed=%s | honest=%.2f | val_loss=%.4f | branch=%s",
                seed, honest_reward_worker, val_loss, branch
            )
            self.evomem.register_lethal(config)
            self._register_mycelium_toxic_signature(self.evomem._config_signature(config))
            return {
                "success": False,
                "config": config,
                "seed": seed,
                "branch": branch,
                "load_info": load_info,
                "interrupted": interrupted,
                "reason": "honesty_kill",
            }

        best_state = {k: v.detach().cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}
        step_time_ms = ((time.time() - start_t) / max(1, len(train_losses))) * 1000.0
        gn_mean = float(np.mean(grad_norms)) if grad_norms else 0.0

        return {
            "success": True,
            "seed": seed,
            "config": config,
            "score": score,
            "val_loss": val_loss,
            "div": div,
            "mi": mi,
            "train_loss": train_loss_avg,
            "gap": gap,
            "stability": stability,
            "entropy_mean": float(np.mean(entropy_vals)) if entropy_vals else 0.0,
            "grad_norm_mean": gn_mean,
            "step_time_ms": step_time_ms,
            "best_state": best_state,
            "load_info": load_info,
            "load_policy": load_info["policy"],
            "runtime_candidate": worker_runtime,
            "branch": branch,
            "interrupted": interrupted,
            "coherence": worker_coherence,
            "alignment": alignment_proxy,
            "prediction_error": worker_pred_error,
            "worker_divergence": worker_divergence,
            "honest_reward_worker": honest_reward_worker,
        }

    def _personality_runtime_adjust(self, runtime_candidate: Dict[str, float], personality: str, plateau: bool, recovery_mode: bool) -> Dict[str, float]:
        rc = dict(runtime_candidate)
        if personality == "red":
            rc["lr_scale"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * (1.10 if not plateau else 1.18), *self.artmoney_bounds()["lr_scale"])
            rc["entropy_weight"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("entropy_weight", self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))) * (1.04 if not plateau else 1.08), *self.artmoney_bounds()["entropy_weight"])
            rc["dropout"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("dropout", self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))) - 0.01, *self.artmoney_bounds()["dropout"])
            rc["teacher_forcing"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("teacher_forcing", self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]))) - 0.01, *self.artmoney_bounds()["teacher_forcing"])
        elif personality == "blue":
            rc["lr_scale"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * (0.92 if not recovery_mode else 0.88), *self.artmoney_bounds()["lr_scale"])
            rc["entropy_weight"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("entropy_weight", self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))) * 0.98, *self.artmoney_bounds()["entropy_weight"])
            rc["dropout"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("dropout", self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))) + 0.02, *self.artmoney_bounds()["dropout"])
            rc["teacher_forcing"] = clip_float(float(rc.get("teacher_forcing", self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]))) + 0.02, *self.artmoney_bounds()["teacher_forcing"])
        return rc

    def _run_swarm_personality(self, cycle: int, batch_size: int, personality: str, num: int, steps: int, plateau: bool, safe_hyperparam_only: bool, regression_mode: bool) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        geometry_slots = 0 if safe_hyperparam_only else int(round(num * self._swarm_geometry_ratio()))
        if personality == "red" and not safe_hyperparam_only:
            geometry_slots = max(geometry_slots, int(round(num * 0.35)))
        if personality == "blue":
            geometry_slots = 0 if regression_mode else min(geometry_slots, 1)
        bad_run_streak = 0
        for i in range(num):
            branch = "geometry_explorer" if i < geometry_slots else "hyperparam_only"
            proposed, mutated, quantum = self.evomem.propose(branch=branch)
            if proposed is None:
                continue
            runtime_candidate = self._sample_artmoney_runtime(branch)
            runtime_candidate = self._personality_runtime_adjust(runtime_candidate, personality, plateau, self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND")
            lr_lo, lr_hi = self._get_artmoney_range("lr_scale")
            proposed["lr"] = clip_float(float(proposed["lr"]), lr_lo * self.cfg["lr"], lr_hi * self.cfg["lr"])
            local_cfg = dict(self.cfg)
            local_cfg.update({
                "lr": float(proposed["lr"]),
                "n_embd": int(proposed["n_embd"]),
                "n_head": int(proposed["n_head"]),
                "n_layer": int(proposed["n_layer"]),
                "dropout": float(runtime_candidate["dropout"]),
            })
            res = self._train_worker_seed(
                seed=cycle * 1000 + (0 if personality == "red" else 500) + i,
                config=local_cfg,
                steps=max(6, int(steps * runtime_candidate.get("langer_scale", 1.0))),
                batch_size=batch_size,
                runtime_candidate=runtime_candidate,
                branch=branch,
            )
            res["mutated"] = mutated
            res["quantum_tunnel"] = quantum
            res["personality"] = personality
            results.append(res)
            if not res["success"]:
                self.evomem.register_lethal(proposed)
                bad_run_streak += 1
                if bad_run_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("swarm_bad_candidate_kill_switch", 3)):
                    self.logger.warning("SWARM[%s] | kill-switch engaged after %s bad candidates", personality.upper(), bad_run_streak)
                    break
            else:
                bad_run_streak = 0
        return results

    def _janus_sovereign_score(self, cand: Dict[str, Any], plateau: bool, recovery_mode: bool) -> float:
        score = float(cand.get("score", -1e9))
        score += 0.10 * float(cand.get("stability", 0.0))
        score += 0.04 * float(cand.get("mi", 0.0))
        score += 0.03 * float(cand.get("div", 0.0))
        score -= 0.12 * max(0.0, float(cand.get("gap", 0.0)))
        score -= 0.03 * max(0.0, float(cand.get("val_loss", 1e9)) - float(self.best_val if math.isfinite(self.best_val) else cand.get("val_loss", 0.0)))
        personality = cand.get("personality", "gold")
        bias = float(self.cfg.get("janus_selector_personality_bias", 0.04))
        if personality == "red" and plateau:
            score += float(self.cfg.get("janus_selector_plateau_red_bonus", 0.08))
        if personality == "blue" and recovery_mode:
            score += float(self.cfg.get("janus_selector_recovery_blue_bonus", 0.08))
        total_wins = max(1.0, float(self.triumvirate_state.get("red_wins", 0) + self.triumvirate_state.get("blue_wins", 0) + 1))
        if personality == "red":
            score += bias * (float(self.triumvirate_state.get("red_wins", 0)) - float(self.triumvirate_state.get("blue_wins", 0))) / total_wins
        elif personality == "blue":
            score += bias * (float(self.triumvirate_state.get("blue_wins", 0)) - float(self.triumvirate_state.get("red_wins", 0))) / total_wins
        return float(score)

    def _janus_select_swarm_candidate(self, cycle: int, candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]], plateau: bool, recovery_mode: bool) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not candidates:
            return []
        scored = []
        for cand in candidates:
            cand = dict(cand)
            cand["janus_score"] = self._janus_sovereign_score(cand, plateau=plateau, recovery_mode=recovery_mode)
            scored.append(cand)
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x["janus_score"], reverse=True)
        margin = float(self.cfg.get("janus_selector_margin", 0.010))
        if len(scored) >= 2 and scored[0]["janus_score"] - scored[1]["janus_score"] < margin:
            self.triumvirate_state["gold_holds"] = int(self.triumvirate_state.get("gold_holds", 0)) + 1
            self.triumvirate_state["last_personality"] = "gold"
            self.logger.info("JANUS SOVEREIGN | cycle=%s | tie_margin=%.4f | gold holds", cycle, scored[0]["janus_score"] - scored[1]["janus_score"])
            return scored[:2]
        winner = scored[0].get("personality", "gold")
        if winner == "red":
            self.triumvirate_state["red_wins"] = int(self.triumvirate_state.get("red_wins", 0)) + 1
        elif winner == "blue":
            self.triumvirate_state["blue_wins"] = int(self.triumvirate_state.get("blue_wins", 0)) + 1
        else:
            self.triumvirate_state["gold_holds"] = int(self.triumvirate_state.get("gold_holds", 0)) + 1
        self.triumvirate_state["last_personality"] = winner
        self.logger.info("JANUS SOVEREIGN | cycle=%s | winner=%s | janus_score=%.4f | alt=%s",
                         cycle, winner, scored[0]["janus_score"], scored[1].get("personality") if len(scored) > 1 else "none")
        return scored if bool(self.cfg.get("janus_selector_alt_retry", True)) else scored[:1]


    def run_swarm_search(self, cycle: int, batch_size: int) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not self.cfg.get("swarm_enabled", True):
            return None
        if self.cfg.get("swarm_trigger_on_stall_only", True) and self.no_improve_streak < 2:
            return None
        if cycle % self.cfg["swarm_every"] != 0:
            return None

        num = max(3, int(self.cfg["swarm_num_seeds"]))
        steps = max(8, int(self.cfg["swarm_steps"]))
        plateau = self._is_plateau()
        safe_hyperparam_only = int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until)
        if plateau and self.no_improve_streak >= 4:
            num = max(num, int(round(num * float(self.cfg.get("plateau_mutation_boost", 1.50)))))
            steps = max(steps, int(round(steps * 1.20)))
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = clip_float(float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * 1.05, 0.35, 1.8)
            self.runtime_state["entropy_weight"] = clip_float(float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])) * 1.03, self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"], self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"])
            self.runtime_state["dropout"] = clip_float(float(self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"])) - 0.02, self.cfg["dropout_min"], self.cfg["dropout_max"])
            self.logger.warning("PLATEAU BREAKER | cycle=%s | num=%s | steps=%s | no_improve=%s", cycle, num, steps, self.no_improve_streak)

        regression_mode = (
            self._main_model_regressed()
            or self.no_improve_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_force_restore_no_improve", 10))
            or self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
        )
        if regression_mode and not self._ensure_best_runtime_anchor(f"swarm_cycle_{cycle}"):
            self.logger.error("SWARM SKIPPED | cycle=%s | reason=missing_best_runtime_anchor_in_regression", cycle)
            self._enter_safe_recovery(cycle, "missing_best_runtime_anchor")
            return None
        if regression_mode and isinstance(self.best_state_dict, dict) and self._restore_best_runtime():
            self.logger.warning("CHAMPION RESTORE BEFORE SWARM | cycle=%s | reason=regression_mode", cycle)

        if plateau and safe_hyperparam_only and (int(self.safe_hyperparam_only_until) - int(self.current_cycle) <= 1):
            safe_hyperparam_only = False
            self.logger.warning("PLATEAU LIFT | cycle=%s | allowing limited geometry explorer", cycle)

        if safe_hyperparam_only:
            self.logger.warning("GEOMETRY EXPLORER TEMPORARILY DISABLED | cycle=%s | safe_hyperparam_only_until=%s", cycle, self.safe_hyperparam_only_until)
        elif int(self.current_cycle) <= int(self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until):
            self.logger.warning("GEOMETRY EXPLORER TEMPORARILY DISABLED | cycle=%s | geometry_disabled_until=%s", cycle, self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until)

        personalities = ["gold"]
        if bool(self.cfg.get("dual_swarm_enabled", True)):
            personalities = ["red", "blue"]

        all_results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for personality in personalities:
            if personality == "red":
                p_num = max(int(self.cfg.get("dual_swarm_min_candidates_each", 2)), int(round(num * float(self.cfg.get("dual_swarm_red_ratio", 0.50)))))
                p_steps = max(8, int(round(steps * 0.90)))
            elif personality == "blue":
                p_num = max(int(self.cfg.get("dual_swarm_min_candidates_each", 2)), int(round(num * float(self.cfg.get("dual_swarm_blue_ratio", 0.50)))))
                p_steps = max(8, int(round(steps * 1.10)))
            else:
                p_num = num
                p_steps = steps
            all_results.extend(self._run_swarm_personality(cycle, batch_size, personality, p_num, p_steps, plateau, safe_hyperparam_only, regression_mode))

        valid = [r for r in all_results if r.get("success")]
        self.artmoney_state["swarm_successes"] = int(self.artmoney_state.get("swarm_successes", 0)) + len(valid)
        self.artmoney_state["swarm_failures"] = int(self.artmoney_state.get("swarm_failures", 0)) + max(0, len(all_results) - len(valid))
        if not valid:
