            self.logger.warning("SWARM | no surviving workers")
            self._transition_search_mode(improved=False, lethal_collision=True, swarm_success_rate=0.0)
            self._refresh_artmoney_ranges(improved=False)
            return None

        swarm_success_rate = float(len(valid) / max(1, len(all_results)))
        by_personality = {}
        for r in valid:
            by_personality.setdefault(r.get("personality", "gold"), []).append(r)
        for k,v in by_personality.items():
            bestk=max(v,key=lambda x: x["score"])
            self.logger.info("SWARM[%s] | survivors=%s | best_score=%.4f | val=%.4f | mi=%.4f | div=%.4f | seed=%s | branch=%s | load=%s",
                             k.upper(), len(v), bestk["score"], bestk["val_loss"], bestk["mi"], bestk["div"], bestk["seed"], bestk.get("branch"), bestk.get("load_policy"))

        selected_order = self._janus_select_swarm_candidate(cycle, [max(v, key=lambda x: x["score"]) for v in by_personality.values()], plateau=plateau, recovery_mode=(self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND"))

        accepted_best = None
        is_record = False
        for cand in selected_order:
            accepted = self._quarantine_swarm_candidate(cycle, cand)
            extra = {
                "val_loss": cand["val_loss"],
                "diversity": cand["div"],
                "mutual_info_unbiased": cand["mi"],
                "gap": cand["gap"],
                "stability": cand["stability"],
                "grad_norm_mean": cand["grad_norm_mean"],
                "step_time_ms": cand["step_time_ms"],
                "swarm": True,
                "personality": cand.get("personality", "gold"),
                "janus_score": cand.get("janus_score", cand["score"]),
            }
            if accepted:
                accepted_best = cand
                is_record = self.evomem.commit(cand["config"], cand["score"], extra)
                if is_record:
                    self.logger.info("SWARM | candidate confirmed as new evolutionary record")
                break

        self._transition_search_mode(
            improved=bool(is_record and accepted_best is not None),
            anomaly_success=bool(accepted_best is not None and accepted_best["val_loss"] < self.best_val),
            lethal_collision=accepted_best is None,
            swarm_success_rate=swarm_success_rate,
        )
        self._refresh_artmoney_ranges(improved=bool(is_record and accepted_best is not None))
        self.evomem.switch_mode()
        return accepted_best


    # ---------- watchdog / evolution ----------

    def _transition_search_mode(
        self,
        improved: bool = False,
        anomaly_success: bool = False,
        lethal_collision: bool = False,
        export_http_ok: Optional[bool] = None,
        export_smb_ok: Optional[bool] = None,
        swarm_success_rate: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> None:
        state = self.artmoney_state
        if improved:
            state["improve_streak"] = int(state.get("improve_streak", 0)) + 1
            state["degrade_streak"] = 0
        else:
            state["degrade_streak"] = int(state.get("degrade_streak", 0)) + 1
            state["improve_streak"] = max(0, int(state.get("improve_streak", 0)) - 1)

        if anomaly_success:
            state["anomaly_success_streak"] = int(state.get("anomaly_success_streak", 0)) + 1
        else:
            state["anomaly_success_streak"] = max(0, int(state.get("anomaly_success_streak", 0)) - 1)

        if export_http_ok is True:
            state["export_http_fail_streak"] = 0
        elif export_http_ok is False:
            state["export_http_fail_streak"] = int(state.get("export_http_fail_streak", 0)) + 1

        if export_smb_ok is True:
            state["export_smb_fail_streak"] = 0
        elif export_smb_ok is False:
            state["export_smb_fail_streak"] = int(state.get("export_smb_fail_streak", 0)) + 1

        mode = state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN")
        if lethal_collision or state["degrade_streak"] >= int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_degrade_for_recovery", 3)):
            mode = "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
        elif (swarm_success_rate is not None and swarm_success_rate < 0.25 and (state.get("swarm_failures", 0) + state.get("swarm_successes", 0)) >= 4):
            mode = "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
        elif state["improve_streak"] >= int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_improve_for_lock", 4)) and self.no_improve_streak <= 1:
            mode = "LOCKED_HUNT"
        elif (
            state["improve_streak"] >= int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_improve_for_narrow", 2))
            or state["anomaly_success_streak"] >= int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_anomaly_for_narrow", 1))
        ):
            mode = "NARROWING"
        else:
            mode = "WIDE_SCAN"

        if state.get("search_mode") != mode:
            state["search_mode"] = mode
            state["last_transition"] = utc_now_z()
            self.logger.info("ARTMONEY MODE -> %s", mode)

    def _refresh_artmoney_ranges(self, improved: bool) -> None:
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        pool = self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", [])
        if not pool:
            return

        winners = pool[: max(1, min(int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_top_winners", 4)), len(pool)))]
        mode = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN")
        mix = 0.65 if improved else float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_range_mix", 0.45))

        for key, (base_lo, base_hi) in self.artmoney_bounds().items():
            vals = [float(w.get(key)) for w in winners if isinstance(w.get(key), (int, float))]
            if not vals:
                continue

            cur_lo, cur_hi = self._get_artmoney_range(key)
            slot = self.artmoney_state["ranges"].setdefault(key, {})
            center = float(np.mean(vals))
            spread = max(float(np.std(vals)) * 2.0, (max(vals) - min(vals)) * 0.65)
            base_span = base_hi - base_lo
            current_span = max(1e-9, cur_hi - cur_lo)

            if mode == "WIDE_SCAN":
                target_span = max(base_span * 0.55, spread * 1.35)
            elif mode == "NARROWING":
                target_span = max(base_span * float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_span_floor", 0.08)), spread * float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_shrink", 0.72)))
            elif mode == "LOCKED_HUNT":
                target_span = max(base_span * float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_locked_floor", 0.03)), spread * float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_shrink_locked", 0.48)))
                slot["locked_center"] = float(center)
            else:
                locked = float(slot.get("locked_center", center))
                center = 0.65 * locked + 0.35 * center
                target_span = min(base_span * 0.60, max(current_span * float(self.cfg.get("artmoney_recovery_expand", 1.18)), spread * 1.25))

            target_lo = clip_float(center - target_span * 0.5, base_lo, base_hi)
            target_hi = clip_float(center + target_span * 0.5, target_lo, base_hi)
            new_lo = (1.0 - mix) * cur_lo + mix * target_lo
            new_hi = (1.0 - mix) * cur_hi + mix * target_hi
            self._set_artmoney_range(key, new_lo, new_hi, center=center)

    def artmoney_register(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], improved: bool):
        if not self.cfg.get("artmoney_enabled", True):
            return
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        anomaly_success = bool(
            improved and (
                diagnostics.get("anomaly_mi", {}).get("is_anomaly")
                or (diagnostics.get("anomaly_val", {}).get("is_anomaly") and diagnostics.get("val_raw", float("inf")) <= self.best_val)
            )
        )
        rank_score = float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)) + 0.12 * float(diagnostics.get("stability", 0.0)) - 0.18 * max(0.0, float(diagnostics.get("gap", 0.0)))
        candidate = {
            "cycle": int(cycle),
            "score": rank_score,
            "raw_score": float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)),
            "val_raw": float(diagnostics.get("val_raw", 0.0)),
            "mi": float(diagnostics.get("mi", 0.0)),
            "div": float(diagnostics.get("div", 0.0)),
            "gap": float(diagnostics.get("gap", 0.0)),
            "stability": float(diagnostics.get("stability", 0.0)),
            "lr_scale": float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)),
            "teacher_forcing": float(self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"])),
            "dropout": float(self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg.get("dropout", 0.10))),
            "langer_scale": float(self.runtime_state.get("langer_scale", 1.0)),
            "reflex_gain": float(self.runtime_state.get("reflex_gain", self.cfg.get("reflex_gain", 0.22))),
            "tachyon_gain": float(self.runtime_state.get("tachyon_gain", 1.0)),
            "entropy_weight": float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])),
            "anomaly_success": bool(anomaly_success),
        }
        signature = "|".join([
            f"lr_scale={candidate['lr_scale']:.4f}",
            f"teacher_forcing={candidate['teacher_forcing']:.4f}",
            f"dropout={candidate['dropout']:.4f}",
            f"langer_scale={candidate['langer_scale']:.4f}",
            f"reflex_gain={candidate['reflex_gain']:.4f}",
            f"tachyon_gain={candidate['tachyon_gain']:.4f}",
            f"entropy_weight={candidate['entropy_weight']:.4f}",
        ])
        candidate["signature"] = self._config_fingerprint(candidate, candidate)
        signature = candidate["signature"]
        pool = list(self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", []))
        pool.append(candidate)
        pool = self._dedupe_runtime_records(
            pool,
            int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_keep_best", 8)),
            prefer="score",
        )
        self.artmoney_state["best_pool"] = pool
        if pool:
            self.artmoney_state["hope_runtime"] = dict(pool[0])
            if improved:
                self.artmoney_state["protective_runtime"] = dict(pool[0])
        cm = list(self.artmoney_state.get("candidate_memory", []))
        cm.append(candidate)
        self.artmoney_state["candidate_memory"] = self._dedupe_runtime_records(
            cm,
            int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_candidate_memory_keep", 24)),
            prefer="score",
        )
        self.artmoney_state["directional_bias"] = self._build_runtime_directional_bias()
        if not improved:
            self._append_failed_signature(signature)

        self._transition_search_mode(improved=improved, anomaly_success=anomaly_success, lethal_collision=False)
        self._refresh_artmoney_ranges(improved=improved)

    def artmoney_apply_bias(self):
        if not self.cfg.get("artmoney_enabled", True):
            return
        self._normalize_artmoney_state()
        mode = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN")
        winners = self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", [])[: int(self.cfg.get("artmoney_top_winners", 4))]
        protective_runtime = self.artmoney_state.get("protective_runtime", {})

        for key in self.artmoney_bounds():
            lo, hi = self._get_artmoney_range(key)
            current = float(self.runtime_state.get(key, (lo + hi) * 0.5))
            center = self._blend_with_physarum_hint(key, current, winners)
            if isinstance(protective_runtime, dict) and isinstance(protective_runtime.get(key), (int, float)):
                center = 0.78 * center + 0.22 * float(protective_runtime.get(key))
            span = hi - lo
            if mode == "WIDE_SCAN":
                target = 0.70 * current + 0.30 * random.uniform(lo, hi)
            elif mode == "NARROWING":
                target = 0.72 * current + 0.28 * (center + random.uniform(-0.08 * span, 0.08 * span))
            elif mode == "LOCKED_HUNT":
                target = 0.55 * current + 0.45 * (center + random.uniform(-0.04 * span, 0.04 * span))
            else:
                locked = float(self.artmoney_state["ranges"].get(key, {}).get("locked_center", center))
                anchor = 0.65 * locked + 0.35 * center
                target = 0.60 * current + 0.40 * (anchor + random.uniform(-0.12 * span, 0.12 * span))
            target = self._apply_runtime_directional_bias(key, target, lo, hi)
            self.runtime_state[key] = clip_float(target, lo, hi)

        self.runtime_state["dropout"] = self._apply_runtime_dropout(self.model, self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))

    def artmoney_note_export(self, cycle: int, http_ok: Optional[bool], smb_ok: bool) -> None:
        self._transition_search_mode(
            improved=False,
            anomaly_success=False,
            lethal_collision=False,
            export_http_ok=http_ok,
            export_smb_ok=smb_ok,
            swarm_success_rate=self.evomem.swarm_success_rate(),
        )
        if smb_ok or http_ok:
            good_state = {
                "cycle": int(cycle),
                "http_ok": http_ok,
                "smb_ok": bool(smb_ok),
                "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
            }
            self.artmoney_state["last_good_export"] = dict(good_state)
            self.last_export_state["last_good_export"] = dict(good_state)

    def entropy_reversal(self):
        self.runtime_state["entropy_weight"] = min(self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"], self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0) + 0.10)
        self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.45, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.94)

    def blackhole_escape(self, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], train_loss: float, val_smooth: float):
        self.logger.warning("BLACKHOLE ESCAPE | cycle=%s | diversity=%.6f", cycle, diagnostics.get("div", 0.0))
        self.register_failure("blackhole_escape", cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)
        self.entropy_reversal()
        self.inject_noise(self.cfg["mutation_noise_small"])
        self.procedural_mutation(seed=cycle + self.restart_count + 17, strength=0.05)
        self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_http_backoff_cycles"]))

    def self_heal(self, reason: str, cycle: int, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], train_loss: float, val_smooth: float):
        self.restart_count += 1
        self.last_restart_reason = reason
        self.last_self_heal_cycle = int(cycle)
        self.register_failure(reason, cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)
        self.logger.warning("SELF-HEAL | cycle=%s | reason=%s | restart_count=%s", cycle, reason, self.restart_count)

        restored = False
        if self.cfg.get("self_heal_restore_first", True):
            restored = self._restore_best_runtime()
            if restored:
                self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.45, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.82)
                self.logger.warning("ROLLBACK TO BEST_RUNTIME | cycle=%s | reason=self_heal:%s", cycle, reason)

        if not (restored and self.cfg.get("self_heal_skip_mutation_if_best_runtime", True)):
            stage = self.restart_count % 4
            if stage == 1:
                self.inject_noise(self.cfg["mutation_noise_small"])
            elif stage == 2:
                self.inject_noise(self.cfg["mutation_noise_medium"])
                self.procedural_mutation(seed=cycle + 101, strength=0.06)
            elif stage == 3:
                self.partial_reset(self.cfg["partial_reset_fraction"])
                self.procedural_mutation(seed=cycle + 202, strength=0.08)
            else:
                self.hard_reset(self.cfg["hard_reset_fraction"])
                self.procedural_mutation(seed=cycle + 303, strength=0.12)

            if not restored:
                restored = self._restore_best_runtime()
                if restored:
                    self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.45, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * 0.82)
                    self.logger.warning("ROLLBACK TO BEST_RUNTIME | cycle=%s | reason=self_heal:%s", cycle, reason)

        self.entropy_reversal()
        self.orbit_streak = 0
        self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_http_backoff_cycles"]))

        sleep_s = max(0, int(self.cfg.get("self_watchdog_sleep_on_fault", 4)))
        if sleep_s > 0:
            time.sleep(sleep_s)

    def watchdog_check(self, cycle: int, train_loss: float, val_smooth: float, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any], rollback_done: bool, export_http_ok):
        if val_smooth <= self.best_val + 1e-9:
            self.no_improve_streak = 0
        else:
            self.no_improve_streak += 1

        # v14.6: memory says recovery trend is okay -> soften panic
        if diagnostics.get("memory_says_ok", False):
            if self.no_improve_streak > 0:
                self.no_improve_streak = max(0, int(self.no_improve_streak) - 1)
            if self.mid_interrupt_hits > 0:
                self.mid_interrupt_hits = max(0, int(self.mid_interrupt_hits) - 1)

        # v14.6: thermal cold memory can provide a context-aware revert before blunt best-runtime restore
        thermal_match = self.thermal_cold_memory.find_best_match(diagnostics, float(val_smooth))
        if thermal_match is not None and not diagnostics.get("memory_says_ok", False):
            try:
                self.model.load_state_dict(thermal_match["state"], strict=False)
                self.logger.warning(
                    "THERMAL COLD REVERT | cycle=%s | source_cycle=%s | val=%.4f | entropy=%.3f | div=%.3f",
                    cycle,
                    thermal_match.get("cycle"),
                    float(thermal_match.get("val", float("inf"))),
                    float(thermal_match.get("entropy", 0.0)),
                    float(thermal_match.get("div", 0.0)),
                )
                self.no_improve_streak = 0
                self.orbit_streak = 0
                diagnostics["thermal_revert"] = True
                return

            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.warning("THERMAL COLD REVERT FAILED | cycle=%s | error=%s", cycle, e)

        # v14.5: если часы тикают без торможения, слегка отпускаем негативные счётчики
        if diagnostics.get("do_clock_tick") and not diagnostics.get("clock_brake"):
            if self.no_improve_streak > 0:
                self.no_improve_streak = max(0, int(self.no_improve_streak) - 1)
            if self.orbit_streak > 0:
                self.orbit_streak = max(0, int(self.orbit_streak) - 1)

        orbit_ratio = float(diagnostics.get("orbit_ratio", 0.0))
        if orbit_ratio >= self.cfg["orbit_trigger_ratio"] or rollback_done:
            self.orbit_streak += 1
        else:
            self.orbit_streak = 0

        if float(diagnostics.get("div", 1.0)) <= self.cfg.get("blackhole_diversity_threshold", 0.08):
            self.div_collapse_streak += 1
        else:
            self.div_collapse_streak = 0

        if export_http_ok is False:
            self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_http_backoff_cycles"]))
        elif self.http_backoff_left > 0:
            self.http_backoff_left -= 1

        if not self.cfg.get("self_watchdog_enabled", True):
            return

        if (
            isinstance(self.best_state_dict, dict)
            and self.no_improve_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_force_restore_no_improve", 10))
            and val_smooth > float(self.best_val) * float(self.cfg.get("best_runtime_force_restore_stall_ratio", 1.20))
        ):
            if self._restore_best_runtime():
                self.logger.warning(
                    "WATCHDOG FORCE RESTORE BEST_RUNTIME | cycle=%s | val=%.4f | best=%.4f | no_improve=%s",
                    cycle, val_smooth, float(self.best_val), self.no_improve_streak
                )
                self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_http_backoff_cycles"]))
                self.no_improve_streak = 0
                return

        pred_err = float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0))
        alignment = float(diagnostics.get("alignment", 0.0))
        if alignment < 0.0:
            self.negative_alignment_streak += 1
        else:
            self.negative_alignment_streak = 0

        in_recovery = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND"
        if in_recovery and int(self.current_cycle) <= 15:
            self.negative_alignment_streak = 0

        if self.negative_alignment_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("negative_alignment_streak_limit", 5)):
            if self._break_loop_stall(cycle, "negative_alignment_streak"):
                self.logger.warning("NEGATIVE ALIGNMENT STREAK | cycle=%s | alignment=%.4f", cycle, alignment)
                self.negative_alignment_streak = 0
                return
        if self.div_collapse_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("blackhole_diversity_cycles", 4)):
            self.blackhole_escape(cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)
            self.div_collapse_streak = 0
        # v14.3a: не дёргаем stall loop в первые 30 циклов после autoresume/start
        cycles_since_resume = int(self.current_cycle) - int(getattr(self, "start_cycle", 0))
        if cycles_since_resume >= 30 and self.no_improve_streak >= int(self.cfg.get("stall_loop_break_cycles", 8)):
            if self._break_loop_stall(cycle, "stall_loop_break"):
                return
            if self.no_improve_streak >= int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_stall_cycles"]):
                self.self_heal("stall_cycles", cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)
        elif self.no_improve_streak >= int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_stall_cycles"]):
            self.self_heal("stall_cycles", cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)
        elif self.orbit_streak >= int(self.cfg["self_watchdog_orbit_cycles"]):
            self.self_heal("orbit_loop", cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)
        elif pred_err >= float(self.cfg["self_watchdog_pred_err_limit"]):
            self.self_heal("prediction_error_spike", cycle, diagnostics, train_loss, val_smooth)

    # ---------- evaluate / train ----------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(self, eval_batches: int) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        self.model.eval()
        vals, divs, mis = [], [], []
        for _ in range(eval_batches):
            x, y = self._get_batch("val")
            logits = self.model(x)
            vals.append(float(F.cross_entropy(logits.reshape(-1, self.cfg["vocab_size"]), y.reshape(-1)).item()))

            probs = torch.softmax(logits[:, -1, :], dim=-1)
            samples = torch.multinomial(probs, num_samples=1).squeeze(-1)
            divs.append(float(len(torch.unique(samples)) / max(1, samples.numel())))

            hist = torch.bincount(samples, minlength=self.cfg["vocab_size"]).float()
            p = hist / max(hist.sum(), 1.0)
            mis.append(float((-p[p > 0] * torch.log(p[p > 0] + 1e-12)).sum().item()))
        return float(np.mean(vals)), float(np.mean(divs)), float(np.mean(mis))

    
    def _fetch_quantum_sensor_context(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        ctx: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        for url in self.cfg.get("online_telemetry_urls", []):
            try:
                resp = requests.get(url, timeout=float(self.cfg.get("online_telemetry_timeout", 1.5)))
                if resp.status_code != 200:
                    continue
                payload = resp.json()
                stack = [payload]
                while stack:
                    obj = stack.pop()
                    if isinstance(obj, dict):
                        for k, v in obj.items():
                            if isinstance(v, (dict, list)):
                                stack.append(v)
                            elif isinstance(v, (int, float, str, bool)):
                                ctx.setdefault(str(k), v)
                    elif isinstance(obj, list):
                        stack.extend(obj)
                if ctx:
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue
        return ctx

    def _refresh_quantum_export_context(self, diagnostics: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        self.quantum_sensor_context = self._fetch_quantum_sensor_context()
        self.last_quantum_trace_summary = self.exporter.set_runtime_context(
            self.model,
            self.slime.trace,
            sensor_context=self.quantum_sensor_context,
            diagnostics=diagnostics or {},
        )
        return dict(self.last_quantum_trace_summary)

    def train_cycle(self, cycle: int):
        metrics = self.monitor.snapshot()
        quantum_trace_summary = self._refresh_quantum_export_context()

        # === JITTER CLOCK (физический предсказатель) ===
        jitter = float(metrics.get("timing_jitter", 0.001) or 0.001)
        self.clock_phase += jitter * self.clock_gain

        do_clock_tick = False
        if self.clock_phase >= self.clock_threshold:
            self.clock_phase = 0.0
            do_clock_tick = True
            self.last_clock_tick_cycle = int(cycle)

        self.mid_rewind_count_cycle = 0

        batch_size, pause = self.symbiosis.adapt(
            metrics.get("gpu_util", 0.0),
            metrics.get("cpu_util", 0.0),
            metrics.get("gpu_temp", 0.0),
            metrics.get("igpu_load", 0.0),
            metrics.get("cache_ratio", 1.0),
        )

        stress = max(
            metrics.get("gpu_util", 0.0) / 100.0,
            metrics.get("cpu_util", 0.0) / 100.0,
            max(metrics.get("gpu_temp", 0.0) - 50.0, 0.0) / 40.0,
            metrics.get("igpu_load", 0.0) / 100.0,
            max(metrics.get("cache_ratio", 1.0) - 1.0, 0.0),
        )
        stress = min(1.0, stress)

        langer_scale = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("langer_scale", self.cfg["langer_scale"]), self.cfg["langer_scale_min"], self.cfg["langer_scale_max"])
        teacher_forcing = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]), self.cfg["teacher_forcing_min"], self.cfg["teacher_forcing_max"])
        reflex_gain = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("reflex_gain", self.cfg["reflex_gain"]), self.cfg["reflex_gain_min"], self.cfg["reflex_gain_max"])
        tachyon_gain = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("tachyon_gain", self.cfg["tachyon_gain"]), self.cfg["tachyon_gain_min"], self.cfg["tachyon_gain_max"])
        entropy_weight = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]), self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"], self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"])
        self.runtime_state["dropout"] = self._apply_runtime_dropout(self.model, self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"]))

        steps = max(40, int(self.cfg["train_steps_per_cycle"] * (1.0 - 0.45 * stress) * langer_scale))
        eval_batches = max(6, int(self.cfg["eval_batches"] * (1.0 - 0.20 * stress)))

        purity = metrics.get("purity_score", 1.0)
        memory_summary = self.metric_memory.summary()
        reward_bias = self.cfg["world_memory_mix_ratio"] * memory_summary["avg_reward"]
        current_lr = self.cfg["lr"] * self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * (1.0 + self.cfg["purity_reward_scale"] * (purity / 100.0))
        current_lr = max(self.cfg["lr_min"], min(self.cfg["lr_max"], current_lr))

        for pg in self.optimizer.param_groups:
            pg["lr"] = current_lr
            pg["weight_decay"] = max(self.cfg["weight_decay_min"], min(self.cfg["weight_decay_max"], self.cfg["weight_decay"]))

        self.model.train()
        losses = []
        grad_norms = []
        entropy_vals = []
        last_diag = {}
        mid_interrupt = False
        mid_rewind_count = 0
        rewind_anchor = self._capture_cycle_anchor(self.model, self.optimizer)

        for step_idx in range(steps):
            batch = self._get_world_batch(batch_size) if random.random() < self.cfg["world_memory_mix_ratio"] else None
            x, y = batch if batch is not None else self._get_batch("train", batch_size=batch_size)
            x = self._apply_teacher_forcing_inputs(x, teacher_forcing)
            logits = self.model(x)
            loss, entropy = self._entropy_regularized_loss(logits, y, entropy_weight)
            self.optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss.backward()
            diag = self.guard.inspect(self.model)
            diag = self.guard.apply(self.model, self.runtime_state, diag)
            diag.update(self.slime.update_trace(self.model))
            quantum_trace_summary = self._refresh_quantum_export_context(diag)
            diag["quantum_trace_mean"] = float(quantum_trace_summary.get("trace_mean", 0.0))
            diag["quantum_sensor_factor"] = float(quantum_trace_summary.get("sensor_factor", 1.0))
            grad_norms.append(diag["grad_norm"])
            clip_value = max(self.cfg["grad_clip_min"], min(self.cfg["grad_clip_max"], self.cfg["grad_clip"] * self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)))
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.model.parameters(), clip_value)
            self.optimizer.step()
            losses.append(float(loss.item()))
            entropy_vals.append(entropy)
            self.training_loss_ema = float(loss.item()) if self.training_loss_ema is None else 0.92 * self.training_loss_ema + 0.08 * float(loss.item())
            last_diag = diag
            if self._should_interrupt_training_midway(float(step_idx + 1) / max(1, steps), float(loss.item()), losses, diag, -float(loss.item())):
                if (
                    mid_rewind_count < int(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_rewind_max", 1))
                    and self._apply_mid_interrupt_rewind(self.model, self.optimizer, self.runtime_state, rewind_anchor, scope="main")
                ):
                    mid_rewind_count += 1
                    self.mid_rewind_count_cycle += 1
                    trim = min(len(losses), int(self.cfg.get("mid_interrupt_rewind_trim", 6)))
                    if trim > 0:
                        del losses[-trim:]
                        del grad_norms[-min(len(grad_norms), trim):]
                        del entropy_vals[-min(len(entropy_vals), trim):]
                    teacher_forcing = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]), self.cfg["teacher_forcing_min"], self.cfg["teacher_forcing_max"])
                    entropy_weight = clip_float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]), self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"], self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"])
                    current_lr = self.cfg["lr"] * self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * (1.0 + self.cfg["purity_reward_scale"] * (purity / 100.0))
                    current_lr = max(self.cfg["lr_min"], min(self.cfg["lr_max"], current_lr))
                    for pg in self.optimizer.param_groups:
                        pg["lr"] = current_lr
                    continue
                mid_interrupt = True
                break

        train_loss = float(np.mean(losses))
        val_raw, div, mi = self.evaluate(eval_batches)
        val_smooth = self.ema.update(val_raw)
        stability = self._composite_stability(losses, grad_norms)

        gap = train_loss - val_raw
        score = self.compose_score(val_raw, gap, mi, div, stability)
        pred_score, pred_aux, velocity, acceleration = self.tachyon.step(score, mi)
        pred_score = score + (pred_score - score) * tachyon_gain
        pred_aux = mi + (pred_aux - mi) * tachyon_gain
        self.last_score, self.last_aux = score, mi
        self.last_train_interrupt = bool(mid_interrupt)

        orbit_ratio = float(sum([1 for x in list(self.metric_memory.items)[-self.cfg["orbit_window"]:] if x.get("rollback")]) / max(1, min(len(self.metric_memory.items), self.cfg["orbit_window"])))
        prediction_error = abs(float(pred_aux) - float(mi))
        anchor_for_divergence = self._effective_best_anchor()
        divergence = max(0.0, float(val_smooth) - float(anchor_for_divergence if math.isfinite(anchor_for_divergence) else val_smooth))
        coherence = self.compute_coherence(prediction_error, stability, divergence)
        alignment, orientation_bonus = self.compute_orientation_metrics(prediction_error, stability, divergence, coherence)
        overfit_penalty = max(0.0, self.cfg["overfit_train_threshold"] - float(train_loss)) * 0.35 if val_smooth > self._effective_best_anchor() else 0.0
        honest_reward = (
            0.60 * (-float(val_smooth))
            + 0.20 * float(purity) / 100.0
            - self.cfg["divergence_penalty_scale"] * divergence
            - self.cfg["false_prediction_penalty_scale"] * prediction_error
            - overfit_penalty
            + reward_bias
        ) * (0.80 + float(self.cfg.get("orientation_bonus_scale", 0.20)) * orientation_bonus)

        action = "continue"
        rollback = False
        rollback_margin = max(1.04, self.cfg["rollback_margin"] - 0.10 * (reflex_gain - self.cfg["reflex_gain"]))
        hard_rollback_margin = max(rollback_margin + 0.20, self.cfg["hard_rollback_margin"] - 0.14 * (reflex_gain - self.cfg["reflex_gain"]))
        effective_best_for_governor = self._effective_best_anchor()
        if val_smooth > effective_best_for_governor * hard_rollback_margin:
            rollback = True
            action = "freeze"
        elif val_smooth > effective_best_for_governor * rollback_margin:
            action = "overfit" if train_loss < self.cfg["overfit_train_threshold"] else "continue"
        if mid_interrupt:
            action = "mid_interrupt"

        anomaly_mi = self.anomaly_gate("mi", mi)
        anomaly_val = self.anomaly_gate("val_smooth", val_smooth)

        diagnostics = {
            "thermal_mode": "STABLE",
            "load_scale": max(0.25, 1.0 - stress),
            "purity_score": purity,
            "predicted_score": pred_score,
            "predicted_aux": pred_aux,
            "velocity": velocity,
            "acceleration": acceleration,
            "batch_size": batch_size,
            "steps": steps,
            "eval_batches": eval_batches,
            "pause_s": pause,
            "lr_effective": current_lr,
            "guard": last_diag,
            "val_raw": val_raw,
            "val_smooth": val_smooth,
            "div": div,
            "mi": mi,
            "score": score,
            "gap": gap,
            "stability": stability,
            "entropy_mean": float(np.mean(entropy_vals)) if entropy_vals else 0.0,
            "teacher_forcing": teacher_forcing,
            "dropout": self.runtime_state["dropout"],
            "langer_scale": langer_scale,
            "reflex_gain": reflex_gain,
            "tachyon_gain": tachyon_gain,
            "mid_interrupt": bool(mid_interrupt),
            "mid_rewind_count": int(mid_rewind_count),
            "rollback_margin_effective": rollback_margin,
            "hard_rollback_margin_effective": hard_rollback_margin,
            "grad_norm_mean": float(np.mean(grad_norms)) if grad_norms else 0.0,
            "memory_summary": memory_summary,
            "honest_reward": honest_reward,
            "divergence": divergence,
            "prediction_error": prediction_error,
            "effective_best_anchor": self._effective_best_anchor(),
            "adaptive_best_val": self.adaptive_best_val,
            "coherence": coherence,
            "alignment": alignment,
            "orientation_bonus": orientation_bonus,
            "overfit_penalty": overfit_penalty,
            "rollback": rollback,
            "action": action,
            "orbit_ratio": orbit_ratio,
            "anomaly_mi": anomaly_mi,
            "anomaly_val": anomaly_val,
            "occam_penalty": float(self.last_occam_penalty),
            "beta": float(self.beta),
            "xi": float(self.xi),
            "tau": float(self.tau),
            "phi_trust": float(self.phi_meta_state.get("trust", 1.0)),
            "phi_penalty": float(self.phi_meta_state.get("penalty", 1.0)),
            "phi_self_score": float(self.phi_meta_state.get("self_score", 0.0)),
            "jitter": float(jitter),
            "clock_phase": float(self.clock_phase),
            "do_clock_tick": bool(do_clock_tick),
            "val_smooth": float(val_smooth),
            "ema": float(val_smooth),
            "quantum_trace_mean": float(quantum_trace_summary.get("trace_mean", 0.0)),
            "quantum_sensor_factor": float(quantum_trace_summary.get("sensor_factor", 1.0)),
            "sensor_context": dict(self.quantum_sensor_context),
        }

        # === GEX (Gamma Exposure) — управление волатильностью ===
        if self.cfg.get("gex_enabled", True):
            grad_norm = float(last_diag.get("grad_norm", diagnostics.get("grad_norm_mean", 0.0)))
            grad_ema = float(last_diag.get("grad_norm_ema", grad_norm))
            gex = (grad_norm - grad_ema) / (grad_ema + 1e-8)
            diagnostics["gex"] = float(gex)
            if gex > float(self.cfg.get("gex_positive_threshold", 0.10)):
                self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] *= (1.0 - float(self.cfg.get("gex_lr_adjust", 0.02)))
            elif gex < float(self.cfg.get("gex_negative_threshold", -0.10)):
                self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] *= (1.0 + float(self.cfg.get("gex_lr_adjust", 0.02)))
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = clip_float(float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)), 0.4, 1.6)
        else:
            diagnostics["gex"] = 0.0

        # === TACHYON MEMORY ENGINE ===
        diagnostics["tachyon_memory_pred"] = None
        diagnostics["memory_says_ok"] = False
        future_val_mem = self.tachyon_memory.predict_outcome(diagnostics, self.runtime_state, self.cfg)
        if future_val_mem is not None:
            diagnostics["tachyon_memory_pred"] = float(future_val_mem)

        # === TACHYON CLOCK BRAKE / ACCEL ===
        projected_val = float(diagnostics.get("tachyon_projected_val", val_smooth))
        best_anchor_clock = float(self._effective_best_anchor())
        trust = max(0.2, min(1.5, jitter / 0.001))

        relax_ratio = float(self.cfg.get("tachyon_memory_relax_ratio", 1.05))
        if future_val_mem is not None and math.isfinite(best_anchor_clock):
            diagnostics["memory_says_ok"] = bool(float(future_val_mem) <= best_anchor_clock * relax_ratio)

        if do_clock_tick:
            if math.isfinite(best_anchor_clock) and projected_val > best_anchor_clock * 1.05 and not diagnostics.get("memory_says_ok", False):
                brake_factor = float(0.95 ** trust)
                self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * brake_factor
                self.active_swarm_candidate = None
                diagnostics["clock_brake"] = True
                diagnostics["clock_brake_factor"] = brake_factor
            else:
                diagnostics["clock_brake"] = False
                diagnostics["clock_brake_factor"] = 1.0

            if math.isfinite(best_anchor_clock) and projected_val < best_anchor_clock * 0.95 and float(val_smooth) < best_anchor_clock:
                accel_factor = float(1.02 ** trust)
                self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * accel_factor
                diagnostics["clock_accel"] = True
                diagnostics["clock_accel_factor"] = accel_factor
            else:
                diagnostics["clock_accel"] = False
                diagnostics["clock_accel_factor"] = 1.0
        else:
            diagnostics["clock_brake"] = False
            diagnostics["clock_brake_factor"] = 1.0
            diagnostics["clock_accel"] = False
            diagnostics["clock_accel_factor"] = 1.0

        # === ЭНЕРГЕТИЧЕСКАЯ БАТАРЕЙКА ===
        self.battery_charge *= max(0.95, 1.0 - jitter * 50.0)
        if float(diagnostics.get("honest_reward", 0.0)) > 0.0:
            self.battery_charge = min(1.5, self.battery_charge + 0.01)
        self.battery_charge = max(0.5, min(1.5, self.battery_charge))
        diagnostics["battery_charge"] = float(self.battery_charge)
        current_lr = float(current_lr) * float(self.battery_charge)
        diagnostics["lr_effective"] = float(current_lr)

        # v14.4: в recovery adaptive best и best_runtime могут подтягиваться быстрее
        if self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND":
            if math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)):
                if float(val_smooth) < float(self.adaptive_best_val) * 0.98:
                    self.adaptive_best_val = float(val_smooth)
                    self.logger.info("ADAPTIVE BEST UPDATED IN RECOVERY | new=%.4f", self.adaptive_best_val)

        if math.isfinite(float(self.best_val)):
            if float(val_raw) < float(self.best_val) * 0.98 and float(val_raw) < float(self.adaptive_best_val if math.isfinite(float(self.adaptive_best_val)) else float("inf")) * 0.98:
                self.logger.info("FORCE BEST UPDATE | raw=%.4f < best=%.4f", float(val_raw), float(self.best_val))
                self.best_val = float(val_raw)

        self.last_cycle_jitter = float(jitter)
        self._meta_learn_phi(diagnostics)

        effective_best = self._effective_best_anchor()
        improved = bool(
            math.isfinite(float(val_smooth))
            and math.isfinite(float(effective_best))
            and float(val_smooth) < float(effective_best)
        )

        self.tachyon_memory.add_sample(diagnostics, self.runtime_state, self.cfg, float(val_smooth))
        self.last_cycle_diagnostics = dict(diagnostics)
        if improved or (
            math.isfinite(float(val_smooth))
            and math.isfinite(float(effective_best))
            and float(val_smooth) <= float(effective_best) * 1.03
        ):
            self.thermal_cold_memory.add_state(cycle, diagnostics, self.runtime_state, self.model.state_dict())

        self._update_occam_beta_and_mycelium(diagnostics)
        return train_loss, val_raw, val_smooth, metrics, diagnostics

    def apply_governor(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        rollback = bool(diagnostics["rollback"])
        if rollback:
            restored = self._restore_best_runtime()
            if restored:
                self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = max(0.5, self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0) * self.cfg["rollback_escape_lr_factor"])
                self.logger.warning(
                    "ROLLBACK TO BEST_RUNTIME | reason=governor | action=%s | val=%.4f | best=%.4f",
                    diagnostics.get("action"),
                    diagnostics.get("val_smooth", float("inf")),
                    self.best_val,
                )
            return restored
        return False

    def replay_retrain(self, cycle: int):
        if cycle % self.cfg["retrain_every"] != 0:
            return
        texts = self.replay.sample(self.cfg["retrain_steps"])
        local_losses = []
        for t in texts:
            windows = self._text_to_windows(t)
            if not windows:
                continue
            w = random.choice(windows)
            x = torch.tensor([w[:-1]], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
            y = torch.tensor([w[1:]], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
            x = self._apply_teacher_forcing_inputs(x, self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"]))
            logits = self.model(x)
            loss, _entropy = self._entropy_regularized_loss(logits, y, self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"]))
            self.optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss.backward()
            clip_value = max(self.cfg["grad_clip_min"], min(self.cfg["grad_clip_max"], self.cfg["grad_clip"] * self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)))
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.model.parameters(), clip_value)
            self.optimizer.step()
            local_losses.append(float(loss.item()))
        if local_losses:
            self.logger.info("Replay retrain done | steps=%s | avg=%.4f", len(local_losses), float(np.mean(local_losses)))

    def _meta_learn_phi(self, diagnostics: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if not bool(self.cfg.get("phi_meta_enabled", True)):
            return
        meta_lr = float(self.cfg.get("phi_meta_lr", 0.06))
        trust_gain = float(self.cfg.get("phi_meta_trust_gain", 0.04))
        penalty_gain = float(self.cfg.get("phi_meta_penalty_gain", 0.08))

        coherence = float(diagnostics.get("coherence", 0.0))
        pred_err = float(diagnostics.get("prediction_error", 0.0))
        divergence = float(diagnostics.get("divergence", 0.0))
        honest = float(diagnostics.get("honest_reward", 0.0))
        mem_ok = bool(diagnostics.get("memory_says_ok", False))

        self.phi_meta_state["trust"] = clip_float(
            float(self.phi_meta_state.get("trust", 1.0)) + (trust_gain if mem_ok and coherence > 0.70 else -0.5 * trust_gain),
            0.5, 1.8
        )
        self.phi_meta_state["penalty"] = clip_float(
            float(self.phi_meta_state.get("penalty", 1.0)) + penalty_gain * (pred_err + divergence - 0.5 * max(0.0, coherence)),
            0.6, 2.2
        )
        self.phi_meta_state["self_score"] = 0.92 * float(self.phi_meta_state.get("self_score", 0.0)) + 0.08 * honest

        trust = float(self.phi_meta_state["trust"])
        penalty = float(self.phi_meta_state["penalty"])

        self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = clip_float(
            float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * (1.0 + meta_lr * (0.20 * trust - 0.28 * penalty * (pred_err + divergence))),
            0.35, 1.8
        )
        self.runtime_state["teacher_forcing"] = clip_float(
            float(self.runtime_state.get("teacher_forcing", self.cfg["teacher_forcing_base"])) + meta_lr * (0.08 * penalty - 0.05 * trust),
            self.cfg["teacher_forcing_min"], self.cfg["teacher_forcing_max"]
        )
        self.runtime_state["entropy_weight"] = clip_float(
            float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])) + meta_lr * (0.16 * penalty - 0.10 * trust),
            self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"], self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"]
        )
        self.runtime_state["dropout"] = clip_float(
            float(self.runtime_state.get("dropout", self.cfg["dropout"])) + meta_lr * (0.10 * penalty - 0.06 * trust),
            self.cfg["dropout_min"], self.cfg["dropout_max"]
        )
        self.runtime_state["reflex_gain"] = clip_float(
            float(self.runtime_state.get("reflex_gain", self.cfg["reflex_gain"])) + meta_lr * (0.18 * penalty - 0.08 * trust),
            self.cfg["reflex_gain_min"], self.cfg["reflex_gain_max"]
        )
        self.runtime_state["tachyon_gain"] = clip_float(
            float(self.runtime_state.get("tachyon_gain", self.cfg["tachyon_gain"])) + meta_lr * (0.15 * trust - 0.08 * penalty),
            self.cfg["tachyon_gain_min"], self.cfg["tachyon_gain_max"]
        )

        # v15.2: gradient-free φ update
        if bool(self.cfg.get("phi_gradient_free_enabled", True)):
            prev_self_score = float(self.phi_meta_state.get("prev_self_score", honest))
            reward_delta = float(honest - prev_self_score)
            probe_scale = float(self.cfg.get("phi_probe_scale", 0.03))
            direction = 1.0 if reward_delta >= 0.0 else -1.0
            self.phi_meta_state["probe_delta"] = float(direction * probe_scale * max(0.5, min(1.5, abs(reward_delta) + 0.5)))
            self.runtime_state["lr_scale"] = clip_float(
                float(self.runtime_state.get("lr_scale", 1.0)) * (1.0 + self.phi_meta_state["probe_delta"] * (0.25 * trust - 0.20 * penalty)),
                0.35, 1.8
            )
            self.runtime_state["entropy_weight"] = clip_float(
                float(self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"])) + self.phi_meta_state["probe_delta"] * (0.10 * penalty - 0.08 * trust),
                self.cfg["entropy_weight_base"], self.cfg["entropy_weight_max"]
            )
            self.phi_meta_state["prev_self_score"] = honest

        diagnostics["phi_trust"] = float(self.phi_meta_state.get("trust", 1.0))
        diagnostics["phi_penalty"] = float(self.phi_meta_state.get("penalty", 1.0))
        diagnostics["phi_self_score"] = float(self.phi_meta_state.get("self_score", 0.0))
        diagnostics["phi_probe_delta"] = float(self.phi_meta_state.get("probe_delta", 0.0))


    def maybe_prune(self, cycle: int):
        if cycle % self.cfg["slime_prune_every"] != 0:
            return
        if cycle - self.last_improvement_cycle <= int(self.cfg.get("slime_prune_grace_after_improve", 1)):
            return
        mode = self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN")
        if mode == "LOCKED_HUNT" or self.artmoney_state.get("improve_streak", 0) >= 2:
            threshold_scale = 0.75
            cap_scale = 0.40
        elif mode == "RECOVERY_EXPAND" or self.no_improve_streak >= 4:
            threshold_scale = 1.25
            cap_scale = 1.20
        else:
            threshold_scale = 1.0
            cap_scale = 0.80
        removed = self.slime.prune(self.model, threshold_scale=threshold_scale, cap_scale=cap_scale)
        if removed > 0:
            self.logger.info("Light prune removed approx params: %s | mode=%s", removed, mode)

    def should_export(self, cycle: int, val_smooth: float, diagnostics: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        deploy_trust = 0.0 if diagnostics is None else self._compute_deploy_trust(diagnostics, val_smooth)
        if diagnostics is not None:
            diagnostics["deploy_trust"] = float(deploy_trust)
        val_raw = float(val_smooth if diagnostics is None else diagnostics.get("val_raw", val_smooth))
        raw_good = (
            math.isfinite(val_raw)
            and math.isfinite(float(self.best_val))
            and val_raw < float(self.best_val) * 0.98
        )
        if raw_good:
            return True, "raw_loss_override"
        if cycle <= int(self.export_suppressed_until):
            if diagnostics is not None and deploy_trust >= float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_export_threshold", 0.55)) and float(val_smooth) <= float(self._effective_best_anchor()) * float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_val_ratio", 1.05)):
                return True, "deploy_trust_override"
            return False, "regression_cooldown"
        if isinstance(self.active_swarm_candidate, dict) and int(self.active_swarm_candidate.get("regression_hits", 0) or 0) > 0:
            return False, "swarm_aftershock_pending"
        if diagnostics is not None:
            if str(diagnostics.get("action")) == "mid_interrupt" and self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND":
                return False, "mid_interrupt_recovery"
            if diagnostics.get("anomaly_val", {}).get("is_anomaly") and float(val_smooth) > float(self.best_val) * 1.003:
                return False, "anomaly_recovery_collapse"
        if int(self.last_self_heal_cycle) > 0 and int(cycle) <= int(self.last_self_heal_cycle) + 1:
            return False, "post_self_heal"
        if self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode") == "RECOVERY_EXPAND" and self.no_improve_streak >= 2 and float(val_smooth) > float(self._effective_best_anchor()) * 1.002:
            return False, "recovery_expand_unconfirmed"
        if self.no_improve_streak >= self.cfg["forced_export_after_stall"]:
            return True, "forced_after_stall"
        if cycle - self.last_export_cycle < self.cfg["export_min_cycle_gap"]:
            return False, "cycle_gap"
        if val_smooth > self._effective_best_anchor() * self.cfg["export_block_ratio"]:
            if deploy_trust >= float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_export_threshold", 0.75)) and float(val_smooth) <= float(self._effective_best_anchor()) * float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_val_ratio", 1.05)):
                return True, "deploy_trust_safe"
            return False, "export_block_ratio"
        allowed = self.last_export_best == float("inf") or (self.last_export_best - val_smooth) >= self.cfg["export_min_improvement"]
        if not allowed and deploy_trust >= float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_export_threshold", 0.75)) and float(val_smooth) <= float(self._effective_best_anchor()) * float(self.cfg.get("deploy_trust_val_ratio", 1.05)):
            return True, "deploy_trust_safe"
        return bool(allowed), ("improved" if allowed else "min_improvement")

    def preflight_validate_http_payload(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]):
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            return False, "payload_not_dict"
        if not isinstance(payload.get("weights"), dict) or not payload["weights"]:
            return False, "weights_missing"
        if not payload.get("quant_mode"):
            return False, "quant_mode missing"
        if not isinstance(payload.get("model_config"), dict) or not payload["model_config"]:
            return False, "model_config missing"
        meta = payload.get("meta")
        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
            return False, "meta_missing"
        if not payload.get("transport") or not payload.get("payload_branch"):
            return False, "transport_or_branch_missing"
        return True, "ok"

    def sync_http(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        ok, reason = self.preflight_validate_http_payload(payload)
        if not ok:
            self.logger.warning("HTTP preflight rejected: %s", reason)
            self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg.get("nas_http_reject_backoff_cycles", 4)))
            return False
        try:
            response = requests.post(self.cfg["nas_http_import_url"], json=payload, timeout=self.cfg["nas_http_timeout"])
            if response.status_code == 200:
                self.logger.info("NAS HTTP sync ok")
                return True
            self.logger.warning("NAS HTTP sync rejected: status=%s body=%s", response.status_code, response.text[:200])
            self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg.get("nas_http_reject_backoff_cycles", 4)))
            return False
        except requests.Timeout as exc:
            self.http_backoff_left = min(
                int(self.cfg.get("nas_http_backoff_cap", 24)),
                max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg.get("nas_http_timeout_backoff_cycles", 10))),
            )
            self.logger.warning("NAS HTTP sync timeout (HTTP-first mode): %s", exc)
            return False
        except Exception as exc:
            self.http_backoff_left = max(self.http_backoff_left, int(self.cfg.get("nas_http_reject_backoff_cycles", 4)))
            self.logger.warning("NAS HTTP sync failed (HTTP-first mode): %s", exc)
            return False

    def sync_smb(self, payload: Dict[str, Any], cycle: int) -> bool:
        try:
            export_name = f"nas_quant_cycle_{cycle}_{self.cfg['quantize_mode']}.json"
            export_path = os.path.join(self.cfg["nas_quant_inbox"], export_name)
            os.makedirs(self.cfg["nas_quant_inbox"], exist_ok=True)
            with open(export_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(payload, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            self.logger.info("NAS package copied to inbox: %s", export_path)
            if self.cfg.get("write_import_request", True):
                req_path = os.path.join(self.cfg["nas_quant_inbox"], "import_request.json")
                payload_meta = payload.get("meta", {}) if isinstance(payload.get("meta"), dict) else {}
                atomic_json_write(req_path, {
                    "requested_file": export_name,
                    "source": self.cfg["nas_source_name"],
                    "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
                    "cycle": int(cycle),
                    "device_id": payload_meta.get("device_id", self.cfg["nas_http_device_id"]),
                    "quant_mode": payload.get("quant_mode", self.cfg["quantize_mode"]),
                    "transport": payload.get("transport", "smb_inbox"),
                    "payload_branch": payload.get("payload_branch", "smb_quantized_model"),
                    "search_mode": payload_meta.get("search_mode"),
                    "best_val": payload_meta.get("best_val"),
                    "event_id": payload_meta.get("event_id"),
                    "dedupe_key": payload_meta.get("dedupe_key"),
                    "priority": "hint_only",
                })
                self.logger.info("Import request updated: %s", req_path)
            return True
        except Exception as exc:
            self.logger.warning("NAS SMB sync failed: %s", exc)
            return False

    def _trim_jsonl_tail(self, path: str, keep_last_lines: int) -> None:
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return
        try:
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                lines = f.readlines()
            if len(lines) <= keep_last_lines:
                return
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.writelines(lines[-keep_last_lines:])
        except Exception:
            pass

    def cleanup_local_artifacts(self, cycle: int) -> None:
        if not self.cfg.get("cleanup_enabled", True):
            return
        if cycle % int(self.cfg.get("cleanup_every", 10)) != 0:
            return
        cps = []
        bad_cps = []
        for name in os.listdir(self.cfg["save_dir"]):
            m = re.match(r"checkpoint_(\d+)\.pt$", name)
            if m:
                cps.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], name)))
                continue
            m = re.match(r"checkpoint_recovery_(\d+)\.pt$", name)
            if m:
                bad_cps.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["save_dir"], name)))
        cps.sort(reverse=True)
        for _, p in cps[self.cfg["keep_last_checkpoints"]:]:
            try:
                os.remove(p)
            except Exception:
                pass
        bad_cps.sort(reverse=True)
        for _, p in bad_cps[self.cfg.get("keep_last_bad_checkpoints", 4):]:
            try:
                os.remove(p)
            except Exception:
                pass

        exports = []
        signal_exports = []
        for name in os.listdir(self.cfg["exports_dir"]):
            m = re.match(r"nas_quant_cycle_(\d+)_.*\.json$", name)
            if m:
                exports.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["exports_dir"], name)))
                continue
            m = re.match(r"janus_signal_cycle_(\d+)\.json$", name)
            if m:
                signal_exports.append((int(m.group(1)), os.path.join(self.cfg["exports_dir"], name)))
        exports.sort(reverse=True)
        signal_exports.sort(reverse=True)
        removed_exports = 0
        for _, p in exports[self.cfg.get("keep_last_local_exports", 12):]:
            try:
                os.remove(p)
                removed_exports += 1
            except Exception:
                pass
        removed_signals = 0
        for _, p in signal_exports[self.cfg.get("keep_last_local_exports", 12):]:
            try:
                os.remove(p)
                removed_signals += 1
            except Exception:
                pass

        self._trim_jsonl_tail(os.path.join(self.cfg["logs_dir"], "cycle_history.jsonl"), int(self.cfg.get("keep_last_history_lines", 5000)))
        self._trim_jsonl_tail(os.path.join(self.cfg["logs_dir"], "janus_ecosystem_signal.jsonl"), int(self.cfg.get("keep_last_history_lines", 5000)))
        self.logger.info("CLEANUP done | cycle=%s | kept_checkpoints=%s | kept_exports=%s | removed_exports=%s | removed_signals=%s",
                         cycle, self.cfg["keep_last_checkpoints"], self.cfg.get("keep_last_local_exports", 12), removed_exports, removed_signals)

    def run(self, cycles: int, autocontinue: bool = False):
        self.load_real_corpus()
        self.auto_resume()
        self.logger.info(
            "RUN START | start_cycle=%s | cycles_per_block=%s | autocontinue=%s",
            self.start_cycle,
            cycles,
            bool(autocontinue),
        )
        self._log_terminal_legend(self.start_cycle, force=True)

        while True:
            block_start = self.start_cycle
            end_cycle = self.start_cycle + cycles
            for cycle in range(self.start_cycle, end_cycle):
                self.current_cycle = int(cycle)
                self._normalize_recovery_windows(cycle)
                self._log_terminal_legend(cycle, force=False)
                if cycle == self.start_cycle or (cycle % self.cfg["dataset_refresh_every"] == 0):
                    self.refresh_world_memory(cycle)

                metrics = self.monitor.snapshot()
                batch_size, _pause = self.symbiosis.adapt(
                    metrics.get("gpu_util", 0.0),
                    metrics.get("cpu_util", 0.0),
                    metrics.get("gpu_temp", 0.0),
                    metrics.get("igpu_load", 0.0),
                    metrics.get("cache_ratio", 1.0),
                )

                swarm_best = self.run_swarm_search(cycle, batch_size)
                if swarm_best is not None and swarm_best.get("best_state") is not None:
                    swarm_signature = {
                        "n_embd": int(swarm_best["config"].get("n_embd", self.cfg["n_embd"])),
                        "n_head": int(swarm_best["config"].get("n_head", self.cfg["n_head"])),
                        "n_layer": int(swarm_best["config"].get("n_layer", self.cfg["n_layer"])),
                        "block_size": int(self.cfg["block_size"]),
                        "vocab_size": int(self.cfg["vocab_size"]),
                    }
                    load_info = self._safe_load_model_state(
                        self.model,
                        swarm_best["best_state"],
                        "SWARM->MAIN INJECT",
                        source_signature=swarm_signature,
                    )
                    if load_info["policy"] == "fresh init due to geometry mismatch":
                        self.logger.warning(
                            "SWARM main injection skipped heavy geometry mismatch | score=%.4f | branch=%s",
                            swarm_best["score"],
                            swarm_best.get("branch"),
                        )
                    else:
                        self._apply_swarm_runtime_candidate(swarm_best)
                        self._post_inject_warmup(
                            int(self.cfg.get("post_inject_warmup_steps", 20)),
                            float(self.cfg.get("post_inject_warmup_lr_scale_mul", 0.25)),
                        )
                        self._arm_swarm_post_inject_watch(cycle, swarm_best, load_info)
                        self.logger.info(
                            "SWARM injected into main model | score=%.4f | branch=%s | load=%s | accept_reason=%s",
                            swarm_best["score"],
                            swarm_best.get("branch"),
                            load_info["policy"],
                            swarm_best.get("quarantine", {}).get("reason", "holdout_ok"),
                        )

                self.artmoney_apply_bias()
                train_loss, val_raw, val_smooth, metrics, diagnostics = self.train_cycle(cycle)

                improved = False
                effective_best_before = self._effective_best_anchor()
                if val_smooth < self.best_val:
                    self.best_val = val_smooth
                    self.last_improvement_cycle = int(cycle)
                    self._capture_best_runtime()
                    improved = True
                self._update_adaptive_best_anchor(val_smooth, improved)
                if improved or (math.isfinite(val_smooth) and val_smooth <= self._effective_best_anchor() * float(self.cfg.get("champion_pool_keep_margin", 1.08))):
                    self._capture_champion_pool_entry(cycle, diagnostics, tag="cycle_snapshot")

                tachyon_restore = False
                if self._should_preemptive_tachyon_rollback(cycle, diagnostics):
                    restored = self._enter_safe_recovery(cycle, "tachyon_preemptive")
                    if restored:
                        diagnostics["rollback"] = True
                        diagnostics["forced_champion_restore"] = True
                        diagnostics["tachyon_preemptive"] = True
                        self.tachyon_rollback_hits += 1
                        tachyon_restore = True
                        self.logger.warning(
                            "TACHYON PREEMPTIVE ROLLBACK | cycle=%s | projected=%.4f | effective_best=%.4f | score=%.4f | pred=%.4f",
                            cycle,
                            float(diagnostics.get("tachyon_projected_val", float("inf"))),
                            self._effective_best_anchor(),
                            float(diagnostics.get("score", 0.0)),
                            float(diagnostics.get("predicted_score", 0.0)),
                        )

                forced_restore = False if tachyon_restore else self._maybe_force_restore_after_mid_interrupt(cycle, diagnostics, val_smooth)
                if forced_restore:
                    diagnostics["rollback"] = True
                    diagnostics["forced_champion_restore"] = True
                else:
                    diagnostics["forced_champion_restore"] = False

                self.artmoney_register(cycle, diagnostics, improved)
                rollback_done = bool(forced_restore or self.apply_governor(diagnostics))

                # anomaly logs
                if diagnostics["anomaly_mi"]["is_anomaly"]:
                    self.logger.info("ANOMALY MI | z=%.2f p=%.2e d=%.2f",
                                     diagnostics["anomaly_mi"]["z_score"],
                                     diagnostics["anomaly_mi"]["p_value"],
                                     diagnostics["anomaly_mi"]["effect_size"])
                if diagnostics["anomaly_val"]["is_anomaly"]:
                    self.logger.info("ANOMALY VAL | z=%.2f p=%.2e d=%.2f",
                                     diagnostics["anomaly_val"]["z_score"],
                                     diagnostics["anomaly_val"]["p_value"],
                                     diagnostics["anomaly_val"]["effect_size"])

                # commit into evolutionary memory
                evo_cfg = {
                    "lr": diagnostics["lr_effective"],
                    "gain": self.runtime_state.get("entropy_weight", 1.0),
                    "temperature": max(0.3, 2.0 - min(1.5, diagnostics["prediction_error"])),
                    "n_embd": self.cfg["n_embd"],
                    "n_head": self.cfg["n_head"],
                    "n_layer": self.cfg["n_layer"],
                    "id": f"main_{cycle}",
                }
                self.evomem.commit(evo_cfg, diagnostics["score"], {
                    "val_loss": diagnostics["val_raw"],
                    "diversity": diagnostics["div"],
                    "mutual_info_unbiased": diagnostics["mi"],
                    "gap": diagnostics["gap"],
                    "grad_norm_mean": diagnostics["grad_norm_mean"],
                    "step_time_ms": diagnostics["steps"],
                    "swarm": False,
                })

                memory_entry = {
                    "cycle": cycle,
                    "train_loss": train_loss,
                    "val_raw": val_raw,
                    "val_smooth": val_smooth,
                    "honest_reward": diagnostics["honest_reward"],
                    "rollback": rollback_done,
                    "mi": diagnostics["mi"],
                    "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
                }
                self.metric_memory.push(memory_entry)
                self.world_memory.add(
                    json.dumps({
                        "cycle": cycle,
                        "train_loss": train_loss,
                        "val_raw": val_raw,
                        "val_smooth": val_smooth,
                        "diagnostics": {k: diagnostics[k] for k in ["action", "predicted_score", "divergence", "prediction_error", "mi", "div"]},
                    }, ensure_ascii=False),
                    score=max(0.0, 1.0 / max(1e-6, val_smooth)),
                    source="metrics",
                )
                self.replay.add(
                    text=json.dumps(memory_entry, ensure_ascii=False),
                    priority=abs(diagnostics["prediction_error"]) + max(0.0, diagnostics["divergence"]),
                    meta={"cycle": cycle},
                )

                self.replay_retrain(cycle)
                self.maybe_prune(cycle)

                self._post_inject_regression_check(cycle, diagnostics, val_smooth, rollback_done, self_heal_triggered=False)

                export_allowed, export_reason = self.should_export(cycle, val_smooth, diagnostics)
                if export_allowed:
                    ecosystem_signal = self._build_ecosystem_signal(cycle, diagnostics, rollback_done)
                    http_attempted = bool(self.cfg["nas_http_enabled"] and self.http_backoff_left <= 0)
                    smb_attempted = bool(self.cfg["export_to_nas"]) and not bool(self.cfg.get("nas_http_push_only", True))
                    payload_diag = diagnostics | {
                        "corpus_meta": self.corpus_meta,
                        "memory_summary": self.metric_memory.summary(),
                        "world_memory_size": len(self.world_memory.records),
                        "failure_count": len(self.failures.items),
                        "restart_count": self.restart_count,
                        "mode": self.evomem.mode,
                        "mode_label": "EXPLORE" if self.evomem.mode == 0 else "EXPLOIT",
                        "search_mode": self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"),
                        "artmoney_ranges": self.artmoney_state.get("ranges", {}),
                        "best_pool_size": len(self.artmoney_state.get("best_pool", [])),
                        "candidate_memory_size": len(self.artmoney_state.get("candidate_memory", [])),
                        "failed_recent_size": len(self.artmoney_state.get("failed_recent", [])),
                        "load_policy_counts": self.evomem.load_policy_counts,
                        "last_load_policy": self.evomem.last_load_policy,
                        "swarm_success_rate": self.evomem.swarm_success_rate(),
                        "swarm_successes": self.evomem.swarm_successes,
                        "swarm_failures": self.evomem.swarm_failures,
                        "total_lethal": self.evomem.total_lethal,
                        "total_alive": self.evomem.total_alive,
                        "active_swarm_candidate": self.active_swarm_candidate,
                        "last_export_state": self.last_export_state,
                        "model_config": self.exporter.model_config(),
                        "transport_priority": ["smb_inbox_primary", "http_opportunistic"],
                        "ecosystem_context": ["HRAIN", "Titan", "janus_nas", "graph", "pc_to_nas_quant_handoff"],
                        "event_id": ecosystem_signal.get("event_id"),
                        "dedupe_key": ecosystem_signal.get("dedupe_key"),
                        "lineage": ecosystem_signal.get("lineage"),
                        "graph_hint": ecosystem_signal.get("graph_hint"),
                        "thought_hint": ecosystem_signal.get("thought_hint"),
                        "experience_hints": ecosystem_signal.get("experience_hints"),
                        "titan_hints": ecosystem_signal.get("titan_hints"),
                        "memory_contract": ecosystem_signal.get("memory_contract"),
                        "import_contract": ecosystem_signal.get("import_contract"),
                        "transfer_manifest": ecosystem_signal.get("transfer_manifest"),
                        "janus_context": ecosystem_signal,
                    }
                    self.exporter.set_runtime_context(
                        self.model,
                        self.slime.trace,
                        sensor_context=self.quantum_sensor_context,
                        diagnostics=payload_diag,
                    )
                    http_payload = self.exporter.build_http_payload(self.model.state_dict(), cycle, self.best_val, payload_diag)
                    smb_payload = self.exporter.build_smb_payload(self.model.state_dict(), cycle, self.best_val, payload_diag)

                    if http_attempted:
                        ok_http = False
                        for _http_try in range(max(1, int(self.cfg.get("nas_http_retry_count", 2)))):
                            ok_http = bool(self.sync_http(http_payload))
                            if ok_http:
                                break
                    elif self.cfg["nas_http_enabled"]:
                        self.logger.info("NAS HTTP skipped due to cooldown | backoff_left=%s", self.http_backoff_left)
                        ok_http = None
                    else:
                        ok_http = None
                    ok_smb = self.sync_smb(smb_payload, cycle) if smb_attempted else False

                    local_export = os.path.join(self.cfg["exports_dir"], f"nas_quant_cycle_{cycle}_{self.cfg['quantize_mode']}.json")
                    atomic_json_write(local_export, http_payload)
                    self.logger.info("NAS package exported locally: %s", local_export)
                    self.last_export_state = {
                        "cycle": int(cycle),
                        "http_ok": ok_http,
                        "smb_ok": bool(ok_smb),
                        "http_attempted": http_attempted,
                        "smb_attempted": smb_attempted,
                        "http_backoff_left": int(self.http_backoff_left),
                        "local_export": local_export,
                        "event_id": ecosystem_signal.get("event_id"),
                        "dedupe_key": ecosystem_signal.get("dedupe_key"),
                        "search_mode": self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"),
                        "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
                    }
                    self.artmoney_note_export(cycle, ok_http, ok_smb)
                    ecosystem_signal["delivery"] = {
                        "http_attempted": http_attempted,
                        "http_ok": ok_http,
                        "smb_attempted": smb_attempted,
                        "smb_ok": bool(ok_smb),
                        "local_export": local_export,
                        "http_backoff_left": int(self.http_backoff_left),
                    }
                    ecosystem_signal["last_export_state"] = dict(self.last_export_state)
                    self._persist_ecosystem_signal(cycle, ecosystem_signal)

                    if ok_http or ok_smb:
                        self.last_export_cycle = cycle
                        self.last_export_best = val_smooth
                        if ok_smb and self.cfg.get("delete_export_after_smb_ok", False):
                            try:
                                os.remove(local_export)
                                self.logger.info("Local export removed after SMB success: %s", local_export)
                            except Exception:
                                pass
                    else:
                        self.logger.warning("EXPORT attempt finished without remote ack | http=%s | smb=%s", ok_http, ok_smb)
                else:
                    if export_reason in {
                        "regression_cooldown",
                        "swarm_aftershock_pending",
                        "mid_interrupt_recovery",
                        "anomaly_recovery_collapse",
                        "post_self_heal",
                        "recovery_expand_unconfirmed",
                    }:
                        self.logger.warning(
                            "EXPORT SUPPRESSED DUE TO REGRESSION | cycle=%s | reason=%s | ema=%.4f | best=%.4f",
                            cycle,
                            export_reason,
                            val_smooth,
                            self.best_val,
                        )
                    else:
                        self.logger.info("EXPORT BLOCKED | reason=%s | ema=%.4f best=%.4f", export_reason, val_smooth, self.best_val)
                    ok_http = None
                    ok_smb = False

                restart_count_before_watchdog = int(self.restart_count)
                self.watchdog_check(cycle, train_loss, val_smooth, diagnostics, rollback_done, ok_http)
                self._post_inject_regression_check(
                    cycle,
                    diagnostics,
                    val_smooth,
                    rollback_done,
                    self_heal_triggered=bool(self.restart_count > restart_count_before_watchdog),
                )
                self._update_recovery_discipline(cycle, improved)
                checkpoint_stable, checkpoint_reason = self._checkpoint_state_classification(cycle, diagnostics, val_smooth, rollback_done)
                if checkpoint_stable:
                    self.last_stable_cycle = int(cycle)
                self.save_checkpoint(cycle, stable=checkpoint_stable, reason=checkpoint_reason)
                self._log_cycle_human_summary(
                    cycle=cycle,
                    block_start=block_start,
                    block_end=end_cycle - 1,
                    train_loss=train_loss,
                    val_raw=val_raw,
                    val_smooth=val_smooth,
                    metrics=metrics,
                    diagnostics=diagnostics,
                    rollback_done=rollback_done,
                    export_allowed=export_allowed,
                    export_reason=export_reason,
                    checkpoint_stable=checkpoint_stable,
                    checkpoint_reason=checkpoint_reason,
                )

                append_jsonl(os.path.join(self.cfg["logs_dir"], "cycle_history.jsonl"), {
                    "cycle": cycle,
                    "train_loss": train_loss,
                    "val_raw": val_raw,
                    "val_smooth": val_smooth,
                    "best_val": self.best_val,
                    "metrics": metrics,
                    "diagnostics": diagnostics,
                    "rollback_done": rollback_done,
                    "checkpoint_stable": checkpoint_stable,
                    "checkpoint_reason": checkpoint_reason,
                    "export_allowed": export_allowed,
                    "export_reason": export_reason,
                    "world_memory_size": len(self.world_memory.records),
                    "failure_count": len(self.failures.items),
                    "mode": self.evomem.mode,
                    "search_mode": self.artmoney_state.get("search_mode", "WIDE_SCAN"),
                    "total_lethal": self.evomem.total_lethal,
                    "total_alive": self.evomem.total_alive,
                    "swarm_success_rate": self.evomem.swarm_success_rate(),
                    "active_swarm_candidate": self.active_swarm_candidate,
                    "last_swarm_rejection": self.last_swarm_rejection,
                    "last_swarm_acceptance": self.last_swarm_acceptance,
                    "last_export_state": self.last_export_state,
                    "watchdog": {
                        "no_improve_streak": self.no_improve_streak,
                        "orbit_streak": self.orbit_streak,
                        "http_backoff_left": self.http_backoff_left,
                        "restart_count": self.restart_count,
                        "last_restart_reason": self.last_restart_reason,
                        "last_self_heal_cycle": self.last_self_heal_cycle,
                        "div_collapse_streak": self.div_collapse_streak,
                        "geometry_explorer_disabled_until": self.geometry_explorer_disabled_until,
                        "safe_hyperparam_only_until": self.safe_hyperparam_only_until,
                        "export_suppressed_until": self.export_suppressed_until,
                    },
                    "timestamp": utc_now_z(),
                })

                self.save_memories()
                self.cleanup_local_artifacts(cycle)

                pause = float(diagnostics.get("pause_s", 0.0))
                if pause > 0:
                    time.sleep(pause)

                gc.collect()
                if torch.cuda.is_available():
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

            if not autocontinue:
                self.logger.warning(
                    "RUN BLOCK FINISHED NORMALLY | last_cycle=%s | autocontinue=%s | restart_launcher_with=--autocontinue",
                    end_cycle - 1,
                    bool(autocontinue),
                )
                break

            self.start_cycle = end_cycle
            self.logger.info("Auto-continue enabled: next block starts from cycle %s", self.start_cycle)


def build_config_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    cfg = dict(CONFIG)
    if args.code_root:
        cfg["code_root"] = args.code_root
    if args.batch_size:
        cfg["batch_size"] = int(args.batch_size)
    if args.block_size:
        cfg["block_size"] = int(args.block_size)
    if args.lr:
        cfg["lr"] = float(args.lr)
    if args.n_layer:
        cfg["n_layer"] = int(args.n_layer)
    if args.n_embd:
        cfg["n_embd"] = int(args.n_embd)
    if args.n_head:
        cfg["n_head"] = int(args.n_head)
    if args.swarm_num_seeds:
        cfg["swarm_num_seeds"] = int(args.swarm_num_seeds)
    return cfg


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="JGPT v12 Swarm Demiurge Core")
    parser.add_argument("--cycles", type=int, default=100)
    parser.add_argument("--autocontinue", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--code-root", type=str, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--block-size", type=int, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--n-layer", type=int, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--n-embd", type=int, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--n-head", type=int, default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--swarm-num-seeds", type=int, default=None)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    cfg = build_config_from_args(args)
    logger = setup_logging(cfg["logs_dir"])
    trainer = EvolutionTrainer(cfg, logger)
    trainer.run(args.cycles, autocontinue=bool(args.autocontinue))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
